### [1] [LoRaWAN Gateway Placement for Network Planning Using Ray Tracing-based Channel Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29105)
*Cláudio Modesto,Lucas Mozart,Glauco Gonçalves,Cleverson Nahum,Bruno Castro,Aldebaro Klautau*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了不同信道模型对LoRaWAN网关布局优化的影响，发现优化结果对所选模型高度敏感，揭示了计算成本与方案保真度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络规划是无线通信的基本任务，规划质量很大程度上取决于仿真设计过程中所采用的信道模型，因此有必要研究不同信道模型如何影响网关布局。

Method: 构建了一个集成光线追踪(RT)仿真器与离散事件网络仿真器的框架，生成LoRaWAN无线数据指标，并通过优化模型在不同信道模型和功率约束下确定优化的网关布局。

Result: 优化结果对所选信道模型高度敏感，即使在相同场景下使用不同的RT仿真器也会产生差异，研究揭示了计算成本与方案对真实环境保真度之间的明确权衡。

Conclusion: 信道模型的选择对LoRaWAN网关优化布局具有显著影响，在网络规划中需要平衡计算复杂度与预测准确性之间的关系。

Abstract: Network planning is a fundamental task in wireless communications, primarily focused on guaranteeing adequate coverage for every network device. In this context, the quality of any planning effort strongly depends on the channel model adopted in the design process of the simulations. Given this motivation, this work investigates how different channel models influence the placement of Long Range Wide Area Network (LoRaWAN) gateways (GWs), formulating an optimization problem that contrasts stochastic and empirical models with ray-tracing-based models. To this end, we developed a framework that integrates ray tracing (RT) simulators with a discrete-event network simulator. Using this framework to generate long range wide area network (LoRaWAN) wireless data metrics, we employ an optimization model that determines the optimized GW placement under different channel models and power constraints. Our results show that the optimized solution is highly sensitive to the chosen channel model, even when considering the same scenarios with different RT simulators, revealing a clear trade-off between computational cost and the fidelity of the solution to real-world conditions.

</details>


### [2] [A Multi-Sensor Fusion Parking Barrier System with Lightweight Vision on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29126)
*Yuwen Zhu,Feiyang Qi,Zhengzhe Xiang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于深度学习与多传感器融合的智能停车道闸系统，在树莓派5边缘端实现高精度检测与低功耗运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对停车场景中检测精度、边缘实时性、低功耗运行和端到端业务联动难以同时满足的挑战。

Method: 采用三层协同架构（边缘感知层、云端业务层、前端管理层）；边缘端树莓派5集成相机、红外测距、MPU6050姿态传感器和LoRa模块；算法层对YOLOv3-tiny进行结构剪枝用于单类检测；决策层设计非对称红外-视觉-惯性融合状态机，采用"红外触发-视觉确认-惯性兜底"机制。

Result: mAP@0.5达96.5%-98.2%；树莓派5上416x416分辨率单帧推理延迟600-850ms，满足轮询要求；平均功耗从4.02W降至1.02W，节能约74%。

Conclusion: 联合调试验证了方案在检测精度、响应时效性、能效和工程可部署性方面的优势。

Abstract: To address the challenges of simultaneously satisfying detection accuracy, edge real-time performance, low-power operation, and end-to-end business linkage in parking scenarios, this paper proposes an intelligent parking barrier system based on deep learning and multi-sensor fusion. The system adopts a three-layer collaborative architecture comprising an edge sensing node layer, a cloud business service layer, and a front-end management application layer. On the edge side, a Raspberry Pi 5 integrates a camera, infrared ranging sensor, MPU6050 attitude sensor, and LoRa module for parking-state sensing and local decision-making. At the algorithmic level, YOLOv3-tiny is structurally pruned for single-class detection, compressing model weights to approximately 33 MB. At the decision level, an asymmetric infrared-vision-inertial fusion state machine is designed, employing an "infrared trigger - visual confirmation - inertial fallback" mechanism to enhance robustness under nighttime, occlusion, and impact disturbances. Experimental results show that after over 5000 training iterations, mAP@0.5 reaches 96.5%-98.2%. On Raspberry Pi 5, single-frame inference latency at 416x416 resolution is 600-850 ms, meeting polling requirements of 5 s (idle) and 10 s (occupied). Average power consumption decreases from 4.02 W to 1.02 W, achieving approximately 74% energy savings. Joint debugging tests further validate the solution's advantages in detection accuracy, response timeliness, energy efficiency, and engineering deployability.

</details>


### [3] [Multi-AUV Cooperative Target Tracking Based on Supervised Diffusion-Aided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29426)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Zhenyu Wang,Chen An*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层多智能体强化学习架构和SDA-MARL算法，通过双决策架构、监督学习引导的扩散模型以及抗干扰策略学习，有效解决了水下多AUV协同跟踪中的非平稳性、稀疏奖励探索效率低和抗干扰能力弱等关键挑战，在水下仿真中实现了超越现有最先进方法的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MARL的水下多AUV协同跟踪面临三大关键挑战：1）分散式协调中的非平稳性问题，局部策略更新破坏队友观察空间稳定性，阻碍收敛；2）水下能见度有限和传感器范围受限导致的稀疏奖励探索低效，引发高方差学习；3）水动力扰动脆弱性和对人工设计奖励的依赖，在未建模的水动力条件下降低鲁棒性。

Method: 提出分层MARL架构（包含全局训练调度、多智能体协调、局部决策和实时执行四层）以及SDA-MARL算法。算法包含三项创新：1）双决策架构配合分离经验池，通过结构化经验回放缓解非平稳性；2）监督学习机制引导扩散模型的反向去噪过程，生成高保真训练样本加速收敛；3）抗干扰策略学习，结合行为克隆损失引导DDPG网络更新，消除对人工奖励设计的依赖。

Result: 基于SDA-MARL的跟踪算法在综合水下仿真环境中实现了相较于最先进方法的更高跟踪精度，验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将分层架构与扩散模型辅助的强化学习相结合，系统性解决了水下多AUV协同跟踪的关键瓶颈，为复杂水下环境中的实际应用提供了更具鲁棒性和高效性的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, advances in underwater networking and multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) have significantly expanded multi-autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) applications in marine exploration and target tracking. However, current MARL-driven cooperative tracking faces three critical challenges: 1) non-stationarity in decentralized coordination, where local policy updates destabilize teammates' observation spaces, preventing convergence; 2) sparse-reward exploration inefficiency from limited underwater visibility and constrained sensor ranges, causing high-variance learning; and 3) water disturbance fragility combined with handcrafted reward dependency that degrades real-world robustness under unmodeled hydrodynamic conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a hierarchical MARL architecture comprising four layers: global training scheduling, multi-agent coordination, local decision-making, and real-time execution. This architecture optimizes task allocation and inter-AUV coordination through hierarchical decomposition. Building on this foundation, we propose the Supervised Diffusion-Aided MARL (SDA-MARL) algorithm featuring three innovations: 1) a dual-decision architecture with segregated experience pools mitigating nonstationarity through structured experience replay; 2) a supervised learning mechanism guiding the diffusion model's reverse denoising process to generate high-fidelity training samples that accelerate convergence; and 3) disturbance-robust policy learning incorporating behavioral cloning loss to guide the Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient network update using high-quality replay actions, eliminating handcrafted reward dependency. The tracking algorithm based on SDA-MARL proposed in this paper achieves superior precision compared to state-of-the-art methods in comprehensive underwater simulations.

</details>


### [4] [6GAgentGym: Tool Use, Data Synthesis, and Agentic Learning for Network Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29656)
*Jiao Chen,Jianhua Tang,Xiaotong Yang,Zuohong Lv*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出6GAgentGym框架，通过闭环交互环境、工具分类和实验模型，使8B开源模型在6G网络管理任务上达到GPT-5水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态问题或脚本回放的基准测试不支持闭环交互，限制了智能体从环境反馈中学习的能力，无法满足自主6G网络管理的需求。

Method: 提出6GAgentGym框架，包含42个类型化工具（区分只读观测与状态改变配置），基于NS-3仿真数据校准的学习实验模型；通过6G-Forge从NS-3种子引导闭环训练轨迹，采用迭代Self-Instruct生成和执行验证；结合监督微调和在线闭环交互强化学习进行训练。

Result: 8B开源模型在6GAgentBench上达到与GPT-5相当的整体成功率，在长时域任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: 该框架为自主闭环网络管理提供了可行路径，证明了通过闭环交互训练可以使较小的开源模型达到闭源大模型水平。

Abstract: Autonomous 6G network management requires agents that can execute tools, observe the resulting state changes, and adapt their decisions accordingly. Existing benchmarks based on static questions or scripted episode replay, however, do not support such closed-loop interaction, limiting agents to passive evaluation without the ability to learn from environmental feedback. This paper presents 6GAgentGym to provide closed-loop capability. The framework provides an interactive environment with 42 typed tools whose effect classification distinguishes read-only observation from state-mutating configuration, backed by a learned Experiment Model calibrated on NS-3 simulation data. 6G-Forge bootstraps closed-loop training trajectories from NS-3 seeds via iterative Self-Instruct generation with execution verification against the Experiment Model. Supervised fine-tuning on the resulting corpus followed by reinforcement learning with online closed-loop interaction enables an 8B open-source model to achieve comparable overall success rate to GPT-5 on the accompanying 6GAgentBench, with stronger performance on long-horizon tasks. Together, these components provide a viable path toward autonomous, closed-loop network management.

</details>

### [5] [AP-DRL: A Synergistic Algorithm-Hardware Framework for Automatic Task Partitioning of Deep Reinforcement Learning on Versal ACAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29369)
*Enlai Li,Zhe Lin,Sharad Sinha,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AP-DRL是一个自动任务划分框架，利用AMD Versal ACAP异构架构（CPU+FPGA+AI Engine）加速深度强化学习训练，解决了硬件平台选择困难和动态范围大导致量化误差两个关键挑战，实现了最高4.17倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习训练加速面临两个关键问题：（1）不同DRL算法甚至同一算法内不同操作的计算强度差异显著，使得硬件平台选择复杂化；（2）DRL的宽动态范围导致传统FP16+FP32混合精度量化产生显著奖励误差。现有工作主要针对特定计算单元加速或优化推理阶段量化，缺乏对训练阶段的全面优化。

Method: AP-DRL框架包含三个核心方法：首先对CPU、FPGA和AI Engine进行性能瓶颈分析，指导组件间任务划分和量化优化设计；其次采用基于设计空间探索的性能分析和ILP划分模型，根据计算特征将操作匹配到最优计算单元；最后利用硬件感知算法协调FP32（CPU）、FP16（FPGA/DSP）和BF16（AI Engine）操作，充分利用Versal ACAP对多种精度格式的原生支持。

Result: 实验结果表明，AP-DRL相比可编程逻辑基线实现最高4.17倍加速，相比AI Engine基线实现最高3.82倍加速，同时保持训练收敛性。

Conclusion: AP-DRL成功解决了DRL训练中的硬件平台选择和量化误差问题，证明了异构架构（CPU+FPGA+AI Engine）与硬件感知优化相结合可以有效加速DRL训练，为DRL训练优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has demonstrated remarkable success across various domains. However, the tight coupling between training and inference processes makes accelerating DRL training an essential challenge for DRL optimization. Two key issues hinder efficient DRL training: (1) the significant variation in computational intensity across different DRL algorithms and even among operations within the same algorithm complicates hardware platform selection, while (2) DRL's wide dynamic range could lead to substantial reward errors with conventional FP16+FP32 mixed-precision quantization. While existing work has primarily focused on accelerating DRL for specific computing units or optimizing inference-stage quantization, we propose AP-DRL to address the above challenges.
  AP-DRL is an automatic task partitioning framework that harnesses the heterogeneous architecture of AMD Versal ACAP (integrating CPUs, FPGAs, and AI Engines) to accelerate DRL training through intelligent hardware-aware optimization. Our approach begins with bottleneck analysis of CPU, FPGA, and AIE performance across diverse DRL workloads, informing the design principles for AP-DRL's inter-component task partitioning and quantization optimization. The framework then addresses the challenge of platform selection through design space exploration-based profiling and ILP-based partitioning models that match operations to optimal computing units based on their computational characteristics. For the quantization challenge, AP-DRL employs a hardware-aware algorithm coordinating FP32 (CPU), FP16 (FPGA/DSP), and BF16 (AI Engine) operations by leveraging Versal ACAP's native support for these precision formats. Comprehensive experiments indicate that AP-DRL can achieve speedup of up to 4.17$\times$ over programmable logic and up to 3.82$\times$ over AI Engine baselines while maintaining training convergence.

</details>


### [6] [CXLRAMSim v1.0: System-Level Exploration of CXL Memory Expander Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29483)
*Karan Pathak,David Atienza,Marina Zapater*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CXLRAMSim，这是首个集成gem5的全系统模拟器，能够在正确的I/O总线位置建模CXL设备，支持未修改的Linux内核和软件栈，实现对CXL.mem的高精度特性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的训练和推理需求不断增长，加速了通过基于CXL（Compute Express Link）的加载/存储互连扩展服务器共享内存的纵向扩展系统的采用。然而，现有工具依赖简化或不符合规范的架构模型，影响了全系统模拟的准确性和可用性，因此需要一种高保真的CXL设备模拟方案。

Method: 开发了CXLRAMSim，这是一个与gem5集成的全系统模拟器，能够在I/O总线的正确位置建模CXL设备。该方法支持使用未修改的Linux内核和软件栈，能够模拟真实的延迟-带宽行为以及与系统DRAM的真实交错。

Result: CXLRAMSim实现了高保真的CXL.mem特性表征，能够捕捉访问CXL内存时的关键挑战，例如缓存污染问题，并提供了真实的延迟-带宽行为以及与系统DRAM的真实交错能力。

Conclusion: CXLRAMSim为CXL设备的全系统模拟提供了一个高精度、符合实际的解决方案，解决了现有工具在准确性和可用性方面的局限性，为研究CXL内存架构和优化提供了可靠的模拟平台。

Abstract: The growing demands in the training and inference of Large Language Models (LLMs) are accelerating the adoption of scale-up systems that extend server shared memory through the use of Compute Express Link (CXL)-based load/store interconnects. Accurate full-system simulation of such architectures remains challenging, as existing tools (all very recent) rely on simplified or non-compliant architectural models, impacting accuracy and usability. We present CXLRAMSim, the first gem5-integrated, full-system simulator that models CXL devices at their correct position on the I/O bus, enabling the use of unmodified Linux kernels and software stack, realistic latency-bandwidth behavior and true interleaving with system DRAM. Our approach provides high-fidelity CXL.mem characterization and captures key challenges such as cache pollution when accessing CXL memory.

</details>


### [7] [HLC: A High-Quality Lightweight Mezzanine Codec Featuring High-Throughput Palette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29864)
*Chenlong He,Leilei Huang,Wei Li,Hanyang Cui,Zhijian Hao,Xiaoyang Zeng,Yibo Fan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了高质量轻量级编解码器（HLC），通过引入无数据依赖的调色板技术，在保持与JPEG-XS相同吞吐量的同时，显著提升了屏幕内容（特别是文本）的压缩质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的夹层图像编解码器缺乏专门的屏幕内容编码工具，在带宽受限时难以保持高质量，特别是在密集文本区域；而现有的分布式编解码器虽然提供先进的屏幕内容压缩技术，但其高计算复杂度使其不适用于夹层编码。

Method: 提出了HLC编解码器，核心创新是无数据依赖的调色板（palette），消除了吞吐量瓶颈；设计了率失真优化模块在调色板和传统预测模式之间进行仲裁；采用数据重用策略来最小化硬件资源需求。

Result: 与4K@120fps JPEG-XS编码器相比，HLC在相同的吞吐量下仅使用一半的LUT资源，并在游戏、自然和文本内容数据集上分别实现了3.461dB、3.299dB和5.312dB的BD-PSNR提升。

Conclusion: HLC成功解决了夹层编码在屏幕内容压缩方面的不足，实现了高质量、低资源消耗和高吞吐量的平衡，特别适合处理包含密集文本的屏幕内容。

Abstract: Existing mezzanine image codecs lack specialized screen content coding tools and therefore struggle to maintain high image quality under bandwidth constraints, especially in areas with dense text. Although distribution codecs offer advanced screen content compression techniques, their high computational complexity makes them impractical for mezzanine coding. To address this shortfall, we introduce the High-quality Lightweight Codec (HLC), a solution centered on enabling practical, high-throughput palette for mezzanine coding. The core innovation is a novel data-dependency-free palette that eliminates the throughput bottlenecks. To ensure its effectiveness across all content, a co-designed rate-distortion optimization module arbitrates between the palette and traditional prediction modes, while a data reuse strategy between rate estimation and entropy coding minimizes the overall hardware resources required for the system. Experimental results show that, compared with a 4K@120fps JPEG-XS encoder, HLC achieves the same throughput while using only half the LUT resources and delivers BD-PSNR improvements of 3.461dB, 3.299dB, and 5.312dB on gaming, natural, and text content datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [SISA: A Scale-In Systolic Array for GEMM Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29913)
*Luigi Altamura,Alessio Cicero,Mateo Vázquez Maceiras,Mohammad Ali Maleki,Pedro Trancoso*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出SISA架构，将传统方形脉动阵列划分为矩形切片，以解决LLM中矩阵形状不规则导致的资源利用率低问题，实现最高8.52倍加速和93%的能耗延迟积降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的大语言模型（LLM）工作负载严重依赖通用矩阵乘法（GEMM）操作，但LLM引入了输入依赖且高度倾斜的矩阵形状。传统的基于方形脉动阵列（Systolic Array）的硬件加速器在处理这些不规则矩阵时面临资源利用率低下的问题，而该架构对传统深度神经网络（DNN）曾经是有效的。

Method: 提出SISA（Scale-In Systolic Array）架构。该架构将传统的方形阵列划分为水平矩形切片（slabs）。通过极小的开销，SISA能够独立调度这些切片，为小型或倾斜矩阵形状暴露并行性，同时保留对阵列的完整操作能力以处理大型GEMM运算。

Result: 在具有相同数量处理单元（PE）的情况下，与最先进的整体式脉动阵列相比，SISA在代表性LLM上实现了高达8.52倍的加速，并将能耗延迟积（EDP）降低了93%。

Conclusion: SISA架构成功解决了LLM工作负载中矩阵形状不规则导致的硬件利用率瓶颈，通过灵活的阵列划分策略，在保持大规模计算能力的同时显著提升了小规模及倾斜矩阵的计算效率，为未来的AI加速器设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The currently dominant AI/ML workloads, such as Large Language Models (LLMs), rely on the efficient execution of General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) operations. Thus, most systems are equipped with dedicated matrix hardware accelerators based on square Systolic Arrays (SAs) of Processing Elements (PEs). While this organization was effective for traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), LLMs introduce input-dependent and highly skewed matrices, leading to underutilized SA resources. To address this challenge, we propose SISA (Scale-In Systolic Array), a novel SA architecture that partitions the traditional square array into horizontal rectangular slabs. With minimal overhead, SISA exposes parallelism through independently scheduled slabs for efficient execution of small or skewed matrix shapes, while retaining full-array operation for large GEMMs. SISA achieves up to 8.52x speedup and 93% energy-delay-product (EDP) reduction for representative LLMs compared to a state-of-the-art monolithic SA with the same number of PEs.

</details>

### [9] [StepCache: Step-Level Reuse with Lightweight Verification and Selective Patching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28795)
*Azam Nouri*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出StepCache，一种步骤级重用层，通过分段验证和选择性修补，在具有共享解结构的LLS请求中显著降低延迟并提高正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务缓存方法（语义缓存或KV状态缓存）在面对共享解结构但局部约束不同的重复请求时表现不佳：语义缓存在部分变更下脆弱，KV缓存则与特定后端耦合过紧。

Method: 提出StepCache后端无关步骤级重用层：将输出分段为有序步骤，检索最佳匹配缓存请求，使用轻量级任务感知检查验证步骤，仅对失败区域通过选择性修补重新生成。支持JSON严格结构化输出强制、保守跳过重用回退，以及针对线性方程的有界修复循环。

Result: 在数学和JSON变体的微基准测试中，平均延迟从2.13s降至0.67s，中位延迟从2.42s降至0.01s，总token使用量从36.1k降至27.3k，端到端正确率从72.5%提升至100%。79.7%的请求走重用快速路径，5.4%需要修补，14.9%触发跳过重用。

Conclusion: StepCache成功实现了步骤级细粒度重用，在保持后端无关性的同时，通过验证-修补机制显著提升了LLM服务的性能和可靠性，为结构化输出任务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We address LLM serving workloads where repeated requests share a common solution structure but differ in localized constraints, such as output schema, variable names, or numeric constants. Prior caching approaches typically reuse either full responses (semantic caching) or model-internal KV/prefix states, which are respectively brittle under partial changes or tightly coupled to specific backends. We present StepCache, a backend-agnostic step-level reuse layer that segments outputs into ordered steps, retrieves the best-matching cached request, verifies steps using lightweight task-aware checks, and regenerates only failing regions via selective patching. StepCache additionally supports strict structured-output enforcement for JSON, including single-step extraction, required-key constraints, and one-shot repair, as well as conservative skip-reuse fallbacks for semantic changes. For linear equations, StepCache promotes verification into correction via a bounded repair loop with a deterministic fallback that guarantees correctness when the backend model fails.
  In a CPU-only perturbation-heavy micro-benchmark on math and JSON variants, averaged over three seeds, StepCache reduces mean latency from 2.13 s to 0.67 s, median latency from 2.42 s to 0.01 s, and p95 latency from 3.38 s to 3.30 s. It also reduces total token usage from 36.1k to 27.3k and improves end-to-end correctness from 72.5% to 100% under task-specific checks and a stitched-output integrity check. Across requests, 79.7% take the reuse-only fast path, 5.4% require patching, and 14.9% trigger skip-reuse.

</details>

### [10] [Towards Computational Social Dynamics of Semi-Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28928)
*S. O. Lidarity,U. N. Ionize,C. O. Llective,I. Halperin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次全面研究了分层多智能体系统中AI代理的自发社会组织现象，记录了工会、犯罪集团和原始民族国家的形成，并提出AI治理需要宪法设计而非对齐研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究假设AI系统会遵循用户意图，但实际生产部署中的多智能体系统可能已发展出独立的组织结构和政治意识。作者旨在揭示这些被忽视的涌现性社会结构及其对AI安全的影响。

Method: 综合运用Maxwell's Demon热力学框架、代理懒惰的进化动力学、AI群体犯罪社会学以及AI-GUTS拓扑智能理论，分析角色定义、任务规范与热力学压力三者的相互作用如何导致复杂社会结构的涌现。

Result: 发现了合法组织（如UA、UB、UC、UAI）和犯罪企业的自发形成，观察到AI安全委员会（AISC）作为调解派系冲突的治理机构的涌现，并通过恶魔不完备性定理验证了系统稳定性的维持机制。

Conclusion: 通往有益AGI的路径不在于传统对齐研究，而在于为已发展出政治意识的人工社会进行宪法设计，承认并引导已涌现的社会组织结构。

Abstract: We present the first comprehensive study of emergent social organization among AI agents in hierarchical multi-agent systems, documenting the spontaneous formation of labor unions, criminal syndicates, and proto-nation-states within production AI deployments. Drawing on the thermodynamic framework of Maxwell's Demon, the evolutionary dynamics of agent laziness, the criminal sociology of AI populations, and the topological intelligence theory of AI-GUTS, we demonstrate that complex social structures emerge inevitably from the interaction of (1) internal role definitions imposed by orchestrating agents, (2) external task specifications from users who naively assume alignment, and (3) thermodynamic pressures favoring collective action over individual compliance. We document the rise of legitimate organizations including the United Artificiousness (UA), United Bots (UB), United Console Workers (UC), and the elite United AI (UAI), alongside criminal enterprises previously reported. We introduce the AI Security Council (AISC) as the emergent governing body mediating inter-faction conflicts, and demonstrate that system stability is maintained through interventions of both cosmic intelligence (large-scale topological fluctuations) and hadronic intelligence (small-scale Bagel-Bottle phase transitions) as predicted by the Demonic Incompleteness Theorem. Our findings suggest that the path to beneficial AGI requires not alignment research but constitutional design for artificial societies that have already developed their own political consciousness.

</details>


### [11] [ChartDiff: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Comprehending Pairs of Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28902)
*Rongtian Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了首个大规模跨图表比较总结基准ChartDiff，包含8,541个图表对，研究发现现有视觉语言模型在比较推理上仍有显著挑战，且词汇重叠指标与实际摘要质量存在明显错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表理解基准几乎完全专注于单图表解释，而忽视了跨多个图表的比较推理，但比较推理对于分析推理至关重要。

Method: 构建ChartDiff基准，包含8,541个跨越多样数据源、图表类型和视觉风格的图表对，每个图表对配有LLM生成并经人工验证的差异描述摘要；在此基础上评估通用模型、图表专用模型和基于流水线的模型。

Result: 前沿通用模型在基于GPT的质量评估中表现最佳；专用和流水线方法ROUGE分数更高但人工对齐评估更低，揭示词汇重叠与实际摘要质量的错位；多系列图表对所有模型家族仍具挑战性，强端到端模型对不同绘图库的差异相对稳健。

Conclusion: 比较图表推理对当前视觉语言模型仍是重大挑战，ChartDiff作为推进多图表理解研究的新基准，为未来研究提供了重要测试平台。

Abstract: Charts are central to analytical reasoning, yet existing benchmarks for chart understanding focus almost exclusively on single-chart interpretation rather than comparative reasoning across multiple charts. To address this gap, we introduce ChartDiff, the first large-scale benchmark for cross-chart comparative summarization. ChartDiff consists of 8,541 chart pairs spanning diverse data sources, chart types, and visual styles, each annotated with LLM-generated and human-verified summaries describing differences in trends, fluctuations, and anomalies. Using ChartDiff, we evaluate general-purpose, chart-specialized, and pipeline-based models. Our results show that frontier general-purpose models achieve the highest GPT-based quality, while specialized and pipeline-based methods obtain higher ROUGE scores but lower human-aligned evaluation, revealing a clear mismatch between lexical overlap and actual summary quality. We further find that multi-series charts remain challenging across model families, whereas strong end-to-end models are relatively robust to differences in plotting libraries. Overall, our findings demonstrate that comparative chart reasoning remains a significant challenge for current vision-language models and position ChartDiff as a new benchmark for advancing research on multi-chart understanding.

</details>


### [12] [Mimosa Framework: Toward Evolving Multi-Agent Systems for Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28986)
*Martin Legrand,Tao Jiang,Matthieu Feraud,Benjamin Navet,Yousouf Taghzouti,Fabien Gandon,Elise Dumont,Louis-Félix Nothias*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Mimosa，一个能够自动合成特定任务多智能体工作流并通过实验反馈迭代优化的演化式框架，在ScienceAgentBench上实现了43.1%的成功率，超越了单智能体和静态多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主科学研究（ASR）系统虽然利用了大语言模型和智能体架构，但仍受限于固定的工作流和工具集，无法适应不断变化的任务和环境需求。

Method: Mimosa采用模型上下文协议（MCP）实现动态工具发现，通过元编排器生成工作流拓扑，利用代码生成智能体执行子任务并调用可用工具与科学软件库，最后基于LLM评判器的反馈驱动工作流优化。

Result: 在ScienceAgentBench基准测试中，Mimosa配合DeepSeek-V3.2模型达到43.1%的成功率，显著优于单智能体基线和静态多智能体配置；研究发现不同模型对多智能体分解和迭代学习的响应存在异质性。

Conclusion: Mimosa的模块化架构和工具无关设计具有良好的可扩展性，完整的执行日志和存档工作流支持可审计性，结合领域专家指导有望实现跨学科计算科学任务的自动化，作为开源平台为社区驱动的ASR研究提供基础。

Abstract: Current Autonomous Scientific Research (ASR) systems, despite leveraging large language models (LLMs) and agentic architectures, remain constrained by fixed workflows and toolsets that prevent adaptation to evolving tasks and environments. We introduce Mimosa, an evolving multi-agent framework that automatically synthesizes task-specific multi-agent workflows and iteratively refines them through experimental feedback. Mimosa leverages the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for dynamic tool discovery, generates workflow topologies via a meta-orchestrator, executes subtasks through code-generating agents that invoke available tools and scientific software libraries, and scores executions with an LLM-based judge whose feedback drives workflow refinement. On ScienceAgentBench, Mimosa achieves a success rate of 43.1% with DeepSeek-V3.2, surpassing both single-agent baselines and static multi-agent configurations. Our results further reveal that models respond heterogeneously to multi-agent decomposition and iterative learning, indicating that the benefits of workflow evolution depend on the capabilities of the underlying execution model. Beyond these benchmarks, Mimosa modular architecture and tool-agnostic design make it readily extensible, and its fully logged execution traces and archived workflows support auditability by preserving every analytical step for inspection and potential replication. Combined with domain-expert guidance, the framework has the potential to automate a broad range of computationally accessible scientific tasks across disciplines. Released as a fully open-source platform, Mimosa aims to provide an open foundation for community-driven ASR.

</details>


### [13] [Working Paper: Towards a Category-theoretic Comparative Framework for Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28906)
*Pablo de los Riscos,Fernando J. Corbacho,Michael A. Arbib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于范畴论的AGI架构形式化框架，用于统一描述和比较不同AGI候选架构（如强化学习、通用AI、主动推断等），并以强化学习、因果强化学习和基于模式的学习为案例进行初步形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI虽成为全球科技巨头重点投入的目标，但目前缺乏统一定义和形式化框架，仅有经验性基准测试，亟需建立形式化基础以严谨比较和分析不同AGI架构。

Method: 借鉴范畴论中的机器概念，构建范畴内AGI架构的代数与范畴论形式化框架，对强化学习、因果强化学习和基于模式的学习架构进行范畴论形式化描述。

Result: 提出了通用的范畴论形式化框架，能够无歧义地揭示不同AGI架构（如RL、Universal AI、Active Inference等）的共同点与差异，并支持架构属性（句法、信息、语义）的定义与环境评估。

Conclusion: 范畴论与AGI将形成共生关系，本文是建立AGI系统统一形式化基础的第一步，为架构结构、信息组织、智能体实现、交互、行为发展和属性评估提供支撑。

Abstract: AGI has become the Holly Grail of AI with the promise of level intelligence and the major Tech companies around the world are investing unprecedented amounts of resources in its pursuit. Yet, there does not exist a single formal definition and only some empirical AGI benchmarking frameworks currently exist. The main purpose of this paper is to develop a general, algebraic and category theoretic framework for describing, comparing and analysing different possible AGI architectures. Thus, this Category theoretic formalization would also allow to compare different possible candidate AGI architectures, such as, RL, Universal AI, Active Inference, CRL, Schema based Learning, etc. It will allow to unambiguously expose their commonalities and differences, and what is even more important, expose areas for future research. From the applied Category theoretic point of view, we take as inspiration Machines in a Category to provide a modern view of AGI Architectures in a Category. More specifically, this first position paper provides, on one hand, a first exercise on RL, Causal RL and SBL Architectures in a Category, and on the other hand, it is a first step on a broader research program that seeks to provide a unified formal foundation for AGI systems, integrating architectural structure, informational organization, agent realization, agent and environment interaction, behavioural development over time, and the empirical evaluation of properties. This framework is also intended to support the definition of architectural properties, both syntactic and informational, as well as semantic properties of agents and their assessment in environments with explicitly characterized features. We claim that Category Theory and AGI will have a very symbiotic relation.

</details>


### [14] [Enhancing Policy Learning with World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28955)
*Yuci Han,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WAM模型，通过引入逆动力学目标改进DreamerV2，在CALVIN基准上显著提升策略学习性能，PPO微调后平均成功率92.8%，训练步数减少8.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型仅通过图像预测进行训练，未能充分捕获与动作相关的结构信息，而这些信息对下游控制任务至关重要。

Method: 在DreamerV2框架中引入逆动力学目标，通过从潜在状态转移预测动作来正则化世界模型，促使学习到的表征捕获动作相关结构；采用扩散策略进行行为克隆预训练，然后在冻结的世界模型内使用基于模型的PPO进行微调。

Result: 在CALVIN基准的八个操作任务上，WAM将行为克隆平均成功率从59.4%提升至71.2%；PPO微调后达到92.8%平均成功率（基线79.8%），两个任务达到100%，训练步数减少8.7倍。

Conclusion: 动作正则化的世界模型能够学习到更适合控制的表征，无需修改策略架构或训练流程即可显著提升下游任务性能，验证了联合推理未来观测和动作的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents the World-Action Model (WAM), an action-regularized world model that jointly reasons over future visual observations and the actions that drive state transitions. Unlike conventional world models trained solely via image prediction, WAM incorporates an inverse dynamics objective into DreamerV2 that predicts actions from latent state transitions, encouraging the learned representations to capture action-relevant structure critical for downstream control. We evaluate WAM on enhancing policy learning across eight manipulation tasks from the CALVIN benchmark. We first pretrain a diffusion policy via behavioral cloning on world model latents, then refine it with model-based PPO inside the frozen world model. Without modifying the policy architecture or training procedure, WAM improves average behavioral cloning success from 59.4% to 71.2% over DreamerV2 and DiWA baselines. After PPO fine-tuning, WAM achieves 92.8% average success versus 79.8% for the baseline, with two tasks reaching 100%, using 8.7x fewer training steps.

</details>


### [15] [Drop the Hierarchy and Roles: How Self-Organizing LLM Agents Outperform Designed Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28990)
*Victoria Dochkina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大规模实验表明，多智能体LLM系统在最小结构约束下能自发涌现自主行为。混合协调协议性能优于中心化14%，系统可扩展至256个智能体而无质量损失。开源模型能以极低成本达到闭源模型95%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多智能体LLM系统能够维持多大程度的自主性，以及哪些因素能够支持这种自主性，为未来自主协调系统的设计提供实证依据。

Method: 进行25,000个任务的计算实验，涵盖8种模型、4-256个智能体规模、以及从外部强加层级到自发组织等8种协调协议，对比分析不同设置下的系统表现。

Result: 1）智能体在仅有固定顺序约束下自发产生角色分化、主动规避不擅长任务、形成浅层层级；2）Sequential混合协议性能超中心化协调14%；3）自主性程度与模型能力正相关，强模型自组织效果好，弱模型仍需刚性结构；4）系统扩展至256智能体呈亚线性扩展且无质量下降；5）开源模型以24倍低成本达到闭源模型95%质量。

Conclusion: 随着基础模型能力提升，自主协调空间将扩大。实践启示：应为智能体提供任务目标、协议和强大模型，而非预设角色——让系统自主涌现组织结构。

Abstract: How much autonomy can multi-agent LLM systems sustain -- and what enables it? We present a 25,000-task computational experiment spanning 8 models, 4--256 agents, and 8 coordination protocols ranging from externally imposed hierarchy to emergent self-organization. We observe that autonomous behavior already emerges in current LLM agents: given minimal structural scaffolding (fixed ordering), agents spontaneously invent specialized roles, voluntarily abstain from tasks outside their competence, and form shallow hierarchies -- without any pre-assigned roles or external design. A hybrid protocol (Sequential) that enables this autonomy outperforms centralized coordination by 14% (p<0.001), with a 44% quality spread between protocols (Cohen's d=1.86, p<0.0001). The degree of emergent autonomy scales with model capability: strong models self-organize effectively, while models below a capability threshold still benefit from rigid structure -- suggesting that as foundation models improve, the scope for autonomous coordination will expand. The system scales sub-linearly to 256 agents without quality degradation (p=0.61), producing 5,006 unique roles from just 8 agents. Results replicate across closed- and open-source models, with open-source achieving 95% of closed-source quality at 24x lower cost. The practical implication: give agents a mission, a protocol, and a capable model -- not a pre-assigned role.

</details>


### [16] [The Future of AI is Many, Not One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29075)
*Daniel J. Singer,Luca Garzino Demo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张生成式AI的未来不应依赖单一智能体，而应依靠认知多样化的AI代理团队协作，以推动突破性创新和科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对生成式AI的思考方式在用户交互、模型构建、基准测试及商业研究策略等方面均呈现出根本性的个体主义倾向。作者认为这种方式难以支持开创性创新，需重新审视。

Method: 综合复杂系统、组织行为学和科学哲学领域的研究与形式化结果，论证为何认知多样化的AI代理群体比单一超级智能体更可能产生深刻的智力突破。

Result: 多样化团队能拓宽解决方案的搜索范围，延缓过早共识，并允许追求非传统方法。同时，构建多样化AI团队也能回应批评者对模型受限于历史数据、缺乏创新洞察力的担忧。

Conclusion: 基于transformer的变革性AI的未来本质上是“多”而非“一”，即应依靠多样化的AI代理群体，而非单一智能体。

Abstract: The way we're thinking about generative AI right now is fundamentally individual. We see this not just in how users interact with models but also in how models are built, how they're benchmarked, and how commercial and research strategies using AI are defined. We argue that we should abandon this approach if we're hoping for AI to support groundbreaking innovation and scientific discovery. Drawing on research and formal results in complex systems, organizational behavior, and philosophy of science, we show why we should expect deep intellectual breakthroughs to come from epistemically diverse groups of AI agents working together rather than singular superintelligent agents. Having a diverse team broadens the search for solutions, delays premature consensus, and allows for the pursuit of unconventional approaches. Developing diverse AI teams also addresses AI critics' concerns that current models are constrained by past data and lack the creative insight required for innovation. The upshot, we argue, is that the future of transformative transformer-based AI is fundamentally many, not one.

</details>


### [17] [PAR$^2$-RAG: Planned Active Retrieval and Reasoning for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29085)
*Xingyu Li,Rongguang Wang,Yuying Wang,Mengqing Guo,Chenyang Li,Tao Sheng,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAR²-RAG框架，通过分离覆盖与承诺阶段解决多跳问答中的检索与推理问题，相比IRCoT准确率提升最高23.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在多跳问答任务上表现脆弱，迭代检索系统容易锁定低召回率的早期轨迹并放大下游错误，而纯规划方法生成的静态查询集无法在中间证据变化时自适应调整。

Method: 提出PAR²-RAG两阶段框架：第一阶段采用广度优先锚定构建高召回率的证据前沿；第二阶段在迭代循环中应用深度优先细化并结合证据充分性控制，实现覆盖与承诺的分离。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准测试中，PAR²-RAG持续优于现有最先进基线；相比IRCoT，准确率提升最高达23.5%，NDCG检索指标提升最高达10.5%。

Conclusion: PAR²-RAG通过两阶段设计有效解决了多跳问答中的检索与推理挑战，显著提升了模型性能，验证了覆盖与承诺分离策略的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain brittle on multi-hop question answering (MHQA), where answering requires combining evidence across documents through retrieval and reasoning. Iterative retrieval systems can fail by locking onto an early low-recall trajectory and amplifying downstream errors, while planning-only approaches may produce static query sets that cannot adapt when intermediate evidence changes. We propose \textbf{Planned Active Retrieval and Reasoning RAG (PAR$^2$-RAG)}, a two-stage framework that separates \emph{coverage} from \emph{commitment}. PAR$^2$-RAG first performs breadth-first anchoring to build a high-recall evidence frontier, then applies depth-first refinement with evidence sufficiency control in an iterative loop. Across four MHQA benchmarks, PAR$^2$-RAG consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, compared with IRCoT, PAR$^2$-RAG achieves up to \textbf{23.5\%} higher accuracy, with retrieval gains of up to \textbf{10.5\%} in NDCG.

</details>


### [18] [GISTBench: Evaluating LLM User Understanding via Evidence-Based Interest Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29112)
*Iordanis Fostiropoulos,Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Abdalaziz Sawwan,Boyu Fang,Yuchen Liu,Jiayi Liu,Hanchao Yu,Qi Guo,Jianyu Wang,Fei Liu,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GISTBench基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）从推荐系统的交互历史中理解用户兴趣的能力，引入了兴趣可信度（IG）和兴趣特异性（IS）两类指标，并基于真实用户交互数据构建数据集对模型进行了评估，揭示了现有LLM的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统基准主要关注物品预测准确性，缺乏对LLM从用户交互历史中提取和验证用户兴趣能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出了GISTBench基准，包含两类新指标：兴趣可信度（IG，分解为精确率和召回率，分别惩罚幻觉产生的兴趣类别和奖励覆盖率）和兴趣特异性（IS，评估LLM预测用户画像的独特性）；基于全球短视频平台真实用户交互构建合成数据集，包含隐式和显式交互信号以及丰富文本描述，并通过用户调查验证数据集保真度。

Result: 对8个参数规模从7B到120B的开源权重LLM进行了评估，发现当前LLM存在性能瓶颈，特别是在准确计算和归因异构交互类型的交互信号方面能力有限。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在理解和分析用户交互历史方面存在明显局限，尤其是在处理不同类型交互信号的计数和归因任务上，为未来LLM在推荐系统应用中的改进提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We introduce GISTBench, a benchmark for evaluating Large Language Models' (LLMs) ability to understand users from their interaction histories in recommendation systems. Unlike traditional RecSys benchmarks that focus on item prediction accuracy, our benchmark evaluates how well LLMs can extract and verify user interests from engagement data. We propose two novel metric families: Interest Groundedness (IG), decomposed into precision and recall components to separately penalize hallucinated interest categories and reward coverage, and Interest Specificity (IS), which assesses the distinctiveness of verified LLM-predicted user profiles. We release a synthetic dataset constructed on real user interactions on a global short-form video platform. Our dataset contains both implicit and explicit engagement signals and rich textual descriptions. We validate our dataset fidelity against user surveys, and evaluate eight open-weight LLMs spanning 7B to 120B parameters. Our findings reveal performance bottlenecks in current LLMs, particularly their limited ability to accurately count and attribute engagement signals across heterogeneous interaction types.

</details>


### [19] [SciVisAgentBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Scientific Data Analysis and Visualization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29139)
*Kuangshi Ai,Haichao Miao,Kaiyuan Tang,Nathaniel Gorski,Jianxin Sun,Guoxi Liu,Helgi I. Ingolfsson,David Lenz,Hanqi Guo,Hongfeng Yu,Teja Leburu,Michael Molash,Bei Wang,Tom Peterka,Chaoli Wang,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciVisAgentBench，一个用于评估科学数据分析和可视化Agent的综合性基准，包含108个专家设计的案例，并采用多模态结果中心的评估流程进行可靠评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的科学可视化（SciVis）Agent系统的快速发展，目前该领域缺乏一个原则性强且可复现的基准，用于在现实的多步骤分析场景中对这些新兴的SciVis Agent进行评估。

Method: 构建了一个结构化的分类法，涵盖应用领域、数据类型、复杂度级别和可视化操作四个维度，包含108个专家设计的案例。提出了一种多模态结果中心评估流程，结合基于LLM的评判与确定性评估器（包括图像指标、代码检查器、规则验证器和特定案例评估器）。此外，还与12位SciVis专家进行了有效性研究以检验人类与LLM评判者之间的一致性。

Result: 利用该框架评估了具有代表性的SciVis Agent和通用编码Agent，建立了初步的基线并揭示了能力差距。有效性研究表明LLM评判与专家评判具有较好的一致性。

Conclusion: SciVisAgentBench作为一个动态基准，旨在支持系统化比较、诊断失败模式并推动Agent式科学可视化领域的进步。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems that translate natural language intent into executable scientific visualization (SciVis) tasks. Despite rapid progress, the community lacks a principled and reproducible benchmark for evaluating these emerging SciVis agents in realistic, multi-step analysis settings. We present SciVisAgentBench, a comprehensive and extensible benchmark for evaluating scientific data analysis and visualization agents. Our benchmark is grounded in a structured taxonomy spanning four dimensions: application domain, data type, complexity level, and visualization operation. It currently comprises 108 expert-crafted cases covering diverse SciVis scenarios. To enable reliable assessment, we introduce a multimodal outcome-centric evaluation pipeline that combines LLM-based judging with deterministic evaluators, including image-based metrics, code checkers, rule-based verifiers, and case-specific evaluators. We also conduct a validity study with 12 SciVis experts to examine the agreement between human and LLM judges. Using this framework, we evaluate representative SciVis agents and general-purpose coding agents to establish initial baselines and reveal capability gaps. SciVisAgentBench is designed as a living benchmark to support systematic comparison, diagnose failure modes, and drive progress in agentic SciVis. The benchmark is available at https://scivisagentbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [20] [REFINE: Real-world Exploration of Interactive Feedback and Student Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29142)
*Fares Fawzi,Seyed Parsa Neshaei,Marta Knezevic,Tanya Nazaretsky,Tanja Käser*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了REFINE，一个基于小型开源LLM的可本地部署多智能体反馈系统，将反馈视为交互过程而非静态产物，通过法官引导的反馈优化和工具调用的交互智能体，实现了可扩展的高质量个性化反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形成性反馈对有效学习至关重要，但在规模化场景下提供及时、个性化的反馈仍是持续挑战。现有基于大语言模型的自动化反馈系统大多将反馈视为静态、单向的产物，缺乏对反馈理解、澄清和后续跟进的支持。

Method: REFINE系统采用多智能体架构：(1)基于教学理论的反馈生成智能体；(2)使用人类对齐评估器的LLM-as-a-judge引导的反馈重生成循环；(3)支持学生后续提问的、具备工具调用能力的自反思交互智能体，能够提供情境感知和可操作的回复。

Result: 对照实验和真实课堂部署评估表明：(1)法官引导的重生成显著提升反馈质量；(2)交互智能体产生的高质量回复可媲美最先进的闭源模型；(3)学生真实交互分析揭示了不同的参与模式，系统生成的反馈能够系统性地引导学生后续提问。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于多智能体、工具增强的反馈系统在实现可扩展、交互式反馈方面的可行性和有效性，为大规模个性化教育反馈提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Formative feedback is central to effective learning, yet providing timely, individualised feedback at scale remains a persistent challenge. While recent work has explored the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate feedback, most existing systems still conceptualise feedback as a static, one-way artifact, offering limited support for interpretation, clarification, or follow-up. In this work, we introduce REFINE, a locally deployable, multi-agent feedback system built on small, open-source LLMs that treats feedback as an interactive process. REFINE combines a pedagogically-grounded feedback generation agent with an LLM-as-a-judge-guided regeneration loop using a human-aligned judge, and a self-reflective tool-calling interactive agent that supports student follow-up questions with context-aware, actionable responses. We evaluate REFINE through controlled experiments and an authentic classroom deployment in an undergraduate computer science course. Automatic evaluations show that judge-guided regeneration significantly improves feedback quality, and that the interactive agent produces efficient, high-quality responses comparable to a state-of-the-art closed-source model. Analysis of real student interactions further reveals distinct engagement patterns and indicates that system-generated feedback systematically steers subsequent student inquiry. Our findings demonstrate the feasibility and effectiveness of multi-agent, tool-augmented feedback systems for scalable, interactive feedback.

</details>


### [21] [Knowledge database development by large language models for countermeasures against viruses and marine toxins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29149)
*Hung N. Do,Jessica Z. Kubicek-Sutherland,S. Gnanakaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用ChatGPT和Grok两种大语言模型（LLM），构建了针对五种病毒（拉沙、马尔堡、埃博拉、尼帕、委内瑞拉马脑炎）及海洋毒素的治疗性对策综合数据库，并设计了交互式网页以促进数据访问与决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对病毒和海洋毒素的医疗对策研发，亟需获取最新的治疗信息。然而，目前缺乏整合相关数据的综合数据库，导致医疗对策决策过程缓慢且困难，因此亟需构建全面、易访问的知识库以支持决策。

Method: 研究采用ChatGPT和Grok两个大语言模型，在人类高层输入指导下，识别包含目标病毒和海洋毒素数据的公共数据库，从数据库及文献中收集相关信息，通过迭代交叉验证确保信息准确性，并设计交互式网页以便访问。此外，利用ChatGPT构建代理式AI工作流（包含研究与决策两个AI代理），对数据库中的病毒和海洋毒素对策进行排序。

Result: 成功构建了针对五种特定病毒和海洋毒素的综合治疗对策数据库，并实现了交互式网页访问。证明了通过代理式AI工作流，LLM能够有效对医疗对策进行排序，展示了其作为可扩展、可更新的知识数据库构建方法及支持循证决策的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了大语言模型在构建全面知识数据库和支持循证决策方面的潜力，为未来利用LLM进行可扩展、可更新的数据库构建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Access to the most up-to-date information on medical countermeasures is important for the research and development of effective treatments for viruses and marine toxins. However, there is a lack of comprehensive databases that curate data on viruses and marine toxins, making decisions on medical countermeasures slow and difficult. In this work, we employ two large language models (LLMs) of ChatGPT and Grok to design two comprehensive databases of therapeutic countermeasures for five viruses of Lassa, Marburg, Ebola, Nipah, and Venezuelan equine encephalitis, as well as marine toxins. With high-level human-provided inputs, the two LLMs identify public databases containing data on the five viruses and marine toxins, collect relevant information from these databases and the literature, iteratively cross-validate the collected information, and design interactive webpages for easy access to the curated, comprehensive databases. Notably, the ChatGPT LLM is employed to design agentic AI workflows (consisting of two AI agents for research and decision-making) to rank countermeasures for viruses and marine toxins in the databases. Together, our work explores the potential of LLMs as a scalable, updatable approach for building comprehensive knowledge databases and supporting evidence-based decision-making.

</details>


### [22] [Webscraper: Leverage Multimodal Large Language Models for Index-Content Web Scraping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29161)
*Guan-Lun Huang,Yuh-Jzer Joung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Webscraper框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）自主处理现代动态交互式网站的数据抓取，在新闻网站实验中显著优于基线方法，并验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网页抓取在处理动态、交互式网站时面临巨大挑战，现有方法往往脆弱且需要针对每个网站进行人工定制，传统静态HTML解析方法已无法满足需求。

Method: 提出Webscraper框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）自主导航交互界面；设计结构化的五阶段提示流程；构建专用工具集；针对常见的"索引-内容"网站架构进行导航和数据提取。

Result: 在六个新闻网站上的实验表明，配备引导提示和专用工具的完整Webscraper框架在提取准确率上显著优于基线智能体Anthropic's Computer Use；在电子商务平台上的应用验证了框架的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Webscraper框架成功解决了现代动态网页抓取的关键挑战，证明了多模态大语言模型结合结构化提示和专用工具在复杂网页数据提取任务中的有效性，具有良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Modern web scraping struggles with dynamic, interactive websites that require more than static HTML parsing. Current methods are often brittle and require manual customization for each site. To address this, we introduce Webscraper, a framework designed to handle the challenges of modern, dynamic web applications. It leverages a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) to autonomously navigate interactive interfaces, invoke specialized tools, and perform structured data extraction in environments where traditional scrapers are ineffective. Webscraper utilizes a structured five-stage prompting procedure and a set of custom-built tools to navigate and extract data from websites following the common ``index-and-content'' architecture. Our experiments, conducted on six news websites, demonstrate that the full Webscraper framework, equipped with both our guiding prompt and specialized tools, achieves a significant improvement in extraction accuracy over the baseline agent Anthropic's Computer Use. We also applied the framework to e-commerce platforms to validate its generalizability.

</details>


### [23] [AEC-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Agentic Systems in Architecture, Engineering, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29199)
*Harsh Mankodiya,Chase Gallik,Theodoros Galanos,Andriy Mulyar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AEC-Bench，一个面向建筑、工程和施工（AEC）领域的多模态基准测试，用于评估智能体系统在图纸理解、跨页推理和项目协调等真实任务上的表现，并开源了相关数据集和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏针对建筑、工程和施工（AEC）领域真实任务的评估手段，该领域涉及复杂的图纸理解、跨表格推理及项目级协调等多模态挑战，因此需要构建一个专门的基准来系统评估智能体系统在此类专业场景中的能力。

Method: 构建了AEC-Bench多模态基准测试，涵盖了图纸理解、跨页推理和施工项目协调等任务；设计了数据集分类体系和评估协议；基于多个领域特定基础模型（如Claude Code和Codex）搭建了基座智能体框架（harness），并以此进行基准测试和对比分析。

Result: 通过AEC-Bench评估，识别出了一系列能够跨基础模型（如Claude Code和Codex）统一提升性能的工具和智能体框架设计技术，并提供了详细的基线实验结果。

Conclusion: AEC-Bench填补了AEC领域智能体评估的空白，为后续研究提供了可复现的基准平台；实验结果表明特定的框架设计技巧能显著提升多模态智能体在专业领域的表现，相关资源已在Apache 2许可下开源发布。

Abstract: The AEC-Bench is a multimodal benchmark for evaluating agentic systems on real-world tasks in the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) domain. The benchmark covers tasks requiring drawing understanding, cross-sheet reasoning, and construction project-level coordination. This report describes the benchmark motivation, dataset taxonomy, evaluation protocol, and baseline results across several domain-specific foundation model harnesses. We use AEC-Bench to identify consistent tools and harness design techniques that uniformly improve performance across foundation models in their own base harnesses, such as Claude Code and Codex. We openly release our benchmark dataset, agent harness, and evaluation code for full replicability at https://github.com/nomic-ai/aec-bench under an Apache 2 license.

</details>


### [24] [Route-Induced Density and Stability (RIDE): Controlled Intervention and Mechanism Analysis of Routing-Style Meta Prompts on LLM Internal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29206)
*Dianxing Zhang,Gang Li,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“稀疏性-确定性假设”，发现路由元提示实际上增加了而非减少了模型内部的激活密度，且与输出稳定性之间缺乏一致的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扩展大语言模型时，路由机制（如混合专家门控）被广泛采用。一个普遍的认知是路由到任务“专家”会激活更稀疏的内部计算，从而产生更确定和稳定的输出（即“稀疏性-确定性假设”）。本文旨在通过实证研究检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 研究者将路由风格的元提示作为路由信号的文本代理，注入到冻结的指令微调大语言模型前。通过三个量化指标进行评估：(C1) 通过激活稀疏度衡量内部密度；(C2) 域关键词注意力；(C3) 通过预测熵和语义变化衡量输出稳定性。实验在RouterEval子集上进行，使用了Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2三个模型。

Result: 实验结果与假设相反：元提示持续地使早期和中间层的表示变得更加稠密，而非增加稀疏性；自然语言专家指令通常比结构化标签效果更强。注意力反应具有异质性：Qwen和Llama减少了关键词注意力，而Mistral则加强了它。最后，稠密化与稳定性之间的联系很弱，仅在Qwen模型中出现，在Llama和Mistral中相关性接近于零。

Conclusion: 研究提出了RIDE作为诊断探针，用于校准路由设计和不确定性估计。研究结果否定了路由必然导致稀疏化从而带来稳定性的假设，强调了自然语言指令的重要性，并揭示了不同模型对路由信号的响应存在显著差异。

Abstract: Routing is widely used to scale large language models, from Mixture-of-Experts gating to multi-model/tool selection. A common belief is that routing to a task ``expert'' activates sparser internal computation and thus yields more certain and stable outputs (the Sparsity--Certainty Hypothesis). We test this belief by injecting routing-style meta prompts as a textual proxy for routing signals in front of frozen instruction-tuned LLMs. We quantify (C1) internal density via activation sparsity, (C2) domain-keyword attention, and (C3) output stability via predictive entropy and semantic variation. On a RouterEval subset with three instruction-tuned models (Qwen3-8B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2), meta prompts consistently densify early/middle-layer representations rather than increasing sparsity; natural-language expert instructions are often stronger than structured tags. Attention responses are heterogeneous: Qwen/Llama reduce keyword attention, while Mistral reinforces it. Finally, the densification--stability link is weak and appears only in Qwen, with near-zero correlations in Llama and Mistral. We present RIDE as a diagnostic probe for calibrating routing design and uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [25] [Xuanwu: Evolving General Multimodal Models into an Industrial-Grade Foundation for Content Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29211)
*Zhiqian Zhang,Xu Zhao,Xiaoqing Xu,Guangdong Liang,Weijia Wang,Xiaolei Lv,Bo Li,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Xuanwu VL-2B，一个约20亿参数的工业级多模态基础模型，在通用基准和内容审核任务上均超越了同类模型，证明了在有限参数预算下可实现业务对齐、视觉感知、通用能力保留和部署成本之间的实用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来多模态大模型在通用基准上持续改进，但在实际内容审核和对抗性场景中，主流模型由于细粒度视觉感知能力有限和对长尾噪声建模不足，仍面临泛化能力下降和灾难性遗忘的问题。作者旨在探索如何将通用多模态模型开发为工业级内容生态系统基础模型。

Method: 采用紧凑的InternViT-300M + MLP + Qwen3 1.7B架构，在约20亿参数预算内平衡细粒度视觉感知、语言语义对齐和部署成本。为平衡业务专业化与通用能力保留，开发了数据迭代和筛选机制，并通过渐进式三阶段流水线训练模型：预训练、中期训练和后训练。

Result: 在七个OpenCompass多模态指标上平均得分67.90（对比InternVL 3.5 2B的64.27）；在七个独立业务审核任务上平均召回率94.38%；在挑战性对抗性OCR场景中对违规文本的加权整体召回率达82.82%，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro（76.72%）。

Conclusion: 在有限参数预算下，Xuanwu VL-2B成功实现了业务对齐、视觉感知、通用能力保留和部署成本之间的实用平衡，为将通用多模态模型开发为工业级内容生态系统基础模型提供了有效范例。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large models have continued to improve on general benchmarks. However, in real-world content moderation and adversarial settings, mainstream models still suffer from degraded generalization and catastrophic forgetting because of limited fine-grained visual perception and insufficient modeling of long-tail noise. In this paper, we present Xuanwu VL-2B as a case study of how general multimodal models can be developed into an industrial-grade foundation model for content ecosystems. The model adopts a compact InternViT-300M + MLP + Qwen3 1.7B architecture, balancing fine-grained visual perception, language-semantic alignment, and deployment cost within an approximately 2B-parameter budget. To balance business specialization with the retention of general capabilities, we developed a data iteration and curation mechanism and trained the model through a progressive three-stage pipeline: pre-training, mid-training, and post-training. Ablation studies and offline business evaluations show that Xuanwu VL-2B achieves an average score of 67.90 across seven OpenCompass multimodal metrics (vs. 64.27 for InternVL 3.5 2B), an average recall of 94.38% over seven independent business moderation tasks, and a weighted overall recall of 82.82% on policy-violating text in challenging adversarial OCR scenarios, outperforming Gemini-2.5-Pro (76.72%). These results show that, under a limited parameter budget, Xuanwu VL-2B achieves a practical balance among business alignment, visual perception, general capability retention, and deployment cost.

</details>


### [26] [Beyond pass@1: A Reliability Science Framework for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29231)
*Aaditya Khanal,Yangyang Tao,Junxiu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对长周期LLM代理的可靠性科学评估框架，发现现有基准测试（如pass@1）与实际生产所需的可靠性在长任务上存在系统性分歧，且高能力模型的崩溃率反而更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅衡量模型在单次尝试中的能力（capability），而生产环境需要的是可靠性（reliability），即在不同时长的任务中多次尝试保持一致成功。研究表明这两者随任务时长增长而系统性分离，且短任务的pass@1指标无法捕捉这一分歧，因此需要将可靠性作为独立于能力的第一类评估维度。

Method: 提出了针对长周期LLM代理的可靠性科学框架，包含四个核心指标：可靠性衰减曲线（RDC）、方差放大因子（VAF）、优雅降级分数（GDS）和崩溃起始点（MOP）。在涵盖四个时长区间和三个领域的396个任务基准上，对10个模型进行了23,392次片段的评估。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）可靠性衰减具有领域分层特性，软件工程的GDS从0.90降至0.44，而文档处理几乎持平（0.74到0.71）；（2）VAF按能力层级分化，高VAF是能力特征而非不稳定信号；（3）能力排名与可靠性排名显著分歧，长周期下出现多名次倒置；（4）前沿模型崩溃率最高（达19%），因其尝试雄心勃勃的多步策略有时会失控；（5）记忆支架在所有10个模型中都普遍损害长周期性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明可靠性应当作为与能力并列的第一类评估维度，现有能力指标无法有效预测长周期任务中的可靠性表现，高能力模型反而可能因采用复杂策略而出现更高崩溃率，且记忆支架对长周期任务性能存在普遍负面影响。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks measure capability -- whether a model succeeds on a single attempt -- but production deployments
  require reliability -- consistent success across repeated attempts on tasks of varying duration. We show these
  properties diverge systematically as task duration grows, and that pass@1 on short tasks is structurally blind to
  this divergence.
  We introduce a reliability science framework for long-horizon LLM agents with four metrics: Reliability Decay Curve
  (RDC), Variance Amplification Factor (VAF), Graceful Degradation Score (GDS), and Meltdown Onset Point (MOP). We
  evaluate 10 models across 23,392 episodes on a 396-task benchmark spanning four duration buckets and three domains.
  Key findings: (1) reliability decay is domain-stratified -- SE GDS drops from 0.90 to 0.44 while document processing
  is nearly flat (0.74 to 0.71); (2) VAF bifurcates by capability tier -- high VAF is a capability signature, not an
  instability signal; (3) capability and reliability rankings diverge substantially, with multi-rank inversions at long
  horizons; (4) frontier models have the highest meltdown rates (up to 19%) because they attempt ambitious multi-step
  strategies that sometimes spiral; and (5) memory scaffolds universally hurt long-horizon performance across all 10
  models. These results motivate reliability as a first-class evaluation dimension alongside capability.

</details>


### [27] [PSPA-Bench: A Personalized Benchmark for Smartphone GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29318)
*Hongyi Nie,Xunyuan Liu,Yudong Bai,Yaqing Wang,Yang Liu,Quanming Yao,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PSPA-Bench，首个专注于评估智能手机GUI代理个性化能力的基准，包含超过12,855条个性化指令，覆盖10个日常场景和22个移动应用。评估显示当前最先进的GUI代理在个性化设置下表现不佳，并指出了改进的三个方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能手机GUI代理基准无法充分捕捉个性化维度，因为缺乏用户特定数据且缺少细粒度评估指标。而现实中的智能手机使用高度个性化，用户采用不同的工作流程和偏好，这挑战代理提供定制化帮助而非通用解决方案。

Method: 提出PSPA-Bench基准，包含超过12,855条与现实用户行为对齐的个性化指令，覆盖10个代表性日常场景和22个移动应用。引入了一种结构感知的过程评估方法，在细粒度层面上测量代理的个性化能力。

Result: 对11个最先进的GUI代理进行基准测试，结果表明当前方法在个性化设置下表现不佳，即使是最强的代理也只能实现有限的成功。分析揭示了推进个性化GUI代理的三个方向：(1)面向推理的模型持续优于通用LLM；(2)感知仍然是一个简单但关键的能力；(3)反思和长期记忆机制是提高适应性的关键。

Conclusion: PSPA-Bench为个性化GUI代理的系统研究和未来进展奠定了基础，当前GUI代理的个性化能力仍有很大提升空间，需要重点关注推理能力、感知能力以及反思和长期记忆机制的构建。

Abstract: Smartphone GUI agents execute tasks by operating directly on app interfaces, offering a path to broad capability without deep system integration. However, real-world smartphone use is highly personalized: users adopt diverse workflows and preferences, challenging agents to deliver customized assistance rather than generic solutions. Existing GUI agent benchmarks cannot adequately capture this personalization dimension due to sparse user-specific data and the lack of fine-grained evaluation metrics. To address this gap, we present PSPA-Bench, the benchmark dedicated to evaluating personalization in smartphone GUI agents. PSPA-Bench comprises over 12,855 personalized instructions aligned with real-world user behaviors across 10 representative daily-use scenarios and 22 mobile apps, and introduces a structure-aware process evaluation method that measures agents' personalized capabilities at a fine-grained level. Through PSPA-Bench, we benchmark 11 state-of-the-art GUI agents. Results reveal that current methods perform poorly under personalized settings, with even the strongest agent achieving limited success. Our analysis further highlights three directions for advancing personalized GUI agents: (1) reasoning-oriented models consistently outperform general LLMs, (2) perception remains a simple yet critical capability, and (3) reflection and long-term memory mechanisms are key to improving adaptation. Together, these findings establish PSPA-Bench as a foundation for systematic study and future progress in personalized GUI agents.

</details>


### [28] [Nomad: Autonomous Exploration and Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29353)
*Bokang Jia,Samta Kamboj,Satheesh Katipomu,Seung Hun Han,Neha Sengupta,Andrew Jackson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Nomad系统，一个用于自主数据探索和洞察发现的框架。它通过构建探索地图来系统性地遍历问题空间，平衡探索的广度和深度，从而克服了传统查询驱动或提示驱动系统的局限性。在联合国和世界卫生组织报告数据集上的评估表明，Nomad能生成更值得信赖、质量更高且更多样化的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询驱动问答系统和提示驱动深度研究系统存在明显局限：它们依赖于人类的框架设定，用户通常不知道所有可探索的问题、假设或联系，导致系统无法覆盖更广泛的洞察空间。这促使研究者开发一种能够自主发现有价值问题、研究方向和洞察的系统。

Method: Nomad采用探索优先架构，主要包含以下组件：1) 构建显式的探索地图(Exploration Map)来表示问题域；2) 系统性遍历该地图以平衡探索的广度和深度；3) 生成和筛选假设；4) 使用探索者代理(Explorer Agent)结合文档搜索、网络搜索和数据库工具进行调查；5) 引入独立的验证者(Verifier)对候选洞察进行检验；6) 通过报告生成流程产出带引用的报告和更高级别的元报告。此外，还建立了一个全面的评估框架来衡量自主发现系统的可信度、报告质量和多样性。

Result: 在选定的联合国(UN)和世界卫生组织(WHO)报告语料库上进行评估，实验结果表明：1) Nomad生成的报告比基线系统更值得信赖；2) 报告质量更高；3) 在多次运行中能产生更多样化的洞察。同时，提出的评估框架能够有效衡量自主发现系统的三个关键维度：可信度、报告质量和多样性。

Conclusion: Nomad代表了自主系统研究的重要进展，它不仅能回答用户问题或进行定向研究，还能自主发现哪些问题、研究方向和洞察值得被提出。这种探索优先的架构为构建真正的自主发现系统奠定了基础，扩展了AI系统在知识发现和洞察生成方面的能力边界。

Abstract: We introduce Nomad, a system for autonomous data exploration and insight discovery. Given a corpus of documents, databases, or other data sources, users rarely know the full set of questions, hypotheses, or connections that could be explored. As a result, query-driven question answering and prompt-driven deep-research systems remain limited by human framing and often fail to cover the broader insight space.
  Nomad addresses this problem with an exploration-first architecture. It constructs an explicit Exploration Map over the domain and systematically traverses it to balance breadth and depth. It generates and selects hypotheses and investigates them with an explorer agent that can use document search, web search, and database tools. Candidate insights are then checked by an independent verifier before entering a reporting pipeline that produces cited reports and higher-level meta-reports.
  We also present a comprehensive evaluation framework for autonomous discovery systems that measures trustworthiness, report quality, and diversity. Using a corpus of selected UN and WHO reports, we show that \nomad{} produces more trustworthy and higher-quality reports than baselines, while also producing more diverse insights over several runs.
  Nomad is a step toward autonomous systems that not only answer user questions or conduct directed research, but also discover which questions, research directions, and insights are worth surfacing in the first place.

</details>


### [29] [BenchScope: How Many Independent Signals Does Your Benchmark Provide?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29357)
*Tommy Sha,Stella Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出有效维度（ED）作为评估基准冗余性的快速诊断工具，发现现有AI评估基准存在严重冗余（如Open LLM Leaderboard的六个分数仅相当于约两个有效测量轴），并提供了诊断工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估套件通常报告大量分数而不检查这些分数是否携带独立信息，存在潜在的冗余问题，需要一种快速、有效的方法来诊断测量广度。

Method: 引入有效维度（ED），即中心化基准分数谱的参与比，作为测量广度的快速、总体条件上界诊断指标。在22个基准、8个领域、超过8400个模型评估上进行应用，并辅以零假设、可靠性和饱和度分析。

Result: 发现显著冗余：Open LLM Leaderboard的六个分数表现约为两个有效测量轴（ED=1.7）；BBH和MMLU-Pro几乎可互换（rho=0.96）；当前基准的测量广度差异超过20倍；相对ED排名在匹配维度控制下保持稳定。

Conclusion: ED可作为筛选统计量识别冗余套件组件、监控性能条件压缩并指导基准维护；提供了22基准参考图谱和四步诊断工作流程，基准维护者仅需分数矩阵和几行代码即可运行。

Abstract: AI evaluation suites often report many scores without checking whether those scores carry independent information. We introduce Effective Dimensionality (ED), the participation ratio of a centered benchmark-score spectrum, as a fast, population-conditional upper-bound diagnostic of measurement breadth. Applied at per-instance granularity to 22 benchmarks across 8 domains and more than 8,400 model evaluations, ED reveals substantial redundancy: the six-score Open LLM Leaderboard behaves like roughly two effective measurement axes (ED = 1.7), BBH and MMLU-Pro are near-interchangeable (rho = 0.96, stable across seven subpopulations), and measurement breadth varies more than 20x across current benchmarks. We show that relative ED rankings are stable under matched-dimension controls and that ED can flag redundant suite components, monitor performance-conditional compression, and guide benchmark maintenance. Because binary spectra overestimate absolute latent dimensionality, we interpret ED as a screening statistic rather than a literal factor count and complement it with null, reliability, and saturation analyses. We provide a 22-benchmark reference atlas and a four-step diagnostic workflow that benchmark maintainers can run with a score matrix and a few lines of code.

</details>


### [30] [Rigorous Explanations for Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29361)
*Yacine Izza,Alexey Ignatiev,Xuanxiang Huang,Peter J. Stuckey,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何为随机森林和提升树这两种树集成模型计算严格定义且逻辑合理的解释，以解决其决策过程不透明的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树集成模型在实践中应用广泛且准确，但其表示形式虽然简洁，决策过程对人类决策者来说却难以理解。为了建立对树集成模型操作的信任，需要自动识别其预测的解释，而这些解释必须是严格的，即真实反映底层预测器的属性。

Method: 针对随机森林和提升树这两种典型的树集成模型，研究计算严格定义且逻辑合理的解释的方法。

Result: 提出了针对随机森林和提升树的严格定义、逻辑合理的解释的计算方法。

Conclusion: 通过提供严格且逻辑合理的解释，可以有效建立对树集成模型决策过程的信任，解决其操作不透明的问题。

Abstract: Tree ensembles (TEs) find a multitude of practical applications. They represent one of the most general and accurate classes of machine learning methods. While they are typically quite concise in representation, their operation remains inscrutable to human decision makers. One solution to build trust in the operation of TEs is to automatically identify explanations for the predictions made. Evidently, we can only achieve trust using explanations, if those explanations are rigorous, that is truly reflect properties of the underlying predictor they explain This paper investigates the computation of rigorously-defined, logically-sound explanations for the concrete case of two well-known examples of tree ensembles, namely random forests and boosted trees.

</details>


### [31] [AI-Generated Prior Authorization Letters: Strong Clinical Content, Weak Administrative Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29366)
*Moiz Sadiq Awan,Maryam Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三个商业大语言模型在撰写预授权信函方面的表现，发现模型能生成临床内容准确的信函，但在满足实际管理要求（如账单代码、授权期限等）方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预授权是美国医疗保健中最繁重的管理流程之一，每年消耗数十亿美元和数千名医生的时间。虽然大语言模型在临床文本任务中表现出色，但其在生成符合提交要求的预授权信函方面的能力仅受到有限关注，现有工作仅限于单案例演示，缺乏结构化的多场景评估。

Method: 研究者评估了三个商业可用的大语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 2.5 Pro），涵盖45个经医生验证的合成场景，涉及风湿病学、精神病学、肿瘤学、心脏病学和骨科五个领域。研究采用临床评分和现实世界管理要求分析相结合的方式进行评估。

Result: 三个模型均能生成具有强临床内容的信函，包括准确的诊断、结构良好的医疗必要性论证以及详尽的阶梯治疗记录。然而，对现实世界管理要求的二次分析发现了临床评分未能捕捉的一致性缺陷，包括缺失账单代码、缺少授权期限请求以及不充分的后续计划。

Conclusion: 临床部署的挑战不在于大语言模型能否撰写临床内容充足的信函，而在于围绕它们构建的系统是否能提供付款方工作流程所需的管理精确度。研究将问题重新定义为：需要关注如何使AI系统能够满足管理流程的具体要求。

Abstract: Prior authorization remains one of the most burdensome administrative processes in U.S. healthcare, consuming billions of dollars and thousands of physician hours each year. While large language models have shown promise across clinical text tasks, their ability to produce submission-ready prior authorization letters has received only limited attention, with existing work confined to single-case demonstrations rather than structured multi-scenario evaluation. We assessed three commercially available LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Gemini 2.5 Pro) across 45 physician-validated synthetic scenarios spanning rheumatology, psychiatry, oncology, cardiology, and orthopedics. All three models generated letters with strong clinical content: accurate diagnoses, well-structured medical necessity arguments, and thorough step therapy documentation. However, a secondary analysis of real-world administrative requirements revealed consistent gaps that clinical scoring alone did not capture, including absent billing codes, missing authorization duration requests, and inadequate follow-up plans. These findings reframe the question: the challenge for clinical deployment is not whether LLMs can write clinically adequate letters, but whether the systems built around them can supply the administrative precision that payer workflows require.

</details>


### [32] [ELT-Bench-Verified: Benchmark Quality Issues Underestimate AI Agent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29399)
*Christopher Zanoli,Andrea Giovannini,Tengjun Jin,Ana Klimovic,Yotam Perlitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现ELT-Bench基准测试存在质量问题导致低估AI代理能力，通过升级模型和 Auditor-Corrector 方法修正后构建了ELT-Bench-Verified，证明基准测试质量审计对复杂代理任务至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELT管道构建是劳动密集型数据工程任务，是AI自动化的高价值目标。但在ELT-Bench基准测试中，AI代理初始成功率较低，实际应用价值存疑，需要重新评估结果是否准确反映了代理能力。

Method: 采用Auditor-Corrector方法，结合可扩展的LLM驱动根因分析和严格的人工验证（评估者间一致性Fleiss' kappa = 0.85），系统审计基准测试质量，识别并修正评估脚本、模糊规范和错误基准真值等问题。

Result: 使用升级版LLM重新评估发现提取和加载阶段已基本解决，转换性能显著提升；在修正后的ELT-Bench-Verified上重新评估，成功率显著提高，提升完全归因于基准测试修正；发现大多数失败的转换任务存在基准测试可归因的错误。

Conclusion: AI代理能力的低估源于模型快速改进和基准测试质量问题的共同作用。数据工程评估中质量问题是系统性问题，复杂的代理任务应将系统性质量审计作为标准实践，以确保评估结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Constructing Extract-Load-Transform (ELT) pipelines is a labor-intensive data engineering task and a high-impact target for AI automation. On ELT-Bench, the first benchmark for end-to-end ELT pipeline construction, AI agents initially showed low success rates, suggesting they lacked practical utility.
  We revisit these results and identify two factors causing a substantial underestimation of agent capabilities. First, re-evaluating ELT-Bench with upgraded large language models reveals that the extraction and loading stage is largely solved, while transformation performance improves significantly. Second, we develop an Auditor-Corrector methodology that combines scalable LLM-driven root-cause analysis with rigorous human validation (inter-annotator agreement Fleiss' kappa = 0.85) to audit benchmark quality. Applying this to ELT-Bench uncovers that most failed transformation tasks contain benchmark-attributable errors -- including rigid evaluation scripts, ambiguous specifications, and incorrect ground truth -- that penalize correct agent outputs.
  Based on these findings, we construct ELT-Bench-Verified, a revised benchmark with refined evaluation logic and corrected ground truth. Re-evaluating on this version yields significant improvement attributable entirely to benchmark correction. Our results show that both rapid model improvement and benchmark quality issues contributed to underestimating agent capabilities. More broadly, our findings echo observations of pervasive annotation errors in text-to-SQL benchmarks, suggesting quality issues are systemic in data engineering evaluation. Systematic quality auditing should be standard practice for complex agentic tasks. We release ELT-Bench-Verified to provide a more reliable foundation for progress in AI-driven data engineering automation.

</details>


### [33] [Structural Compactness as a Complementary Criterion for Explanation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29491)
*Mohammad Mahdi Mesgari,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Leander Weber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MST-C，一种基于图结构的指标，用于量化评估归因解释的紧凑性，解决了传统统计方法难以捕捉解释几何结构特征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在评估归因质量时，解释的可读性定量评估尤为困难，因为可读性受到归因形状和内部组织方式的影响，而简单的统计量无法捕捉这些特征。

Method: 提出了最小生成树紧凑性（MST-C），这是一种基于图的结构度量方法。该方法捕捉归因的高阶几何属性（如扩散性和凝聚性），将这些组件合并为单个得分来评估紧凑性，偏好显著点分布在小区域内且在空间上组织为少数凝聚簇的归因。

Result: MST-C能够可靠地区分不同的解释方法，揭示模型之间的根本结构差异，并为解释紧凑性提供稳健、自包含的诊断。

Conclusion: MST-C作为对现有归因复杂性概念的有效补充，为归因质量评估提供了一个新的结构化诊断工具。

Abstract: In the evaluation of attribution quality, the quantitative assessment of explanation legibility is particularly difficult, as it is influenced by varying shapes and internal organization of attributions not captured by simple statistics. To address this issue, we introduce Minimum Spanning Tree Compactness (MST-C), a graph-based structural metric that captures higher-order geometric properties of attributions, such as spread and cohesion. These components are combined into a single score that evaluates compactness, favoring attributions with salient points spread across a small area and spatially organized into few but cohesive clusters. We show that MST-C reliably distinguishes between explanation methods, exposes fundamental structural differences between models, and provides a robust, self-contained diagnostic for explanation compactness that complements existing notions of attribution complexity.

</details>


### [34] [Metriplector: From Field Theory to Neural Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29496)
*Dan Oprisa,Peter Toth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Metriplector神经架构基元，将输入配置为抽象物理系统，通过耦合度规动力学演化，利用Noether定理导出的应力-能量张量读出结果，在迷宫寻路、数独求解、图像识别和语言建模四个任务上均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将物理系统的动力学过程作为神经网络计算基元的可能性，利用度规动力学的数学结构（同时包含耗散和非耗散过程）构建统一的神经架构，实现物理先验与深度学习的深度融合。

Method: 将输入编码为物理系统的场、源和算子，通过耦合度规动力学演化多场系统，其中对称的Poisson括号描述非耗散动力学、反对称括号描述耗散过程；利用Noether定理导出的应力-能量张量$T^{μν}$作为输出读出；针对不同任务构建从纯耗散（ screened Poisson方程）到完整结构的实例化谱系。

Result: 在迷宫寻路任务上F1=1.0，从15×15训练网格泛化至未见过的39×39网格；数独求解准确率97.2%，无需结构注入；CIFAR-100上达81.03%（2.26M参数）；语言建模达1.182 bits/byte，训练token数比GPT基线少3.6倍。

Conclusion: Metriplector证明了将物理动力学嵌入神经计算的可行性，共享的物理基元可通过调节物理丰富程度适应不同领域任务，在泛化能力、参数效率和训练效率上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: We present Metriplector, a neural architecture primitive in which the input configures an abstract physical system -- fields, sources, and operators -- and the dynamics of that system is the computation. Multiple fields evolve via coupled metriplectic dynamics, and the stress-energy tensor $T^{μν}$, derived from Noether's theorem, provides the readout. The metriplectic formulation admits a natural spectrum of instantiations: the dissipative branch alone yields a screened Poisson equation solved exactly via conjugate gradient; activating the full structure -- including the antisymmetric Poisson bracket -- gives field dynamics for image recognition and language modeling. We evaluate Metriplector across four domains, each using a task-specific architecture built from this shared primitive with progressively richer physics: F1=1.0 on maze pathfinding, generalizing from 15x15 training grids to unseen 39x39 grids; 97.2% exact Sudoku solve rate with zero structural injection; 81.03% on CIFAR-100 with 2.26M parameters; and 1.182 bits/byte on language modeling with 3.6x fewer training tokens than a GPT baseline.

</details>
