*Luoxin Chen,Yichi Zhou,Huishuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRoSFI方法，通过结构化中间步骤的形式化验证提供过程奖励，解决了LLMs中结果奖励忽视错误中间步骤的问题，提升了推理可靠性且不损失准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果奖励的强化学习方法往往忽视错误的中间推理步骤，导致即使最终答案正确，推理过程仍不可靠。

Method: 提出PRoSFI（Process Reward over Structured Formal Intermediates）方法。模型生成与自然语言推理对齐的结构化中间步骤，而非直接生成形式化证明；使用形式化证明器验证每个步骤，仅完全验证的推理链获得高奖励。

Result: 该方法在不牺牲准确性的前提下增强了推理可靠性，引导模型生成逐步可机器检验的证明，产生更可信的最终答案。

Conclusion: PRoSFI为训练可信推理模型提供了一种简单有效的方法，成功平衡了推理可靠性与准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated impressive performance on complex, multi-step reasoning tasks, especially when post-trained with outcome-rewarded reinforcement learning Guo et al. 2025. However, it has been observed that outcome rewards often overlook flawed intermediate steps, leading to unreliable reasoning steps even when final answers are correct. To address this unreliable reasoning, we propose PRoSFI (Process Reward over Structured Formal Intermediates), a novel reward method that enhances reasoning reliability without compromising accuracy. Instead of generating formal proofs directly, which is rarely accomplishable for a modest-sized (7B) model, the model outputs structured intermediate steps aligned with its natural language reasoning. Each step is then verified by a formal prover. Only fully validated reasoning chains receive high rewards. The integration of formal verification guides the model towards generating step-by-step machine-checkable proofs, thereby yielding more credible final answers. PRoSFI offers a simple and effective approach to training trustworthy reasoning models.

</details>


### [36] [FlowPIE: Test-Time Scientific Idea Evolution with Flow-Guided Literature Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29557)
*Qiyao Wang,Hongbo Wang,Longze Chen,Zhihao Yang,Guhong Chen,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Hui Li,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FlowPIE框架，通过流引导的MCTS检索和测试时想法演化过程，解决了科学想法生成中同质化问题，显著提升了想法的新颖性、可行性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学想法生成方法受限于静态的“检索-生成”范式，导致生成的想法同质化严重且缺乏多样性；过度依赖参数化知识和静态文献会造成信息茧房效应。

Method: 提出FlowPIE紧密耦合的检索-生成框架：1) 使用流引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)扩展文献轨迹，基于LLM生成奖励模型(GRM)的质量评估指导自适应检索，构建多样化初始种群；2) 将想法生成建模为测试时演化过程，应用选择、交叉、变异操作和隔离岛范式，结合GRM适应度计算引入跨领域知识。

Result: 广泛评估表明，FlowPIE相比强大的LLM和智能体基线框架，能够持续产生更高新颖性、可行性和多样性的想法，同时支持测试时的奖励扩展。

Conclusion: FlowPIE通过将文献探索和想法生成建模为协同演化过程，有效缓解了过度依赖参数化知识和静态文献导致的信息茧房问题，为AI驱动的自主研究提供了更有效的科学想法生成方案。

Abstract: Scientific idea generation (SIG) is critical to AI-driven autonomous research, yet existing approaches are often constrained by a static retrieval-then-generation paradigm, leading to homogeneous and insufficiently divergent ideas. In this work, we propose FlowPIE, a tightly coupled retrieval-generation framework that treats literature exploration and idea generation as a co-evolving process. FlowPIE expands literature trajectories via a flow-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) inspired by GFlowNets, using the quality of current ideas assessed by an LLM-based generative reward model (GRM) as a supervised signal to guide adaptive retrieval and construct a diverse, high-quality initial population. Based on this population, FlowPIE models idea generation as a test-time idea evolution process, applying selection, crossover, and mutation with the isolation island paradigm and GRM-based fitness computation to incorporate cross-domain knowledge. It effectively mitigates the information cocoons arising from over-reliance on parametric knowledge and static literature. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FlowPIE consistently produces ideas with higher novelty, feasibility and diversity compared to strong LLM-based and agent-based frameworks, while enabling reward scaling during test time.

</details>


### [37] [ASI-Evolve: AI Accelerates AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29640)
*Weixian Xu,Tiantian Mi,Yixiu Liu,Yang Nan,Zhimeng Zhou,Lyumanshan Ye,Lin Zhang,Yu Qiao,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASI-Evolve是一个智能体框架，通过“学习-设计-实验-分析”闭环，首次在数据、架构和学习算法三大AI核心组件上实现AI驱动的自动化发现，在神经架构设计、预训练数据治理和强化学习算法设计上均取得超越现有SOTA的显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统在有快速反馈的短程任务上表现优异，但能否胜任成本高昂、周期长、监督弱的真实AI研究闭环尚不清楚。本文旨在探索AI能否加速AI自身的开发。

Method: 提出ASI-Evolve框架，在标准进化智能体基础上引入两个关键组件：（1）认知库，将积累的人类先验知识注入每轮探索；（2）专用分析器，将复杂实验结果提炼为可复用的见解供未来迭代使用。整体采用“学习-设计-实验-分析”闭环循环。

Result: 神经架构设计方面，发现105个SOTA线性注意力架构，最佳模型超越DeltaNet +0.97分；预训练数据治理方面，平均基准性能提升+3.96分，MMLU提升超18分；强化学习算法设计方面，在AMC32、AIME24和OlympiadBench上分别比GRPO高出+12.5、+11.67和+5.04分。初步证明该方法可迁移至数学和生物医学领域。

Conclusion: ASI-Evolve为AI加速AI开发提供了统一框架，在AI发展的基础阶段展示了AI驱动发现的可行性和有效性，为闭环AI研究的实现提供了早期证据，代表向AI自我加速迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Can AI accelerate the development of AI itself? While recent agentic systems have shown strong performance on well-scoped tasks with rapid feedback, it remains unclear whether they can tackle the costly, long-horizon, and weakly supervised research loops that drive real AI progress. We present ASI-Evolve, an agentic framework for AI-for-AI research that closes this loop through a learn-design-experiment-analyze cycle. ASI-Evolve augments standard evolutionary agents with two key components: a cognition base that injects accumulated human priors into each round of exploration, and a dedicated analyzer that distills complex experimental outcomes into reusable insights for future iterations. To our knowledge, ASI-Evolve is the first unified framework to demonstrate AI-driven discovery across three central components of AI development: data, architectures, and learning algorithms. In neural architecture design, it discovered 105 SOTA linear attention architectures, with the best discovered model surpassing DeltaNet by +0.97 points, nearly 3x the gain of recent human-designed improvements. In pretraining data curation, the evolved pipeline improves average benchmark performance by +3.96 points, with gains exceeding 18 points on MMLU. In reinforcement learning algorithm design, discovered algorithms outperform GRPO by up to +12.5 points on AMC32, +11.67 points on AIME24, and +5.04 points on OlympiadBench. We further provide initial evidence that this AI-for-AI paradigm can transfer beyond the AI stack through experiments in mathematics and biomedicine. Together, these results suggest that ASI-Evolve represents a promising step toward enabling AI to accelerate AI across the foundational stages of development, offering early evidence for the feasibility of closed-loop AI research.

</details>


### [38] [Optimizing Donor Outreach for Blood Collection Sessions: A Scalable Decision Support Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29643)
*André Carneiro,Pedro T. Monteiro,Rui Henriques*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个献血者邀请调度优化框架，通过比较二进制整数线性规划（BILP）和贪婪启发式算法，解决了多地点献血网络中的供需匹配问题，贪婪算法以较小的性能损失换取了显著的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 献血中心在管理献血者可用性的同时，面临供需匹配的挑战。虽然有针对性的外联活动很重要，但过度招募会导致献血者疲劳。现有研究在献血供应链优化和算法化献血者招募方面已有大量工作，但在多地点网络中将献血者分配到献血场次时，同时考虑资格、容量、血型需求目标、地理便利性和献血者安全性的运营问题尚未得到解决。

Method: 提出了一个献血者邀请调度优化框架，整合了献血者资格、出行便利性、血型需求目标和惩罚机制。评估了两种策略：（i）二进制整数线性规划（BILP）模型；（ii）高效的贪婪启发式算法。构建了前瞻性邀请计划管道，整合了自然出勤预测、基于分位数的需求目标和剩余容量估算。使用葡萄牙血液与移植研究所（IPST）里斯本运营区域的登记数据进行4个月窗口的邀请规划评估。

Result: 该框架在缩小里斯本运营区域供需差距方面发挥了关键作用。对照比较显示，贪婪启发式算法达到与BILP相当的结果，但峰值内存减少188倍，运行时间快115倍。权衡包括需求满足率低3.9个百分点（86.1%对90.0%），献血者-献血场次距离更大，不良反应献血者暴露更高，非高频献血者的邀请负担更重，这反映了局部优化与全局优化的差异。实验证明约束感知调度可以通过动员符合条件的非活跃/即将过期的献血者来缩小供需差距。

Conclusion: 该优化框架成功解决了多地点献血网络中献血者邀请调度的运营问题。虽然贪婪启发式算法在计算效率上远优于BILP，但在需求满足、献血者便利性和安全性方面存在权衡。研究为献血中心提供了实用的决策支持工具，能够在保证献血者体验的同时有效缓解血液供应短缺问题。

Abstract: Blood donation centers face challenges in matching supply with demand while managing donor availability. Although targeted outreach is important, it can cause donor fatigue via over-solicitation. Effective recruitment requires targeting the right donors at the right time, balancing constraints with donor convenience and eligibility. Despite extensive work on blood supply chain optimization and growing interest in algorithmic donor recruitment, the operational problem of assigning donors to sessions across a multi-site network, taking into account eligibility, capacity, blood-type demand targets, geographic convenience, and donor safety, remains unaddressed.
  We address this gap with an optimization framework for donor invitation scheduling incorporating donor eligibility, travel convenience, blood-type demand targets, and penalties. We evaluate two strategies: (i) a binary integer linear programming (BILP) formulation and (ii) an efficient greedy heuristic. Evaluation uses the registry from Instituto Português do Sangue e da Transplantação (IPST) for invite planning in the Lisbon operational region using 4-month windows. A prospective pipeline integrates organic attendance forecasting, quantile-based demand targets, and residual capacity estimation for forward-looking invitation plans. Results reveal its key role in closing the supply-demand gap in the Lisbon operational region. A controlled comparison shows that the greedy heuristic achieves results comparable to the BILP, with 188x less peak memory and 115x faster runtime; trade-offs include 3.9 pp lower demand fulfillment (86.1% vs. 90.0%), larger donor-session distance, higher adverse-reaction donor exposure, and greater invitation burden per non-high-frequency donor, reflecting local versus global optimization. Experiments assess how constraint-aware scheduling can close gaps by mobilizing eligible inactive/lapsing donors.

</details>


### [39] [View-oriented Conversation Compiler for Agent Trace Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29678)
*Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VCC（面向视图的对话编译器），将复杂的智能体原始JSONL日志编译为多种结构化视图，显著提升了上下文学习的性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体对话包含嵌套工具调用、思维链推理、子智能体调用、上下文窗口压缩边界和系统指令等深度结构化内容，其复杂性远超简单的用户-助手交互。现有工作将对话格式视为琐碎的工程细节，直接使用纯文本、JSON、YAML等格式输入会实质性降低分析质量。

Method: 提出VCC编译器，采用编译器架构（词法分析、解析、中间表示、降维、输出），将原始智能体JSONL日志转换为三类结构化视图：完整视图（无损转录，作为规范行号坐标系）、用户界面视图（重构用户实际感知的交互）和自适应视图（由相关性谓词控制的结构保持投影）。

Result: 在AppWorld上的上下文学习实验中，仅将反思器的输入格式从原始JSONL替换为VCC编译视图，在所有三种模型配置下均获得更高的通过率，同时将反思器token消耗减少一半到三分之二，并产生更简洁的学习记忆。

Conclusion: 消息格式在上下文学习中起着基础设施的作用，而非偶然的实现选择，对智能体系统的分析质量和学习效率具有重要影响。

Abstract: Agent traces carry increasing analytical value in the era of context learning and harness-driven agentic cognition, yet most prior work treats conversation format as a trivial engineering detail. Modern agent conversations contain deeply structured content, including nested tool calls and results, chain-of-thought reasoning blocks, sub-agent invocations, context-window compaction boundaries, and harness-injected system directives, whose complexity far exceeds that of simple user-assistant exchanges. Feeding such traces to a reflector or other analytical mechanism in plain text, JSON, YAML, or via grep can materially degrade analysis quality. This paper presents VCC (View-oriented Conversation Compiler), a compiler (lex, parse, IR, lower, emit) that transforms raw agent JSONL logs into a family of structured views: a full view (lossless transcript serving as the canonical line-number coordinate system), a user-interface view (reconstructing the interaction as the user actually perceived it), and an adaptive view (a structure-preserving projection governed by a relevance predicate). In a context-learning experiment on AppWorld, replacing only the reflector's input format, from raw JSONL to VCC-compiled views, leads to higher pass rates across all three model configurations tested, while cutting reflector token consumption by half to two-thirds and producing more concise learned memory. These results suggest that message format functions as infrastructure for context learning, not as an incidental implementation choice.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond the Steeper Curve: AI-Mediated Metacognitive Decoupling and the Limits of the Dunning-Kruger Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29681)
*Christopher Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“AI介导的元认知解耦”模型，指出LLM使用虽能提升短期产出表现，但会导致元认知准确性下降和能力-自信梯度扁平化，比单纯的“邓宁-克鲁格效应放大”说法更准确地解释了人机交互中的过度自信、依赖不当和迁移困难等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍观点认为生成式AI仅放大了邓宁-克鲁格效应，这种解释过于粗糙，无法准确概括现有证据，需要更精细的理论模型来解释LLM使用对认知和表现的复杂影响。

Method: 综合人机交互、学习研究和模型评估三个领域的证据，构建四变量工作模型（产出输出、潜在理解、校准准确性、自我评估能力），分析它们之间的解耦关系。

Result: 发现LLM使用能改善可观察的输出和短期任务表现，但会降低元认知准确性，并导致各技能群体间的能力-自信梯度扁平化。四变量模型比简单的邓宁-克鲁格曲线能更好地解释过度自信、过度依赖和依赖不足、拐杖效应以及迁移能力弱等问题。

Conclusion: 为工具设计、评估方法和知识工作实践提供了新的理论框架和指导原则，建议关注产出与理解之间的解耦现象，而非简单关注自信水平的变化。

Abstract: The common claim that generative AI simply amplifies the Dunning-Kruger effect is too coarse to capture the available evidence. The clearest findings instead suggest that large language model (LLM) use can improve observable output and short-term task performance while degrading metacognitive accuracy and flattening the classic competence-confidence gradient across skill groups. This paper synthesizes evidence from human-AI interaction, learning research, and model evaluation, and proposes the working model of AI-mediated metacognitive decoupling: a widening gap among produced output, underlying understanding, calibration accuracy, and self-assessed ability. This four-variable account better explains overconfidence, over- and under-reliance, crutch effects, and weak transfer than the simpler metaphor of a uniformly steeper Dunning-Kruger curve. The paper concludes with implications for tool design, assessment, and knowledge work.

</details>


### [41] [A First Step Towards Even More Sparse Encodings of Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29691)
*Florian Andreas Marwitz,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从概率分布中提取一阶逻辑公式的方法，通过减少分布中的数值数量并提取逻辑公式来极大提高编码的稀疏性，同时保留核心信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界场景可以用提升概率分布来捕获，但这些分布通常以表格或列表形式编码，需要指数级的数值数量，导致存储和计算成本高昂。

Method: 首先减少概率分布中的数值数量，然后为每个数值提取逻辑公式，并进一步最小化这些公式。这种减少和最小化过程能够增加编码的稀疏性，同时对给定的分布进行泛化。

Result: 实验评估表明，通过提取一小组短公式，可以在保留核心信息的同时，极大地提高稀疏性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效解决概率分布编码中指数级数值数量的问题，通过提取一阶逻辑公式实现高稀疏性编码，在保持信息完整性的同时显著降低了表示复杂度。

Abstract: Real world scenarios can be captured with lifted probability distributions. However, distributions are usually encoded in a table or list, requiring an exponential number of values. Hence, we propose a method for extracting first-order formulas from probability distributions that require significantly less values by reducing the number of values in a distribution and then extracting, for each value, a logical formula to be further minimized. This reduction and minimization allows for increasing the sparsity in the encoding while also generalizing a given distribution. Our evaluation shows that sparsity can increase immensely by extracting a small set of short formulas while preserving core information.

</details>


### [42] [Measuring the metacognition of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29693)
*Richard Servajean,Philippe Servajean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张采用meta-d'框架作为评估AI元认知敏感性的金标准，并结合信号检测理论（SDT）测量AI在不确定性下的决策调节能力，通过在三个大型语言模型上的实验验证了这些心理物理学框架的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地集成到决策工作流程中，管理不确定性越来越依赖于系统的元认知能力，即评估自身决策可靠性并进行调节的能力。然而，目前缺乏稳健的方法来测量AI的元认知能力，因此迫切需要建立标准化的评估框架。

Method: 采用meta-d'框架（或其无模型替代方案）评估AI的元认知敏感性——即生成能区分正确与错误响应的置信度评分的能力。同时利用信号检测理论（SDT）测量AI基于不确定性和风险自发调节决策的能力。研究在三个大型语言模型（GPT-5、DeepSeek-V3.2-Exp和Mistral-Medium-2508）上进行了两组实验：第一组进行主要判断后给出置信度评分；第二组仅进行主要判断，同时操纵与响应相关的风险。

Result: 应用meta-d'框架实现了三个维度的比较：将LLM与最优性进行比较、在不同LLM之间就特定任务进行比较、以及将同一LLM在不同任务间进行比较。应用SDT成功评估了LLM在高风险情境下是否会变得更加保守。

Conclusion: meta-d'框架和信号检测理论为评估AI元认知能力提供了稳健的心理物理学方法，能够有效测量AI的元认知敏感性和风险情境下的决策调节能力，应被采纳为AI元认知评估的金标准。

Abstract: A robust decision-making process must take into account uncertainty, especially when the choice involves inherent risks. Because artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly integrated into decision-making workflows, managing uncertainty relies more and more on the metacognitive capabilities of these systems; i.e, their ability to assess the reliability of and regulate their own decisions. Hence, it is crucial to employ robust methods to measure the metacognitive abilities of AI. This paper is primarily a methodological contribution arguing for the adoption of the meta-d' framework, or its model-free alternatives, as the gold standard for assessing the metacognitive sensitivity of AIs--the ability to generate confidence ratings that distinguish correct from incorrect responses. Moreover, we propose to leverage signal detection theory (SDT) to measure the ability of AIs to spontaneously regulate their decisions based on uncertainty and risk. To demonstrate the practical utility of these psychophysical frameworks, we conduct two series of experiments on three large language models (LLMs)--GPT-5, DeepSeek-V3.2-Exp, and Mistral-Medium-2508. In the first experiments, LLMs performed a primary judgment followed by a confidence rating. In the second, LLMs only performed the primary judgment, while we manipulated the risk associated with either response. On the one hand, applying the meta-d' framework allows us to conduct comparisons along three axes: comparing an LLM to optimality, comparing different LLMs on a given task, and comparing the same LLM across different tasks. On the other hand, SDT allows us to assess whether LLMs become more conservative when risks are high.

</details>

*Joakim Edin,Andreas Motzfeldt,Simon Flachs,Lars Maaløe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Symphony医疗编码系统，通过结合临床叙述和编码指南，实现了跨编码系统的自动化医疗编码，并在多个数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化医疗编码方法存在两大局限：1）只能预测固定代码集，难以适应新代码或不同编码系统；2）无法提供预测解释，限制了在安全关键场景中的可信度。医疗编码对计费、临床研究和质量报告至关重要，但目前仍主要依赖人工，效率低且易出错。

Method: 提出Symphony系统，模拟专家编码员的工作方式：通过直接访问编码指南对临床叙述进行推理。该设计使系统能够跨任何编码系统运行，并为每个预测代码提供跨度级别的证据，将其链接到支持该代码的文本。

Result: 在两个公共基准和三个真实世界数据集上进行评估，涵盖美国和英国的住院、门诊、急诊和亚专科环境。Symphony在所有环境中均达到最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: Symphony是一个灵活、可部署的自动化临床编码基础系统，能够跨编码系统工作并提供可解释的预测，解决了现有方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: Medical coding translates free-text clinical documentation into standardized codes drawn from classification systems that contain tens of thousands of entries and are updated annually. It is central to billing, clinical research, and quality reporting, yet remains largely manual, slow, and error-prone. Existing automated approaches learn to predict a fixed set of codes from labeled data, thereby preventing adaptation to new codes or different coding systems without retraining on different data. They also provide no explanation for their predictions, limiting trust in safety-critical settings. We introduce Symphony for Medical Coding, a system that approaches the task the way expert human coders do: by reasoning over the clinical narrative with direct access to the coding guidelines. This design allows Symphony to operate across any coding system and to provide span-level evidence linking each predicted code to the text that supports it. We evaluate on two public benchmarks and three real-world datasets spanning inpatient, outpatient, emergency, and subspecialty settings across the United States and the United Kingdom. Symphony achieves state-of-the-art results across all settings, establishing itself as a flexible, deployment-ready foundation for automated clinical coding.

</details>

*Chenyang Zuo,Siqi Fan,Yizhen Luo,Zaiqing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReTriP端到端生成框架，将逆合成规划重构为链式思维推理任务，在RetroBench上达到最先进性能。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆合成规划方法依赖单步预测与外部搜索启发式的混合框架，导致局部分子转换与全局规划目标之间的逻辑连贯性断裂，难以应对组合复杂性挑战。

Method: 提出ReTriP端到端生成框架，将逆合成规划重构为直接的链式思维（Chain-of-Thought）推理任务；建立路径连贯的分子表示，采用从推理蒸馏到带有可验证奖励的强化学习的渐进式训练课程。

Result: 在RetroBench上的实证评估表明，ReTriP达到了最先进性能，在长视野规划中相比混合基线模型展现出更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ReTriP成功将战略前瞻性直接嵌入模型的化学推理中，实现了步骤生成与实际路线效用的有效对齐，为逆合成规划提供了更连贯的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning is a fundamental task in organic chemistry, yet remains challenging due to its combinatorial complexity. To address this, conventional approaches typically rely on hybrid frameworks that combine single-step predictions with external search heuristics, inevitably fracturing the logical coherence between local molecular transformations and global planning objectives. To bridge this gap and embed sophisticated strategic foresight directly into the model's chemical reasoning, we introduce ReTriP, an end-to-end generative framework that reformulates retrosynthesis as a direct Chain-of-Thought reasoning task. We establish a path-coherent molecular representation and employ a progressive training curriculum that transitions from reasoning distillation to reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, effectively aligning stepwise generation with practical route utility. Empirical evaluation on RetroBench demonstrates that ReTriP achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting superior robustness in long-horizon planning compared to hybrid baselines.

</details>

*Junjie Zhang,Zhen Shen,Gang Xiong,Xisong Dong*

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型会自发形成类似人脑的协同核心，其中间层具有协同信息处理能力，这种组织结构在任务难度增加时会以物理相变形式涌现，且移除协同组件会导致灾难性性能损失。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用人工智能系统的进化来识别普遍的计算原则，探索大语言模型与人类大脑在信息整合方面的相似性，从而架起人工生物智能之间的桥梁。

Method: 使用整合信息分解方法，跨多种架构对大语言模型进行分析，研究不同层的信息处理特性，并通过消融实验验证协同组件的功能作用。

Result: 发现大语言模型的中间层展现协同信息处理，而早期和晚期层依赖冗余；这种组织结构是动态的，随着任务难度增加以物理相变形式涌现；移除协同组件会导致灾难性的性能损失。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中自发形成的协同核心是抽象推理的物理实体，这一发现揭示了人工神经网络与生物神经网络在信息整合方面的普遍计算原则，为理解智能的本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: The evolution of intelligence in artificial systems provides a unique opportunity to identify universal computational principles. Here we show that large language models spontaneously develop synergistic cores where information integration exceeds individual parts remarkably similar to the human brain. Using Integrated Information Decomposition across multiple architectures we find that middle layers exhibit synergistic processing while early and late layers rely on redundancy. This organization is dynamic and emerges as a physical phase transition as task difficulty increases. Crucially ablating synergistic components causes catastrophic performance loss confirming their role as the physical entity of abstract reasoning and bridging artificial and biological intelligence.

</details>


### [46] [CausalPulse: An Industrial-Grade Neurosymbolic Multi-Agent Copilot for Causal Diagnostics in Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29755)
*Chathurangi Shyalika,Utkarshani Jaimini,Cory Henson,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CausalPulse，一个面向智能制造的工业级多智能体副驾驶系统，通过神经符号架构统一了异常检测、因果发现和推理，在博世工厂实际部署并实现了高可靠性和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造环境需要实时、可信且可解释的根本原因洞察来维持生产率和质量，而传统分析流程将异常检测、因果推理和根本原因分析视为孤立阶段，限制了可扩展性和可解释性。

Method: 提出CausalPulse系统，采用基于标准化智能体协议的神经符号架构，统一异常检测、因果发现和推理，实现模块化、人在回路的设计，并集成到现有监控工作流中支持生产规模的实时操作。

Result: 在公开数据集和专有数据集上的评估显示总体成功率分别为98.0%和98.73%；规划和工具使用成功率98.75%，自我反思成功率97.3%，协作成功率99.2%；端到端延迟50-60秒，具有近似线性可扩展性(R²=0.97)。

Conclusion: CausalPulse的模块化、人在回路设计能够实现可靠、可解释且生产就绪的自动化，为下一代制造提供了有效的因果诊断解决方案，在模块化、可扩展性和部署成熟度方面优于现有工业副驾驶系统。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing environments demand real-time, trustworthy, and interpretable root-cause insights to sustain productivity and quality. Traditional analytics pipelines often treat anomaly detection, causal inference, and root-cause analysis as isolated stages, limiting scalability and explainability. In this work, we present CausalPulse, an industry-grade multi-agent copilot that automates causal diagnostics in smart manufacturing. It unifies anomaly detection, causal discovery, and reasoning through a neurosymbolic architecture built on standardized agentic protocols. CausalPulse is being deployed in a Robert Bosch manufacturing plant, integrating seamlessly with existing monitoring workflows and supporting real-time operation at production scale. Evaluations on both public (Future Factories) and proprietary (Planar Sensor Element) datasets show high reliability, achieving overall success rates of 98.0% and 98.73%. Per-criterion success rates reached 98.75% for planning and tool use, 97.3% for self-reflection, and 99.2% for collaboration. Runtime experiments report end-to-end latency of 50-60s per diagnostic workflow with near-linear scalability (R^2=0.97), confirming real-time readiness. Comparison with existing industrial copilots highlights distinct advantages in modularity, extensibility, and deployment maturity. These results demonstrate how CausalPulse's modular, human-in-the-loop design enables reliable, interpretable, and production-ready automation for next-generation manufacturing.

</details>


### [47] [Tracking vs. Deciding: The Dual-Capability Bottleneck in Searchless Chess Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29761)
*Quanhao Li,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了双能力瓶颈理论(P ≤ min(T,Q))，指出人类风格象棋引擎需要同时具备状态追踪和决策质量两种矛盾能力，通过模型扩展和Elo加权训练，最终120M参数模型在无搜索情况下达到Lichess快棋2570等级分，人类棋步预测准确率55.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有象棋引擎追求最大强度，而非模仿人类棋手的风格、错误和一致性。研究表明，仅从棋步序列训练会迫使模型学习两个相互矛盾的能力：状态追踪（从历史棋步重建棋盘）和决策质量（选择好棋），低水平对局提供追踪所需的多样性，高水平对局提供决策质量信号，两者存在数据需求冲突。

Method: 提出双能力瓶颈理论框架P ≤ min(T,Q)；将模型参数从28M扩展到120M以改善状态追踪；引入Elo加权训练提升决策质量同时保持多样性；设计2×2因子消融实验验证各组件贡献；提出覆盖率衰减公式t* = log(N/kcrit)/log b作为局内退化风险的可靠性边界。

Result: 120M参数模型在无搜索情况下达到Lichess快棋2570等级分（253局等级对局）；人类棋步预测Top-1准确率55.2%，超越Maia-2快棋和超快棋模型；消融实验表明模型扩展改善追踪能力，加权训练改善决策能力，两者组合具有超加性效应；线性加权效果最佳，过度激进的加权会损害追踪能力。

Conclusion: 双能力瓶颈理论成功指导了人类风格象棋引擎的设计；序列输入相比基于位置的方法能自然编码完整对局历史，实现依赖历史的决策；合理平衡追踪能力和决策质量是关键，为未来类人博弈AI提供了新的理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: A human-like chess engine should mimic the style, errors, and consistency of a strong human player rather than maximize playing strength. We show that training from move sequences alone forces a model to learn two capabilities: state tracking, which reconstructs the board from move history, and decision quality, which selects good moves from that reconstructed state. These impose contradictory data requirements: low-rated games provide the diversity needed for tracking, while high-rated games provide the quality signal for decision learning. Removing low-rated data degrades performance.
  We formalize this tension as a dual-capability bottleneck, P <= min(T,Q), where overall performance is limited by the weaker capability. Guided by this view, we scale the model from 28M to 120M parameters to improve tracking, then introduce Elo-weighted training to improve decisions while preserving diversity. A 2 x 2 factorial ablation shows that scaling improves tracking, weighting improves decisions, and their combination is superadditive. Linear weighting works best, while overly aggressive weighting harms tracking despite lower validation loss. We also introduce a coverage-decay formula, t* = log(N/kcrit)/log b, as a reliability horizon for intra-game degeneration risk.
  Our final 120M-parameter model, without search, reached Lichess bullet 2570 over 253 rated games. On human move prediction it achieves 55.2% Top-1 accuracy, exceeding Maia-2 rapid and Maia-2 blitz. Unlike position-based methods, sequence input naturally encodes full game history, enabling history-dependent decisions that single-position models cannot exhibit.

</details>


### [48] [Reasoning-Driven Synthetic Data Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29791)
*Tim R. Davidson,Benoit Seguin,Enrico Bacis,Cesar Ilharco,Hamza Harkous*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Simula，一个新颖的推理驱动框架，用于大规模合成数据生成与评估。该框架采用无种子、智能体的方法，具有可解释性和可控性，有效解决了多模态AI应用中训练数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AI应用需要专门的模型，但相关训练数据往往稀缺或难以获取。使用人工标注成本高昂、易出错且耗时，而现有的合成数据生成方法依赖手动提示、进化算法或大量目标分布种子数据，限制了其可扩展性、可解释性和可控性。

Method: 提出了Simula框架，采用无种子（seedless）、智能体（agentic）的方法大规模生成合成数据集。用户可以通过可解释且可控的过程定义所需的数据集特征，并实现细粒度的资源分配。该方法不依赖手动提示、进化算法或大量种子数据。

Result: 在多种数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，对内在属性和下游属性进行了严格测试。提供了合成数据机制设计的指导原则，以及在规模化生成和评估合成数据方面的见解。

Conclusion: Simula框架为数据稀缺或隐私问题严重的领域开发和部署AI提供了新的机会，在可扩展性、可解释性和可控性方面优于现有方法，为合成数据生成与评估提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although many AI applications of interest require specialized multi-modal models, relevant data to train such models is inherently scarce or inaccessible. Filling these gaps with human annotators is prohibitively expensive, error-prone, and time-consuming, leading model builders to increasingly consider synthetic data as a scalable alternative. However, existing synthetic data generation methods often rely on manual prompts, evolutionary algorithms, or extensive seed data from the target distribution - limiting their scalability, explainability, and control. In this paper, we introduce Simula: a novel reasoning-driven framework for data generation and evaluation. It employs a seedless, agentic approach to generate synthetic datasets at scale, allowing users to define desired dataset characteristics through an explainable and controllable process that enables fine-grained resource allocation. We show the efficacy of our approach on a variety of datasets, rigorously testing both intrinsic and downstream properties. Our work (1) offers guidelines for synthetic data mechanism design, (2) provides insights into generating and evaluating synthetic data at scale, and (3) unlocks new opportunities for developing and deploying AI in domains where data scarcity or privacy concerns are paramount.

</details>

*Han Deng,Anqi Zou,Hanling Zhang,Ben Fei,Chengyu Zhang,Haobo Wang,Xinru Guo,Zhenyu Li,Xuzhu Wang,Peng Yang,Fujian Zhang,Weiyu Guo,Xiaohong Shao,Zhaoyang Liu,Shixiang Tang,Zhihui Wang,Wanli Ouyang*

TL;DR: 本文提出了Owl-AuraID，一个软硬件协作的具身智能体系统，采用GUI原生范式通过与人专家相同的界面操作仪器，支持十类精密仪器和多种工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现越来越依赖高通量表征，但现有自动化受到专有GUI界面和基于API系统有限泛化能力的阻碍，需要一种更通用、实用的自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出以技能为中心的框架，整合Type-1（GUI操作）和Type-2（数据分析）技能为端到端工作流程，连接物理样品处理与科学解释，采用GUI原生范式操作仪器。

Result: 系统在十类精密仪器上展示广泛覆盖能力，支持多模态光谱分析、显微成像和晶体分析等多种工作流程，涵盖FTIR、NMR、AFM和TGA等模态。

Conclusion: Owl-AuraID为自主实验室提供了实用、可扩展的基础，展示了通过可复用的操作和分析技能发展实验室智能的路径。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly depends on high-throughput characterization, yet automation is hindered by proprietary GUIs and the limited generalizability of existing API-based systems. We present Owl-AuraID, a software-hardware collaborative embodied agent system that adopts a GUI-native paradigm to operate instruments through the same interfaces as human experts. Its skill-centric framework integrates Type-1 (GUI operation) and Type-2 (data analysis) skills into end-to-end workflows, connecting physical sample handling with scientific interpretation. Owl-AuraID demonstrates broad coverage across ten categories of precision instruments and diverse workflows, including multimodal spectral analysis, microscopic imaging, and crystallographic analysis, supporting modalities such as FTIR, NMR, AFM, and TGA. Overall, Owl-AuraID provides a practical, extensible foundation for autonomous laboratories and illustrates a path toward evolving laboratory intelligence through reusable operational and analytical skills. The code are available at https://github.com/OpenOwlab/AuraID.

</details>


### [50] [AgentFixer: From Failure Detection to Fix Recommendations in LLM Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29848)
*Hadar Mulian,Sergey Zeltyn,Ido Levy,Liane Galanti,Avi Yaeli,Segev Shlomov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对LLM智能体系统的综合验证框架，包含15个故障检测工具和2个根因分析模块，通过在IBM CUGA上的应用，成功识别并修复了规划器错位、模式违规等关键问题，使中等规模模型显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体系统在复杂任务中的应用日益广泛，其可靠性问题日益突出。现有的验证方法缺乏系统性，难以全面诊断和改进智能体系统中的各类故障，包括输入处理、提示设计和输出生成等环节的弱点。因此，需要建立一个综合性的验证框架来系统性地识别、分类和修复智能体系统中的可靠性故障。

Method: 研究提出了一个综合验证框架，包含15个故障检测工具和2个根因分析模块，采用轻量级规则检查与LLM-as-a-judge评估相结合的方法，支持结构化的事件检测、分类和修复。框架应用于IBM CUGA系统，在AppWorld和WebArena基准测试上进行评估，并通过探索性研究将诊断输出和智能体描述输入LLM进行自我反思和优先级排序。

Result: 框架成功识别了智能体系统中的重复性规划器错位、模式违规、脆弱的提示依赖等问题。基于这些洞察，通过优化提示和编码策略，在保持CUGA基准测试结果的同时，使Llama 4和Mistral Medium等中等规模模型获得了显著的准确率提升，大幅缩小了与前沿模型的差距。交互式分析产生了关于重复故障模式和改进重点领域的可操作洞察。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了向可扩展的质量保证和生产环境中智能体系统的自适应验证迈进的路径，为构建更强大、可解释和自我改进的智能体架构奠定了基础。验证过程本身可以演变为一个智能化的、对话驱动的流程，为智能体系统的可靠性保障提供了新的方法论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce a comprehensive validation framework for LLM-based agentic systems that provides systematic diagnosis and improvement of reliability failures. The framework includes fifteen failure-detection tools and two root-cause analysis modules that jointly uncover weaknesses across input handling, prompt design, and output generation. It integrates lightweight rule-based checks with LLM-as-a-judge assessments to support structured incident detection, classification, and repair. We applied the framework to IBM CUGA, evaluating its performance on the AppWorld and WebArena benchmarks. The analysis revealed recurrent planner misalignments, schema violations, brittle prompt dependencies, and more. Based on these insights, we refined both prompting and coding strategies, maintaining CUGA's benchmark results while enabling mid-sized models such as Llama 4 and Mistral Medium to achieve notable accuracy gains, substantially narrowing the gap with frontier models. Beyond quantitative validation, we conducted an exploratory study that fed the framework's diagnostic outputs and agent description into an LLM for self-reflection and prioritization. This interactive analysis produced actionable insights on recurring failure patterns and focus areas for improvement, demonstrating how validation itself can evolve into an agentic, dialogue-driven process. These results show a path toward scalable, quality assurance, and adaptive validation in production agentic systems, offering a foundation for more robust, interpretable, and self-improving agentic architectures.

</details>


### [51] [Spatiotemporal Robustness of Temporal Logic Tasks using Multi-Objective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29868)
*Oliver Schön,Lars Lindemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出时空鲁棒性(STR)概念，通过多目标推理联合刻画信号在空间和时间维度的可允许扰动，解决了现有方法仅考虑空间扰动的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于时序逻辑规范的鲁棒语义主要关注空间扰动，即信号与不满足条件之间的几何距离，但忽略了时间扰动的影响。对于多智能体机器人、智慧城市和空中交通控制等交互系统而言，空间和时间扰动往往同时存在且相互关联，因此需要一种能够联合刻画时空扰动的鲁棒性分析方法。

Method: 作者将时空鲁棒性(STR)形式化为一个多目标推理问题，通过在空间和时间扰动上定义偏序关系来刻画。该方法利用多目标优化工具进行求解，将STR解释为描述所有可允许时空扰动的Pareto最优集。为了解决计算挑战，作者提出了具有计算可行性的鲁棒语义，该语义通过适当下逼近的方式保证了对STR的逼近是可靠的，并基于此设计了监测算法。

Result: 提出了能够联合捕捉空间和时间扰动的时空鲁棒性(STR)概念，并建立了相应的多目标推理框架。证明了STR可以解释为描述所有可允许时空扰动的Pareto最优集，并开发了基于可靠鲁棒语义的监测算法。该方法成功实现了通过多目标推理处理多维度鲁棒性问题，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Conclusion: 本研究首次通过多目标推理方法解决了跨多个维度的鲁棒性问题，为交互系统的可靠性分析提供了新的理论框架和实用工具，在多智能体机器人、智慧城市和空中交通控制等应用场景中具有重要的实际价值。

Abstract: The reliability of autonomous systems depends on their robustness, i.e., their ability to meet their objectives under uncertainty. In this paper, we study spatiotemporal robustness of temporal logic specifications evaluated over discrete-time signals. Existing work has proposed robust semantics that capture not only Boolean satisfiability, but also the geometric distance from unsatisfiability, corresponding to admissible spatial perturbations of a given signal. In contrast, we propose spatiotemporal robustness (STR), which captures admissible spatial and temporal perturbations jointly. This notion is particularly informative for interacting systems, such as multi-agent robotics, smart cities, and air traffic control. We define STR as a multi-objective reasoning problem, formalized via a partial order over spatial and temporal perturbations. This perspective has two key advantages: (1) STR can be interpreted as a Pareto-optimal set that characterizes all admissible spatiotemporal perturbations, and (2) STR can be computed using tools from multi-objective optimization. To navigate computational challenges, we propose robust semantics for STR that are sound in the sense of suitably under-approximating STR while being computationally tractable. Finally, we present monitoring algorithms for STR using these robust semantics. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to deal with robustness across multiple dimensions via multi-objective reasoning.

</details>


### [52] [ShapE-GRPO: Shapley-Enhanced Reward Allocation for Multi-Candidate LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29871)
*Rui Ai,Yu Pan,David Simchi-Levi,Chonghuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有GRPO算法在集合级奖励分配中存在的“搭便车”问题，提出ShapE-GRPO方法，利用Shapley值将集合级奖励分解为细粒度的候选级信号，实现了更高效的训练与更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户-智能体交互场景（如推荐、头脑风暴、代码建议）中，大语言模型常生成候选集合，目标是最大化整个集合的效用。然而，现有的强化学习后训练范式（如GRPO）通常将同一集合级标量奖励分配给集合中的每个候选者。这导致了嘈杂的训练信号，使得较差的候选者可以“搭便车”利用单个优秀同伴产生的高奖励，从而导致次优的探索策略。

Method: 本文提出了Shapley增强的GRPO（ShapE-GRPO）。该方法利用集合级效用的排列不变性质，从合作博弈论中推导出一种Shapley增强公式，将集合级奖励分解为细粒度的、针对特定候选者的信号。该公式在保持Shapley值基本公理的同时，实现了多项式时间复杂度的计算效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，ShapE-GRPO在多种数据集上始终优于标准GRPO，并且在训练过程中表现出加速收敛的特性。

Conclusion: 通过引入博弈论中的Shapley值对集合级奖励进行细粒度分解，ShapE-GRPO有效解决了传统方法中的奖励分配噪声问题，为大语言模型在集合生成任务中的强化学习后训练提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: In user-agent interaction scenarios such as recommendation, brainstorming, and code suggestion, Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate sets of candidate recommendations where the objective is to maximize the collective utility of the entire set rather than individual candidates independently. However, existing reinforcement learning post-training paradigms, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), typically assign the same set-level scalar reward to every candidate in the set. This leads to noisy training signals where poor candidates free-ride on the high reward produced by a single strong peer, resulting in suboptimal exploration. To address this, we propose Shapley-Enhanced GRPO (ShapE-GRPO). By leveraging the permutation-invariant nature of set-level utility, we derive a Shapley-enhanced formulation from cooperative game theory to decompose set-level rewards into granular, candidate-specific signals. We show that our formulation preserves the fundamental axioms of the Shapley value while remaining computationally efficient with polynomial-time complexity. Empirically, ShapE-GRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO across diverse datasets with accelerated convergence during training.

</details>


### [53] [A Rational Account of Categorization Based on Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29895)
*Christophe J. MacLellan,Karthik Singaravadivelan,Xin Lian,Zekun Wang,Pat Langley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基于信息论理性分析的分类理论，并在经典实验数据上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类模型在解释人类分类行为时存在局限性，需要一个基于信息论的统一理论框架来更好地解释经典分类实验中的现象。

Method: 提出了一种基于信息论理性分析的分类理论，并在Hayes-Roth和Hayes-Roth (1977)、Medin和Schaffer (1978)以及Smith和Minda (1998)的经典分类实验数据上进行了验证。

Result: 该理论在解释人类分类行为方面表现至少与独立线索模型、情境模型、理性分类模型(Anderson, 1991)和层次狄利克雷过程模型(Griffiths et al., 2007)相当，甚至更优。

Conclusion: 基于信息论的理性分析为理解人类分类行为提供了一个有效的理论框架，能够很好地解释经典实验中的关键发现。

Abstract: We present a new theory of categorization based on an information-theoretic rational analysis. To evaluate this theory, we investigate how well it can account for key findings from classic categorization experiments conducted by Hayes-Roth and Hayes-Roth (1977), Medin and Schaffer (1978), and Smith and Minda (1998). We find that it explains the human categorization behavior at least as well (or better) than the independent cue and context models (Medin & Schaffer, 1978), the rational model of categorization (Anderson, 1991), and a hierarchical Dirichlet process model (Griffiths et al., 2007).

</details>


### [54] [ATP-Bench: Towards Agentic Tool Planning for MLLM Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29902)
*Yinuo Liu,Zi Qian,Heng Zhou,Jiahao Zhang,Yajie Zhang,Zhihang Li,Mengyu Zhou,Erchao Zhao,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic Tool Planning范式，让模型作为中央控制器自主决定何时何地调用何种工具来生成交错文本-图像内容；构建了包含7,702个QA对的ATP-Bench基准，并提出MAM评估系统，揭示现有MLLMs在交错规划和工具使用方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交错文本-图像生成方法将图像生成与检索增强视为互斥路径，无法同时兼顾事实性与创造性。作者认为下一阶段的重要里程碑是Agentic Tool Planning，即模型需要作为中央控制器，自主规划和调用工具以应对视觉关键查询。

Method: 1. 提出Agentic Tool Planning范式：模型作为中央控制器自主决策工具调用；
2. 构建ATP-Bench基准：包含7,702个QA对（含1,592个VQA对），覆盖8个类别和25个视觉关键意图，具备人工验证的查询和标准答案；
3. 提出Multi-Agent MLLM-as-a-Judge (MAM)评估系统：独立于端到端执行和工具后端变化，评估工具调用精度、识别工具使用遗漏机会、评估整体响应质量，无需标准答案参考。

Result: 在10个SOTA MLLMs上的广泛实验表明：模型在连贯的交错规划方面表现困难，工具使用行为存在显著差异，说明当前模型在此能力上有很大的改进空间。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前MLLMs在交错文本-图像生成规划方面的不足，为该领域的未来发展提供了可操作的指导方向，推动了交错生成技术的进步。数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Interleaved text-and-image generation represents a significant frontier for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), offering a more intuitive way to convey complex information. Current paradigms rely on either image generation or retrieval augmentation, yet they typically treat the two as mutually exclusive paths, failing to unify factuality with creativity. We argue that the next milestone in this field is Agentic Tool Planning, where the model serves as a central controller that autonomously determines when, where, and which tools to invoke to produce interleaved responses for visual-critical queries. To systematically evaluate this paradigm, we introduce ATP-Bench, a novel benchmark comprising 7,702 QA pairs (including 1,592 VQA pairs) across eight categories and 25 visual-critical intents, featuring human-verified queries and ground truths. Furthermore, to evaluate agentic planning independent of end-to-end execution and changing tool backends, we propose a Multi-Agent MLLM-as-a-Judge (MAM) system. MAM evaluates tool-call precision, identifies missed opportunities for tool use, and assesses overall response quality without requiring ground-truth references. Our extensive experiments on 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal that models struggle with coherent interleaved planning and exhibit significant variations in tool-use behavior, highlighting substantial room for improvement and providing actionable guidance for advancing interleaved generation. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/ATP-Bench.

</details>


### [55] [C-TRAIL: A Commonsense World Framework for Trajectory Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29908)
*Zhihong Cui,Haoran Tang,Tianyi Li,Yushuai Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了C-TRAIL框架，通过将LLM产生的常识与信任机制相结合，解决了LLM在自动驾驶轨迹规划中输出不可靠的问题，在仿真和真实数据集上显著降低了预测误差并提高了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶轨迹规划方法虽利用大语言模型(LLM)进行常识推理，但LLM输出具有固有的不可靠性，这在安全关键应用中存在风险，因此需要一种机制来量化和管理这种不确定性。

Method: 提出C-TRAIL框架，构建常识世界模型，包含三个闭环模块：Recall模块查询LLM获取语义关系并通过双重信任机制量化可靠性；Plan模块通过Dirichlet信任策略将信任加权的常识注入蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)；Update模块根据环境反馈自适应优化信任分数和策略参数。

Result: 在Highway-env的四个仿真场景和两个真实世界levelXData数据集（highD, rounD）上，C-TRAIL显著优于现有最先进基线，平均将ADE降低40.2%，FDE降低51.7%，成功率(SR)提升16.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的常识推理与显式的信任机制耦合，C-TRAIL有效提升了自动驾驶轨迹规划的安全性、可靠性和预测精度，证明了在安全关键应用中管理LLM不确定性的重要性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving increasingly leverages large language models (LLMs) for commonsense reasoning, yet LLM outputs are inherently unreliable, posing risks in safety-critical applications. We propose C-TRAIL, a framework built on a Commonsense World that couples LLM-derived commonsense with a trust mechanism to guide trajectory planning. C-TRAIL operates through a closed-loop Recall, Plan, and Update cycle: the Recall module queries an LLM for semantic relations and quantifies their reliability via a dual-trust mechanism; the Plan module injects trust-weighted commonsense into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) through a Dirichlet trust policy; and the Update module adaptively refines trust scores and policy parameters from environmental feedback. Experiments on four simulated scenarios in Highway-env and two real-world levelXData datasets (highD, rounD) show that C-TRAIL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing ADE by 40.2%, FDE by 51.7%, and improving SR by 16.9 percentage points on average. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZhihongCui/CTRAIL.

</details>


### [56] [Uncertainty Gating for Cost-Aware Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29915)
*Georgii Mikriukov,Grégoire Montavon,Marina M. -C. Höhne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知不确定性作为解释可靠性的低成本代理，用于筛选和改进黑箱模型的事后解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后解释方法被广泛用于解释黑箱模型预测，但其生成通常计算成本高昂且可靠性无法保证。因此需要一种低成本方法来评估解释的可靠性。

Method: 提出利用认知不确定性作为解释可靠性的低成本代理。高认知不确定性能够识别决策边界不清晰、解释不稳定且不忠实的区域。基于此实现了两种应用场景：1）“改进最坏情况解释”，即根据预期解释可靠性将样本路由到廉价或昂贵的XAI方法；2）“召回高质量解释”，即在预算受限下延迟生成不确定样本的解释。

Result: 在四个表格数据集、五种不同架构和四种XAI方法上，观察到认知不确定性与解释稳定性之间存在强负相关。进一步分析表明，认知不确定性不仅能区分稳定和不稳定的解释，还能区分忠实和不忠实的解释。图像分类实验证实该发现可推广至表格数据之外。

Conclusion: 认知不确定性是一种有效的低成本代理，可用于预测解释的稳定性和忠实度，为在有限预算下高效生成高质量解释提供了可行方案，且具有跨数据模态的泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanation methods are widely used to interpret black-box predictions, but their generation is often computationally expensive and their reliability is not guaranteed. We propose epistemic uncertainty as a low-cost proxy for explanation reliability: high epistemic uncertainty identifies regions where the decision boundary is poorly defined and where explanations become unstable and unfaithful. This insight enables two complementary use cases: `improving worst-case explanations' (routing samples to cheap or expensive XAI methods based on expected explanation reliability), and `recalling high-quality explanations' (deferring explanation generation for uncertain samples under constrained budget). Across four tabular datasets, five diverse architectures, and four XAI methods, we observe a strong negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and explanation stability. Further analysis shows that epistemic uncertainty distinguishes not only stable from unstable explanations, but also faithful from unfaithful ones. Experiments on image classification confirm that our findings generalize beyond tabular data.

</details>


### [57] [ScoringBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Tabular Foundation Models with Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29928)
*Jonas Landsgesell,Pascal Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScoringBench基准测试，通过引入全面概率评分规则解决现有表格基础模型评估仅依赖点估计指标而忽视分布尾部性能的问题，发现模型排名依赖于评分规则选择且无单一预训练目标普适最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格基础模型（如TabPFN和TabICL）已能生成完整预测分布，但主流回归基准测试几乎只使用RMSE和R²等点估计指标评估，这些聚合指标往往掩盖了分布尾部的模型性能，对于金融和临床研究等高风险决策领域（其风险特征常为不对称）构成关键缺陷。

Method: 研究提出ScoringBench开源基准测试，除标准点指标外，还计算全面的proper scoring rules套件，包括CRPS、CRLS、区间评分、能量评分、加权CRPS和Brier评分，以提供概率预测质量的更丰富图景。并评估了使用不同评分规则目标微调的realTabPFNv2.5以及TabICL相对于未调优realTabPFNv2.5的表现。

Result: 结果证实模型排名取决于所选评分规则，不存在单一预训练目标在所有情况下均最优。这表明对于对极端事件敏感的应用，评估指标的选择与数据本身一样，都是特定领域的需求。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了在表格基础模型评估中采用全面概率评分规则的重要性，特别是对于高风险决策场景。ScoringBench通过GitHub维护排行榜，确保透明性、可追溯性、敏捷性和可重复性，为领域提供重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL already produce full predictive distributions yet prevailing regression benchmarks evaluate them almost exclusively via point estimate metrics RMSE R2 These aggregate measures often obscure model performance in the tails of the distribution a critical deficit for high stakes decision making in domains like finance and clinical research where asymmetric risk profiles are the norm We introduce ScoringBench an open benchmark that computes a comprehensive suite of proper scoring rules like CRPS CRLS Interval Score Energy Score weighted CRPS and Brier Score alongside standard point metrics providing a richer picture of probabilistic forecast quality We evaluate realTabPFNv2.5 fine tuned with different scoring rule objectives and TabICL relative to untuned realTabPFNv2.5 across a suite of regression benchmarks Our results confirm that model rankings depend on the chosen scoring rule and that no single pretraining objective is universally optimal This demonstrates that for applications sensitive to extreme events the choice of evaluation metric is as much a domain specific requirement as the data itself ScoringBench is available at https://github.com/jonaslandsgesell/ScoringBench A live preview of the current leaderboard is available at https://scoringbench.bolt.host The leaderboard is maintained via git pull requests to ensure transparency traceability agility and reproducibility

</details>


### [58] [Physiological and Semantic Patterns in Medical Teams Using an Intelligent Tutoring System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29950)
*Xiaoshan Huang,Conrad Borchers,Jiayi Zhang,Susanne P. Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过融合生理信号与对话分析，探究医学双人团队在智能辅导系统中诊断病例时的协作过程。发现生理同步峰值与对话语义转变相关；高生理同步对应较低的语义相似度，涉及探索性语言；成功团队在共同发现时同步，不成功团队在共同不确定时同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的协作学习需要团队通过社会共享学习调节（SSRL）来管理复杂的认知和情感状态。虽然生理同步可以指示这些状态，但其本身难以独立解释，因此需要结合对话等其他模态数据进行深入分析。

Method: 研究调查了四组医学双人团队在智能辅导系统中诊断虚拟病例的过程。方法包括：1）分析对话中的语义转变；2）将对话片段编码以识别SSRL行为，并利用句子嵌入计算余弦相似度；3）关联瞬态生理同步峰值与对话动态；4）通过定性分析对同步峰值进行三角验证。

Result: 结果显示：1）对话语义转变与瞬态生理同步峰值存在相关性；2）在激活先验知识阶段，其语义相似度显著低于简单的任务执行阶段；3）高生理同步与较低的语义相似度相关，表明这些时刻涉及探索性和多样化的语言使用；4）定性分析揭示了“关键时刻”：成功的团队在共同发现时刻出现同步，而不成功的团队则在共同不确定时刻达到同步峰值。

Conclusion: 该研究推动了以人为中心的人工智能发展，展示了如何将生物信号与对话融合，以理解问题解决过程中的关键时刻，为理解协作学习中的复杂状态提供了新的多模态分析视角。

Abstract: Effective collaboration requires teams to manage complex cognitive and emotional states through Socially Shared Regulation of Learning (SSRL). Physiological synchrony (i.e., longitudinal alignment in physiological signals) can indicate these states, but is hard to interpret on its own. We investigate the physiological and conversational dynamics of four medical dyads diagnosing a virtual patient case using an intelligent tutoring system. Semantic shifts in dialogue were correlated with transient physiological synchrony peaks. We also coded utterance segments for SSRL and derived cosine similarity using sentence embeddings. The results showed that activating prior knowledge featured significantly lower semantic similarity than simpler task execution. High physiological synchrony was associated with lower semantic similarity, suggesting that such moments involve exploratory and varied language use. Qualitative analysis triangulated these synchrony peaks as ``pivotal moments'': successful teams synchronized during shared discovery, while unsuccessful teams peaked during shared uncertainty. This research advances human-centered AI by demonstrating how biological signals can be fused with dialogues to understand critical moments in problem solving.

</details>


### [59] [Structured Intent as a Protocol-Like Communication Layer: Cross-Model Robustness, Framework Comparison, and the Weak-Model Compensation Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29953)
*Peng Gang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验和用户研究，验证了基于5W3H的结构化意图表示（PPS）在跨模型、跨语言场景下具有优异的鲁棒性，能显著降低目标对齐的方差，并有效提升人机交互效率与用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究结构化意图表示在跨不同AI模型（Claude、GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro）、跨语言（中英日）及不同提示框架（CO-STAR、RISEN）下的可靠性与鲁棒性，以验证其作为人机交互通信层的实用价值。

Method: 研究设计了包含3,240个模型输出的多维度实验（3种语言 × 6种条件 × 3种模型 × 3个领域 × 20项任务），使用独立评判模型（DeepSeek-V3）进行评估；对比PPS（5W3H框架）与CO-STAR、RISEN及非结构化基线；并开展了50名用户参与的AI辅助意图扩展用户研究。

Result: 结构化提示显著降低了跨语言得分方差（σ从0.470降至约0.020）；发现弱模型补偿效应（Gemini增益+1.006 vs Claude +0.217）；5W3H、CO-STAR和RISEN均达到相似的高目标对齐分数；用户研究中，AI扩展的5W3H提示使交互轮次减少60%，用户满意度从3.16提升至4.04。

Conclusion: 研究支持结构化意图表示作为人机交互中稳健的、协议式的通信层具有重要实用价值；维度分解本身是提升目标对齐的关键要素，且该效益在不同模型和语言间具有泛化性。

Abstract: How reliably can structured intent representations preserve user goals across different AI models, languages, and prompting frameworks? Prior work showed that PPS (Prompt Protocol Specification), a 5W3H-based structured intent framework, improves goal alignment in Chinese and generalizes to English and Japanese. This paper extends that line of inquiry in three directions: cross-model robustness across Claude, GPT-4o, and Gemini 2.5 Pro; controlled comparison with CO-STAR and RISEN; and a user study (N=50) of AI-assisted intent expansion in ecologically valid settings. Across 3,240 model outputs (3 languages x 6 conditions x 3 models x 3 domains x 20 tasks), evaluated by an independent judge (DeepSeek-V3), we find that structured prompting substantially reduces cross-language score variance relative to unstructured baselines. The strongest structured conditions reduce cross-language sigma from 0.470 to about 0.020. We also observe a weak-model compensation pattern: the lowest-baseline model (Gemini) shows a much larger D-A gain (+1.006) than the strongest model (Claude, +0.217). Under the current evaluation resolution, 5W3H, CO-STAR, and RISEN achieve similarly high goal-alignment scores, suggesting that dimensional decomposition itself is an important active ingredient. In the user study, AI-expanded 5W3H prompts reduce interaction rounds by 60 percent and increase user satisfaction from 3.16 to 4.04. These findings support the practical value of structured intent representation as a robust, protocol-like communication layer for human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [60] [Extending MONA in Camera Dropbox: Reproduction, Learned Approval, and Design Implications for Reward-Hacking Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29993)
*Nathan Heath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了MONA方法，构建了模块化的审批机制测试套件，发现校准后的学习型审批者虽能消除奖励破解，但预期行为率显著低于oracle MONA，表明核心挑战转向构建既能保持远见又不引发奖励破解的审批模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MONA原论文提出了一个关键开放问题：审批信号的构建方式（尤其是对结果的依赖程度）如何影响MONA安全保证的有效性。本文旨在通过实证研究回答这一问题，将理论上的审批谱系猜想转化为可运行的实验对象。

Method: 1) 将MONA Camera Dropbox环境重构为标准Python项目并实现脚本化PPO训练；2) 使用公开参考数组复现原始论文结果，对比普通RL（91.5%奖励破解率）与oracle MONA（0.0%破解率）；3) 构建模块化的学习型审批测试套件，涵盖oracle、噪声、误设、学习和校准审批机制；4) 在审批方法、规划视野、数据集规模和校准策略等维度进行消融实验。

Result: 成功复现原论文核心对比结果。在有限预算的实验中，最佳校准学习型审批配置实现了零奖励破解率，但预期行为率仅为11.9%，远低于oracle MONA的99.9%。这一差异源于欠优化而非奖励破解重现，验证了审批谱系猜想。

Conclusion: MONA的安全性保证在多样化审批机制下成立，但存在性能-安全性权衡：学习型审批虽能避免奖励破解，但可能导致次优策略。核心工程挑战已从概念验证转向构建既能保持足够远见又不重开奖励破解通道的审批模型。所有代码、配置和复现命令已公开。

Abstract: Myopic Optimization with Non-myopic Approval (MONA) mitigates multi-step reward hacking by restricting the agent's planning horizon while supplying far-sighted approval as a training signal~\cite{farquhar2025mona}. The original paper identifies a critical open question: how the method of constructing approval -- particularly the degree to which approval depends on achieved outcomes -- affects whether MONA's safety guarantees hold. We present a reproduction-first extension of the public MONA Camera Dropbox environment that (i)~repackages the released codebase as a standard Python project with scripted PPO training, (ii)~confirms the published contrast between ordinary RL (91.5\% reward-hacking rate) and oracle MONA (0.0\% hacking rate) using the released reference arrays, and (iii)~introduces a modular learned-approval suite spanning oracle, noisy, misspecified, learned, and calibrated approval mechanisms. In reduced-budget pilot sweeps across approval methods, horizons, dataset sizes, and calibration strategies, the best calibrated learned-overseer run achieves zero observed reward hacking but substantially lower intended-behavior rates than oracle MONA (11.9\% vs.\ 99.9\%), consistent with under-optimization rather than re-emergent hacking. These results operationalize the MONA paper's approval-spectrum conjecture as a runnable experimental object and suggest that the central engineering challenge shifts from proving MONA's concept to building learned approval models that preserve sufficient foresight without reopening reward-hacking channels. Code, configurations, and reproduction commands are publicly available. https://github.com/codernate92/mona-camera-dropbox-repro

</details>


### [61] [The Triadic Cognitive Architecture: Bounding Autonomous Action via Spatio-Temporal and Epistemic Friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30031)
*Davide Di Gioia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三元认知架构(TCA)，通过将机器推理建立在连续时间物理基础上，引入认知摩擦概念，解决了LLM智能体存在的认知失重问题，在医疗诊断场景中实现了更快的决策和更好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的自主AI智能体处于认知失重状态，缺乏对网络拓扑、时间节奏和认知限制的内在感知。现有启发式智能循环(如ReAct)在交互环境中存在多种失败模式：在拥塞情况下过度使用工具、在时间衰减下长时间沉思、以及在模糊证据下表现脆弱。

Method: 提出三元认知架构(TCA)这一统一的数学框架，通过综合非线性滤波理论、黎曼路由几何和最优控制理论，将智能体的沉思过程映射为耦合随机控制问题。TCA使用受HJB方程启发的停止边界，实例化基于展开的依赖信念的信息价值近似，并采用净效用停止条件，而非依赖任意的启发式停止令牌。

Result: 在模拟的紧急医疗诊断网格(EMDG)中进行实证验证，结果表明：贪婪基线在延迟和拥塞成本下过度沉思，而三元策略在保持诊断准确性的同时，减少了行动时间并提高了患者生存能力。

Conclusion: TCA通过将机器推理建立在连续时间物理基础上并形式化认知摩擦概念，能够有效解决智能体的认知失重问题，在实际应用场景中实现了更优的时间-准确性权衡。

Abstract: Current autonomous AI agents, driven primarily by Large Language Models (LLMs), operate in a state of cognitive weightlessness: they process information without an intrinsic sense of network topology, temporal pacing, or epistemic limits. Consequently, heuristic agentic loops (e.g., ReAct) can exhibit failure modes in interactive environments, including excessive tool use under congestion, prolonged deliberation under time decay, and brittle behavior under ambiguous evidence. In this paper, we propose the Triadic Cognitive Architecture (TCA), a unified mathematical framework that grounds machine reasoning in continuous-time physics. By synthesizing nonlinear filtering theory, Riemannian routing geometry, and optimal control, we formally define the concept of Cognitive Friction. We map the agent's deliberation process to a coupled stochastic control problem where information acquisition is path-dependent and physically constrained. Rather than relying on arbitrary heuristic stop-tokens, the TCA uses an HJB-motivated stopping boundary and instantiates a rollout-based approximation of belief-dependent value-of-information with a net-utility halting condition. Through empirical validation in a simulated Emergency Medical Diagnostic Grid (EMDG), we demonstrate that while greedy baselines over-deliberate under latency and congestion costs, the triadic policy reduces time-to-action while improving patient viability without degrading diagnostic accuracy in this environment.

</details>

### [62] [The impact of multi-agent debate protocols on debate quality: a controlled case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28813)
*Ramtin Zargari Marandi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的多智能体辩论协议RA-CR，在优先考虑共识形成时表现最佳，并揭示了交互与收敛之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体辩论系统性能提升难以归因，因为辩论协议（如智能体数量、轮次、聚合规则）通常固定不变，而模型相关因素在变化，导致难以分离协议效应与模型效应。

Method: 提出三种主要协议进行对比：轮内协议（WR，智能体仅看到当前轮次的贡献）、跨轮协议（CR，完整的先前轮次上下文）、新颖的秩自适应跨轮协议（RA-CR，动态重新排序智能体并通过外部评判模型每轮沉默一个智能体），并与无交互基线（NI，独立响应无同伴可见性）进行比较。在受控的宏观经济案例研究（20个不同事件、五个随机种子、匹配提示/解码）中进行实验。

Result: RA-CR比CR实现更快收敛，WR显示更高的同伴引用率，NI最大化了论证多样性（在主要协议中不受影响）。这些结果揭示了交互（同伴引用率）与收敛（共识形成）之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 协议设计在多智能体辩论系统中具有重要意义。当优先考虑共识时，RA-CR优于其他协议。研究证实了交互与收敛之间存在权衡关系，为多智能体辩论系统的设计提供了指导。

Abstract: In multi-agent debate (MAD) systems, performance gains are often reported; however, because the debate protocol (e.g., number of agents, rounds, and aggregation rule) is typically held fixed while model-related factors vary, it is difficult to disentangle protocol effects from model effects. To isolate these effects, we compare three main protocols, Within-Round (WR; agents see only current-round contributions), Cross-Round (CR; full prior-round context), and novel Rank-Adaptive Cross-Round (RA-CR; dynamically reorders agents and silences one per round via an external judge model), against a No-Interaction baseline (NI; independent responses without peer visibility). In a controlled macroeconomic case study (20 diverse events, five random seeds, matched prompts/decoding), RA-CR achieves faster convergence than CR, WR shows higher peer-referencing, and NI maximizes Argument Diversity (unaffected across the main protocols). These results reveal a trade-off between interaction (peer-referencing rate) and convergence (consensus formation), confirming protocol design matters. When consensus is prioritized, RA-CR outperforms the others.

</details>


### [63] [An Empirical Study of Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29632)
*Yang Shen,Zhenyi Yi,Ziyi Zhao,Lijun Sun,Dongyang Li,Chin-Teng Lin,Yuhui Shi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了单智能体、子智能体架构和智能体团队架构在自动化机器学习优化任务中的表现。发现子智能体模式具有高弹性和高吞吐量，适合严格时间约束下的广度优化；而智能体团队架构虽操作脆弱性较高，但在充足计算预算下能实现深度理论对齐，适合复杂架构重构。研究建议未来自动研究系统应采用动态路由架构以适应任务复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体的发展，社区正从单一LLM转向多智能体系统以突破自动化研究的认知瓶颈，但目前针对自主智能体的最优多智能体协调框架研究尚属空白。

Method: 使用配备Git worktree隔离和显式全局内存的受控执行测试平台，在严格固定的计算时间预算下，对比评估单智能体基线与两种多智能体范式：子智能体架构（并行探索与事后整合）和智能体团队架构（专家协作与预执行交接）。

Result: 揭示了操作稳定性与理论深度之间的基本权衡：子智能体模式在严格时间约束下表现为高弹性、高吞吐量的搜索引擎，适合广泛浅层优化；智能体团队架构因多作者代码生成导致操作脆弱性较高，但在充足计算预算下能实现复杂架构重构所需的深度理论对齐。

Conclusion: 为设计未来自动研究系统提供了可操作指导，主张采用动态路由架构，使其协作结构能够实时适应任务复杂性。

Abstract: As AI agents evolve, the community is rapidly shifting from single Large Language Models (LLMs) to Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) to overcome cognitive bottlenecks in automated research. However, the optimal multi-agent coordination framework for these autonomous agents remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study investigating the comparative efficacy of distinct multi-agent structures for automated machine learning optimization. Utilizing a rigorously controlled, execution-based testbed equipped with Git worktree isolation and explicit global memory, we benchmark a single-agent baseline against two multi-agent paradigms: a subagent architecture (parallel exploration with post-hoc consolidation) and an agent team architecture (experts with pre-execution handoffs). By evaluating these systems under strictly fixed computational time budgets, our findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between operational stability and theoretical deliberation. The subagent mode functions as a highly resilient, high-throughput search engine optimal for broad, shallow optimizations under strict time constraints. Conversely, the agent team topology exhibits higher operational fragility due to multi-author code generation but achieves the deep theoretical alignment necessary for complex architectural refactoring given extended compute budgets. These empirical insights provide actionable guidelines for designing future autoresearch systems, advocating for dynamically routed architectures that adapt their collaborative structures to real-time task complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [64] [A Framework for Hybrid Collective Inference in Distributed Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28778)
*Andrew Nash,Dirk Pesch,Krishnendu Guha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合分布式与分层通信的混合方法，用于物联网和传感器网络中的分布式分类任务，在降低通信开销的同时保持了接近集中式联合推理的分类准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和传感器网络应用（如车辆网络、无人机集群、信息物理系统）中的全局分类任务面临通信和计算资源受限的挑战。现有研究分别关注去中心化分布式数据交换和云/边缘计算范式，但缺乏将两者结合以实现动态运行时通信策略决策的研究。

Method: 提出一种结合分布式与分层通信及分类方法的混合云与分布式架构；推导实施该混合方法的智能体的最优策略，并针对不同的底层数据分布场景进行性能评估。

Result: 该方法能够在理论通信成本降低的情况下，保持高水平的分类准确度，其准确度可与在所有数据上进行集中式联合推理的准确度相当。

Conclusion: 该混合方法具有在现实世界应用中实现高效集体推理的潜力，尤其适用于涉及更复杂底层数据分布的场景。

Abstract: With the ever-increasing range of applications of Internet in Things (IoT) and sensor networks, challenges are emerging in various categories of classification tasks. Applications such as vehicular networking, UAV swarm coordination and cyber-physical systems require global classification over distributed sensors, with tight constraints on communication and computation resources. There has been much research in decentralized and distributed data-exchange for communication-efficient collective inference. Likewise, there has been considerable research involving the use of cloud and edge computing paradigms for efficient task allocation. To the best of our knowledge, there has been no research on the integration of these two concepts to create a hybrid cloud and distributed approach that makes dynamic runtime communication strategy decisions. In this paper, we focus on aspects of combining distributed and hierarchical communication and classification approaches for collective inference. We derive optimal policies for agents that implement this hybrid approach, and evaluate their performance under various scenarios of the distribution of underlying data. Our analysis shows that this approach can maintain a high level of classification accuracy (comparable to that of centralised joint inference over all data), at reduced theoretical communication cost. We expect there is potential for our approach to facilitate efficient collective inference for real-world applications, including instances that involves more complex underlying data distributions.

</details>


### [65] [CRAFT: Cost-aware Expert Replica Allocation with Fine-Grained Layerwise Estimations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28768)
*Adrian Zhao,Zhenkun Cai,Zhenyu Song,Lingfan Yu,Haozheng Fan,Jun Wu,Yida Wang,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRAFT，一个高效的专家复制框架，通过细粒度的逐层复制策略，在给定内存预算下最大化负载均衡，显著提升了大规模MoE模型的服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的专家复制方案在大规模部署中往往存在过度复制问题，许多复制品带来的性能提升微乎其微，却消耗大量GPU内存，导致资源竞争和吞吐量下降。因此需要一种更精细、更高效的复制策略来优化负载均衡。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，基于估计的复制收益执行细粒度的逐层复制策略，在给定内存预算下最大化负载均衡。该框架可以无缝集成到现有服务框架中，无需额外的训练或模型更改。

Result: 在大规模部署中，使用从数千亿到万亿参数不等的模型进行评估，CRAFT相比现有复制技术平均提升端到端服务吞吐量1.14倍（最高达1.2倍）。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过精细化的逐层复制策略有效解决了现有专家复制方案的过度复制问题，在内存约束下实现了更好的负载均衡，显著提升了大规模MoE模型的服务性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has recently emerged as the mainstream architecture for efficiently scaling large language models while maintaining near-constant computational cost. Expert parallelism distributes parameters by partitioning experts across devices, but this introduces token-level load imbalance during inference. Expert replication is a widely adopted load-balancing technique in serving frameworks that alleviates load imbalance in large-scale deployments by replicating experts with high loads. In this work, we demonstrate that existing replication schemes often over-replicate, with many replicas providing marginal improvement. Replicas consume substantial GPU memory, which may lead to resource contention and throughput degradation. We present CRAFT, an efficient expert replication framework that maximizes load balance under a given memory budget by performing fine-grained, per-layer replication based on the estimated replication benefit. CRAFT can be seamlessly integrated into existing serving frameworks without any additional training or model changes. Our evaluation shows that CRAFT increases end-to-end serving throughput by $1.14\times$ on average (up to $1.2\times$) over existing replication techniques in large-scale deployments with models ranging from hundreds of billions to a trillion parameters.

</details>


### [66] [Spark-LLM-Eval: A Distributed Framework for Statistically Rigorous Large Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28769)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Spark-LLM-Eval，一个基于Apache Spark构建的原生分布式大语言模型评估框架，实现了大规模数据集下的线性扩展、统计严谨性评估及成本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估框架在处理数千个样本时表现良好，但当数据集扩展到数十万或数百万样本时（如跨领域评估或全面回归测试）会遇到实际瓶颈，无法满足大规模评估的需求。

Method: 构建了一个基于Apache Spark的原生分布式评估框架，将评估视为数据并行问题，将样本分区到执行器上并聚合结果。引入了严格的统计方法（bootstrap置信区间、配对t检验/McNemar检验/Wilcoxon符号秩检验），并利用Delta Lake支持的内容可寻址响应缓存来解决推理成本问题。

Result: 基准测试表明，该框架随集群规模呈线性扩展，能够有效处理大规模数据集的评估任务。

Conclusion: Spark-LLM-Eval成功解决了大规模LLM评估的可扩展性、统计严谨性和成本问题，框架及所有评估代码已开源。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models at scale remains a practical bottleneck for many organizations. While existing evaluation frameworks work well for thousands of examples, they struggle when datasets grow to hundreds of thousands or millions of samples. This scale is common when assessing model behavior across diverse domains or conducting comprehensive regression testing. We present Spark-LLM-Eval, a distributed evaluation framework built natively on Apache Spark. The system treats evaluation as a data-parallel problem, partitioningexamplesacrossexecutorsandaggregatingresultswithproperstatistical accounting. Beyond raw throughput, we emphasize statistical rigor: every reported metric includes bootstrap confidence intervals, and model comparisons come with appropriate significance tests (paired t-tests, McNemar's test, or Wilcoxon signed-rank, depending on the metric type). The framework also addresses the cost problem inherent in LLM evaluation through content-addressable response caching backed by Delta Lake, which allows iterating on metric definitions without re-running inference. We describe the system architecture, the statistical methodology, and report benchmark results showing linear scaling with cluster size. The framework and all evaluation code are available as open source.

</details>


### [67] [ZEUS: An Efficient GPU Optimization Method Integrating PSO, BFGS, and Automatic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28770)
*Dominik Soos,Marc Paterno,Desh Ranjan,Mohammad Zubair*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ZEUS的高效数值优化方法，通过结合粒子群优化（PSO）和BFGS算法，并利用自动微分和GPU加速，有效解决了高维非凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非凸优化问题通常存在计算复杂度高、易陷入局部最优等挑战。传统的BFGS算法虽然性能优异，但需要用户提供梯度信息，且可能收敛到局部最优。因此，需要一种能够全局搜索且高效计算的方法。

Method: ZEUS方法包含四个关键要素：首先利用粒子群优化（PSO）进行少量迭代以获取潜在的优质起始点集合；然后从这些起始点出发，在GPU上并行运行BFGS算法；通过集成自动微分（AD）技术，自动计算梯度，避免了用户手动求导；最后利用GPU实现大规模并行计算加速。研究还系统性地探索了PSO迭代次数、起始点数量和BFGS迭代深度之间的权衡关系。

Result: 实验研究表明，少量的PSO迭代结合BFGS可以显著改善全局收敛性能。通过在常用测试函数上的性能研究，验证了该方法的有效性。GPU的使用大幅提升了计算速度。研究还揭示了PSO迭代次数、起始点数量和BFGS迭代深度之间的最优权衡策略。

Conclusion: ZEUS方法成功地将全局搜索能力（通过PSO）和局部快速收敛能力（通过BFGS）相结合，通过自动微分和GPU加速技术，实现了高效的高维非凸优化。该方法已开源，为相关领域的数值优化问题提供了一个强有力的工具。

Abstract: We introduce a novel, efficient computational method, ZEUS, for numerical optimization, and provide an open-source implementation. It has four key ingredients: (1) particle swarm optimization (PSO), (2) the use of the Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno (BFGS) method, (3) automatic differentiation (AD), and (4) GPUs. Our approach addresses the computational challenges inherent in high-dimensional, non-convex optimization problems. In the first phase of the algorithm, we get a potentially good set of starting points using PSO. Thereafter, we run BFGS independently in parallel from these starting points. BFGS is one of the best-performing algorithms for numerical optimization. However, it requires the gradient of the function being optimized. ZEUS integrates automatic differentiation into BFGS thus avoiding the need for the user to calculate derivatives explicitly. The use of GPUs allows ZEUS to speed up the calculations substantially. We carry out systematic studies to explore the trade-offs between the number of PSO iterations taken, starting points, and BFGS iteration depth. We show that a handful of iterations of PSO can improve global convergence when combined with BFGS. We also present performance studies using common test functions. The source code can be found at https://github.com/fnal-numerics/global-optimizer-gpu.

</details>


### [68] [Ray Tracing Cores for General-Purpose Computing: A Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28771)
*Enzo Meneses,Cristóbal A. Navarro,Héctor Ferrada,Konstantin Verichev,Cristian Salazar-Concha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于光线追踪核心（RT Cores）在通用计算中应用的系统文献综述，发现最近邻搜索类问题和启发式剪枝问题最能从RT核心中受益，最高可实现200倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探索将光线追踪核心用于非图形学问题，但这些应用缺乏清晰的规律，且RT核心在何种条件下能提供计算优势尚不明确。因此需要系统性地分析RT核心在通用计算中的应用，识别其共同特征、性能增益和局限性。

Method: 采用文献计量学综述和系统文献综述相结合的方法。基于Scopus数据库中索引的59篇研究文章进行文献计量分析，并对其中35篇提出新RT解决方案并与最先进方法进行比较的文章进行系统综述，涵盖32个不同问题。

Result: 分析了RT核心在物理模拟、几何查询、数据库和AI等领域的应用，发现最高可达200倍加速。主要发现包括：(1)最近邻搜索及其变体最能从RT核心受益；(2)依赖启发式方法减少工作量的问题也表现良好；(3)这与RT核心的最大优势一致——遍历树时丢弃分支以避免不必要的工作；(4)多条短光线优于少量长光线。

Conclusion: 本研究结果可作为判断哪些应用更适合利用RT核心进行计算的指导。RT核心在需要树遍历和分支剪枝的问题上具有显著优势，为未来将非图形学问题转化为光线查询形式提供了明确的应用指南。

Abstract: Recent research on ray tracing cores has explored repurposing these cores to solve non-graphical problems by reformulating them as geometric queries, leveraging the inherent parallelism of ray tracing. Although successful in specific cases, these applications lack a clear pattern, and the conditions under which RT cores can provide computational benefits are still not clearly understood. The objective of this literature review is to examine diverse applications of ray tracing cores in general-purpose computation, identifying common features, performance gains, and limitations. By categorizing these efforts, the review aims to provide guidance on the types of problems that can effectively exploit ray tracing hardware beyond traditional rendering tasks. This is achieved with a blibliometric review based on 59 research articles indexed in Scopus, and a systematic literature review on 35 of them which propose new RT solutions and compare them with state-of-the-art methods to solve 32 distinct problems, in some works achieving up to $200\times$ speedup. Most of the problems analyzed in this work have applications in physics simulations and in solving some geometric queries, but problems with potential applications in databases and AI can also be found. Analyzing the characteristics of the problems, it was found that nearest neighbor search, including its variants, benefit the most from ray tracing cores as well as problems that rely on heuristic to diminish the necessary work. This is aligned with the biggest strength of RT cores; discarding tree branches when traversing a tree to avoid unnecessary work. Also, it was found that many short-length rays should be preferred over a few large rays. The results found in this work can serve as a guide for knowing beforehand which applications are better potential candidates to benefit from RT Core computation.

</details>


### [69] [The Computer System Trail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28777)
*Sushant Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本书通过深入研读分布式系统、操作系统和大数据领域的经典论文，为读者提供系统设计的系统性学习路径，帮助读者从表面理解走向深层掌握，提升工程专业能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算领域变化迅速，大多数学习者依赖快速教程或AI生成的摘要，缺乏对系统设计基础的深入理解。作者认为，阅读原始研究论文是掌握该领域的最佳途径，旨在为读者提供一份经典论文的学习指南。

Method: 基于作者在顶尖大学和行业交流会议中多年积累的笔记，本书精选分布式系统、操作系统和大数据领域的奠基性论文，不仅分析这些系统的功能，更深入探讨其设计背后的原因和原理。

Result: 为读者提供了从基础理论到工程实践的系统性学习路径，帮助读者培养技术耐心，能够深入理解系统底层运作机制，而非停留在浅层内容。

Conclusion: 虽然学习过程漫长且具有挑战性，但这种深入研究方式能够显著提升工程师的专业技术水平，是值得投入的学习路径。

Abstract: No matter how much the world of computing changes, system design remains crucial. While most people try to learn it through quick tutorials or AI-generated summaries, there is no better way to master the field than by studying the original research papers. This book serves as a roadmap through those foundational texts, covering seminal papers in distributed systems, operating systems, and big data. It doesn't just look at what these systems do; it digs deep into why they were built that way.
  Built from years of notes taken during discussions at top universities and industry meetups, this guide helps readers understand how systems work under the hood. It is for those who are tired of surface-level content and want to develop the technical patience to wrestle with complex problem-solving. Readers will find the journey long and challenging but highly rewarding, as it enables them to elevate their engineering craft to a truly professional level.

</details>


### [70] [Byzantine-Robust and Communication-Efficient Distributed Training: Compressive and Cyclic Gradient Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28780)
*Chengxi Li,Youssef Allouah,Rachid Guerraoui,Mikael Skoglund,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LAD和Com-LAD方法，通过循环梯度编码技术解决数据异构性下拜占庭攻击的分布式训练问题，显著降低了求解误差并提高了通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式训练方法在面对拜占庭攻击时，由于不同设备间数据异构性导致本地梯度差异较大，使得现有鲁棒聚合规则的求解误差无法有效降低，存在关键局限性。

Method: 提出循环梯度编码的分布式训练方法（LAD）：服务器在训练前将完整数据集分配给设备，每次迭代使用循环梯度编码冗余分配计算任务；诚实设备计算固定数量数据子集的本地梯度并进行编码传输；服务器使用鲁棒聚合规则聚合编码向量。进一步提出压缩变体Com-LAD以减少通信开销。

Result: 理论分析表明，利用设备间计算冗余性，LAD方法在拜占庭攻击下具有改进的收敛性能和显著更低的求解误差；Com-LAD在通信受限环境下进一步降低了通信开销。数值实验验证了所提方法在增强拜占庭鲁棒性和通信效率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入循环梯度编码机制，成功克服了数据异构性下现有方法的局限性，实现了拜占庭攻击鲁棒性与通信效率的双重提升，为通信受限环境下的安全分布式训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of distributed training (DT) under Byzantine attacks with communication constraints. While prior work has developed various robust aggregation rules at the server to enhance robustness to Byzantine attacks, the existing methods suffer from a critical limitation in that the solution error does not diminish when the local gradients sent by different devices vary considerably, as a result of data heterogeneity among the subsets held by different devices. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel DT method, cyclic gradient coding-based DT (LAD). In LAD, the server allocates the entire training dataset to the devices before training begins. In each iteration, it assigns computational tasks redundantly to the devices using cyclic gradient coding. Each honest device then computes local gradients on a fixed number of data subsets and encodes the local gradients before transmitting to the server. The server aggregates the coded vectors from the honest devices and the potentially incorrect messages from Byzantine devices using a robust aggregation rule. Leveraging the redundancy of computation across devices, the convergence performance of LAD is analytically characterized, demonstrating improved robustness against Byzantine attacks and significantly lower solution error. Furthermore, we extend LAD to a communication-efficient variant, compressive and cyclic gradient coding-based DT (Com-LAD), which further reduces communication overhead under constrained settings. Numerical results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods in enhancing both Byzantine resilience and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [71] [When GPUs Fail Quietly: Observability-Aware Early Warning Beyond Numeric Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28781)
*Michael Bidollahkhani,Freja Nordsiek,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对HPC和AI工作负载中GPU节点的“分离类故障”，提出了一种可观测性感知的早期预警框架，通过联合建模GPU遥测热漂移与监控管道退化指标，实现了比仅依赖GPU数据检测更长的故障预警提前量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU节点是现代HPC和AI工作负载的核心，但许多故障并非表现为立即的硬故障。其中一类重要的“分离类故障”发生突兀，几乎没有数值前兆，主要表现为GPU在驱动或互连层面不可用，以及监控数据完整性下降等结构性信号，现有检测方法难以应对。

Method: 提出一种可观测性感知的早期预警框架，联合建模两个方面的信号：(i) GPU遥测中感知利用率的热漂移特征；(ii) 监控管道退化指标，包括抓取延迟增加、样本丢失、时间序列空白和设备指标消失。研究利用GWDG生产环境的遥测数据，关联GPU、节点、监控和调度器信号进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，分离类故障几乎不存在数值前兆，主要通过结构性遥测崩溃被观测到。与仅使用GPU数据的检测方法相比，联合建模方法显著增加了早期预警的提前时间。相关数据集已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了分离类故障的本质特征，证明了联合建模GPU遥测与监控管道信号的有效性，为GPU节点故障预警提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: GPU nodes are central to modern HPC and AI workloads, yet many failures do not manifest as immediate hard faults. While some instabilities emerge gradually as weak thermal or efficiency drift, a significant class occurs abruptly with little or no numeric precursor. In these detachment-class failures, GPUs become unavailable at the driver or interconnect level and the dominant observable signal is structural, including disappearance of device metrics and degradation of monitoring payload integrity.
  This paper proposes an observability-aware early-warning framework that jointly models (i) utilization-aware thermal drift signatures in GPU telemetry and (ii) monitoring-pipeline degradation indicators such as scrape latency increase, sample loss, time-series gaps, and device-metric disappearance. The framework is evaluated on production telemetry from GPU nodes at GWDG, where GPU, node, monitoring, and scheduler signals can be correlated.
  Results show that detachment failures exhibit minimal numeric precursor and are primarily observable through structural telemetry collapse, while joint modeling increases early-warning lead time compared to GPU-only detection. The dataset used in this study is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19052367.

</details>


### [72] [Legible Consensus: Topology-Aware Quorum Geometry for Asymmetric Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28788)
*Tony Mason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将crumbling-wall仲裁构造映射到物理分层网络，分离跨层义务与层内复制两个关注点，使协议故障模式清晰可读，并在星际网络拓扑中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扁平仲裁设计将所有节点视为可互换，无法区分层级不可达与副本丢失，导致在共识失败时无法确定具体故障原因。在非对称拓扑中，跨层安全义务与层内复制策略混在一起，缺乏可观测性。

Method: 将crumbling-wall仲裁构造映射到物理分层网络（地球/LEO/月球/火星10节点拓扑），分离跨层义务与层内复制关注点；使用TLA+穷举验证仲裁交集；比较稀疏与全覆盖拓扑以分离仲裁义务与网络可达性约束。
