Result: 在火星合相黑障期间，四个层级中有三个保持全局活跃性，仅断连层级丧失；各层共识延迟等于到地球的光速往返时间（地球183ms、LEO 131ms、月球5.1s）；wall结构在Multi-Paxos选举中施加了对称网格仲裁无法表达的领导成本梯度。

Conclusion: 该方法使操作员能仅从wall结构和连通状态确定哪些层级保持全局共识能力，无需运行时探测；设计层面结果证明了分层仲裁在非对称拓扑中的优势，为星际网络等场景提供了可观测、可预测的共识机制。

Abstract: Quorum design over asymmetric topologies conflates two independent concerns: inter-tier obligation (which tiers must participate for cross-tier safety) and intra-tier replication (how each tier survives local failures). Flat quorums treat all nodes as interchangeable; when consensus fails, the structure does not reveal whether a tier was unreachable or a tier lost too many replicas. We show that mapping a crumbling-wall quorum construction to a physically tiered network separates these concerns and makes the protocol's failure modes legible: an operator can determine which tiers retain global consensus capability from the wall structure and connectivity state alone, without runtime probing. Using a 10-node Earth/LEO/Moon/Mars topology as a magnifying glass, we confirm that three of four tiers retain global liveness during Mars conjunction blackout; only the disconnected tier loses it. Consensus latency at each tier equals the speed-of-light round-trip to Earth: 183~ms (Earth), 131~ms (LEO), 5.1~s (Moon). The wall also imposes a leadership cost gradient on Multi-Paxos elections that symmetric grid quorums cannot express. A comparison between sparse and full-coverage topologies separates wall obligations from network reachability as independent liveness constraints. All results are design-level; quorum intersection is verified exhaustively in TLA+.

</details>


### [73] [1.5 Million Messages Per Second on 3 Machines: Benchmarking and Latency Optimization of Apache Pulsar at Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29113)
*Muhamed Ramees Cheriya Mukkolakkal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为Apache Pulsar实践者提供了两项独立贡献：在仅三个裸机K8s节点上验证了149.9万msg/s吞吐量与3.88ms中位发布延迟，并记录了从213ms GC尖峰优化至3.88ms的完整过程。硬件选择（NVMe日志盘与25Gbps网络）是吞吐量上限的主要决定因素，同时发现了一个未记录的Linux内核页缓存回写交互问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生产环境中Apache Pulsar面临的严重延迟问题（213ms GC尖峰、13-18ms中位发布延迟），提升系统吞吐量与延迟表现，并为Pulsar运维人员提供硬件选型指导与性能优化路径。

Method: 通过Java Flight Recorder进行根因分析，识别并解决三个独立根因：1）从G1GC切换至ZGC Generational消除GC停顿；2）使用专用NVMe日志盘将fdatasync延迟从5.1ms降至0.02ms；3）发现BookKeeper ForceWriteThread中Linux内核页缓存回写交互导致fdatasync劣化的问题。在三个裸机Kubernetes节点上部署Pulsar 4.0.8与Java 21进行验证。

Result: 实现149.9万msg/s吞吐量与3.88ms中位发布延迟，相比优化前达成4.7倍延迟改进与50倍吞吐量提升；硬件选型（专用NVMe日志盘达0.02ms fdatasync、25Gbps网卡）是吞吐量天花板的决定因素；预测在15节点配置下可达1500万msg/s。

Conclusion: 硬件选型（而非计算或软件调优）是决定Pulsar吞吐量上限的主要因素；发现了BookKeeper中一个未记录的Linux内核页缓存回写交互问题，该问题会导致fdatasync延迟从<1ms劣化至15-22ms，对所有Pulsar运维人员具有参考价值。

Abstract: This paper presents two independent contributions for Apache Pulsar practitioners. First, we validate 1,499,947 msg/s at 3.88 ms median publish latency on just three bare-metal Kubernetes nodes running Pulsar 4.0.8 with Java 21 and ZGC Generational garbage collection, and project a hardware-driven path to 15 million msg/s on 15 machines using five independent clusters with key-based partition routing. Hardware selection -- specifically dedicated NVMe journals achieving 0.02 ms fdatasync and 25 Gbps network interfaces -- is the primary determinant of throughput ceiling, not compute or software tuning. Second, we trace the complete latency optimization journey from 213 ms GC spikes and 13-18 ms median publish latency in production to 3.88 ms through Java Flight Recorder guided root cause analysis. Three independent root causes are identified and resolved: G1GC pauses eliminated by switching to ZGC Generational; journal fdatasync latency reduced from 5.1 ms to 0.02 ms through NVMe journal dedication; and a previously undocumented Linux kernel page cache writeback interaction inside BookKeeper's ForceWriteThread that degrades fdatasync from under 1 ms to 15-22 ms even across physically separate NVMe drives sharing the kernel block layer. This finding is undocumented in official Apache Pulsar and BookKeeper documentation and is relevant to all Pulsar operators experiencing unexplained P99.9 latency spikes. The combined optimizations achieve a 4.7x latency improvement at 50x higher throughput.

</details>


### [74] [Mitigating Temporal Blindness in Kubernetes Autoscaling: An Attention-Double-LSTM Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28790)
*Faraz Shaikh,Gianluca Reali,Mauro Femminella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力增强的双层LSTM与PPO融合的无服务器自动扩缩容框架，有效解决了边缘计算环境下的时间盲目性问题，显著降低了延迟和副本震荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算环境中无服务器负载具有随机性和突发性。传统的反应式控制器（如Kubernetes HPA）存在固有延迟，导致流量高峰期违反SLO以及资源缩减期的抖动。尽管深度强化学习（DRL）能实现主动管理，但标准智能体存在“时间盲目性”，无法有效捕捉非马尔可夫边缘环境中的长期依赖关系。

Method: 提出了一种稳定感知的自动扩缩容框架，将负载预测与控制相结合。该框架采用注意力增强的双层堆叠LSTM架构，并将其集成到近端策略优化（PPO）智能体中。通过深度时间注意力机制选择性地对历史状态进行加权，在过滤高频噪声的同时保留需求变化的关键前兆。

Result: 在异构集群上使用真实Azure Functions追踪数据进行验证。与行业标准HPA、无状态Double DQN和单层LSTM消融实验相比，该方法相对于单层LSTM基线，将第90百分位延迟降低了约29%，同时将副本震荡减少了39%。

Conclusion: 通过深度注意力记忆缓解时间盲目性，是在生产边缘环境中实现可靠、低抖动自动扩缩容的先决条件。

Abstract: In the emerging landscape of edge computing, the stochastic and bursty nature of serverless workloads presents a critical challenge for autonomous resource orchestration. Traditional reactive controllers, such as the Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaler (HPA), suffer from inherent reaction latency, leading to Service Level Objective (SLO) violations during traffic spikes and resource flapping during ramp-downs. While Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a pathway toward proactive management, standard agents suffer from temporal blindness, an inability to effectively capture long-term dependencies in non-Markovian edge environments. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel stability-aware autoscaling framework unifying workload forecasting and control via an Attention-Enhanced Double-Stacked LSTM architecture integrated within a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent. Unlike shallow recurrent models, our approach employs a deep temporal attention mechanism to selectively weight historical states, effectively filtering high-frequency noise while retaining critical precursors of demand shifts. We validate the framework on a heterogeneous cluster using real-world Azure Functions traces. Comparative analysis against industry-standard HPA, stateless Double DQN, and a single-layer LSTM ablation demonstrates that our approach reduces 90th percentile latency by approximately 29% while simultaneously decreasing replica churn by 39%, relative to the single-layer LSTM baseline. These results confirm that mitigating temporal blindness through deep attentive memory is a prerequisite for reliable, low-jitter autoscaling in production edge environments.

</details>


### [75] [Parallel Gauss-Jordan Elimination and System Reduction for Efficient Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28792)
*Filip Noveski,Elena Hadzieva*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种加速电路仿真的方法：并行高斯-约当消元算法和部分系统缩减方法。实验表明，这两种方法单独使用均可提升性能，结合使用时在中等规模矩阵上能保持并行效率，在最大规模矩阵上甚至能提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路仿真中，基于求解线性方程组的迭代仿真模型在各个时间点的计算需要高效的求解方法。为了加速此类电路仿真过程，提高计算效率，需要开发新的算法和技术。

Method: 提出了两种独立的新型加速方法：(1) 并行高斯-约当消元(GJE)算法，采用了一种已知的并行策略但首次应用于此场景；(2) 部分系统缩减方法，在迭代开始前对系统进行缩减，这是一种全新的系统缩减方法。算法使用C++和OpenMP实现，在各种输入矩阵上进行测试。

Result: 对各个方法的分析显示性能均有提升。两种方法的组合使用在中等规模矩阵上能在部分缩减后保持并行效率，在测试机器上的最大规模矩阵上甚至能提高效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行GJE算法和部分系统缩减方法均能有效加速电路仿真，两种方法的结合使用在保持或提升并行效率方面表现出色，为电路仿真提供了有效的加速解决方案。

Abstract: For the purposes of electric circuit simulation, we consider an iterative simulation model based on solving systems of linear equations by Gauss-Jordan elimination (GJE) for individual moments in time. To accelerate the simulation, we propose two independent novel approaches: a parallel GJE algorithm and partial system reduction prior to the start of iterations. The former is based on a well-known strategy applied for the first time in this context, whereas the latter, to the best of our knowledge, proposes an entirely new system reduction approach. To evaluate performance, we implement these algorithms in C++ using OpenMP and run them on various input matrices. Our analyses of the individual methods show improved performance, whilst combining them maintains parallel efficiency after partial reduction on medium-sized matrices and even improves efficiency on the largest matrices on the tested machine.

</details>


### [76] [Understand and Accelerate Memory Processing Pipeline for Disaggregated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29002)
*Zifan He,Rui Ma,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种GPU-FPGA异构系统来加速大语言模型（LLM）的内存处理流程。通过将稀疏、不规则和内存受限的操作卸载到FPGA，而将计算密集型操作保留在GPU上，该系统在多种LLM推理优化中实现了比纯GPU基线快1.04~2.2倍的性能，并节省了1.11~4.7倍的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型（LLM）严重依赖高效的长上下文处理与生成机制（如稀疏注意力、RAG、压缩上下文内存）。系统分析表明，这些优化手段引入了22%-97%的内存处理开销，且其计算特征具有显著的异质性（包含稀疏、不规则及内存受限操作），现有的同构硬件系统难以高效处理这种多样化的负载。

Method: 首先将现有的LLM长上下文优化统一抽象为四步内存处理流水线（准备内存、计算相关性、检索、应用于推理）。基于此，提出利用GPU-FPGA异构系统进行加速：将计算密集型操作保留在GPU上执行，而将具有稀疏、不规则及内存受限特征的内存处理操作卸载至FPGA上执行。实验平台基于AMD MI210 GPU和Alveo U55C FPGA。

Result: 在多种LLM推理优化场景下评估显示，该异构系统相比纯GPU基线实现了1.04~2.2倍的加速，同时能耗降低1.11~4.7倍（在NVIDIA A100上亦得到类似结果）。

Conclusion: 异构系统是加速LLM内存处理及端到端推理的有效且实用的方向，该研究为未来面向LLM的异构硬件设计提供了重要的参考依据。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) increasingly depends on efficient long-context processing and generation mechanisms, including sparse attention, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and compressed contextual memory, to support complex reasoning. We show that these optimizations can be unified into a four-step memory processing pipeline: Prepare Memory, Compute Relevancy, Retrieval, and Apply to Inference. Through systematic profiling, we identify a 22%-97% memory processing overhead in LLM inference and strong heterogeneity in its computational characteristics. Motivated by this insight, we argue that \textbf{heterogeneous systems} are well-suited to accelerate memory processing and thus end-to-end inference. We demonstrate this approach on a GPU-FPGA system by offloading sparse, irregular, and memory-bounded operations to FPGAs while retaining compute-intensive operations on GPUs. Evaluated on an AMD MI210 GPU and an Alveo U55C FPGA, our system is $1.04\sim2.2\times$ faster and requires $1.11\sim4.7\times$ less energy across multiple LLM inference optimizations than the GPU baseline (similar results hold on NVIDIA A100). These results establish heterogeneous systems as a practical direction for efficient LLM memory processing and inform future heterogeneous hardware design.

</details>


### [77] [Building the Palmetto API: Adding granular permissions and caching to the Slurm REST API without sacrificing compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29032)
*Ben Godfrey,Doug Dawson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Palmetto API，这是一个针对Slurm原生REST接口（slurmrestd）的轻量级安全代理，通过高级认证、授权和缓存功能实现了向工具和自动化安全暴露集群调度程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发管理和计算研究工具需要可靠的与集群调度程序进行交互的编程接口，但直接使用原生接口存在安全性等方面的不足。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为Palmetto API的代理层，它位于Slurm原生REST接口（slurmrestd）之上，提供高级认证、授权机制以及缓存功能。

Result: 该代理实现了轻量级且安全的方法来暴露集群调度程序；通过评估，缓存机制带来了性能提升，并验证了与现有slurmrestd客户端的兼容性。

Conclusion: Palmetto API成功提供了一种安全、高效且兼容的解决方案，使得研究工具和自动化流程能够可靠地与集群调度程序交互。

Abstract: The development of administrative and computational research tools requires reliable programmatic interfaces with the cluster scheduler. The Research Computing and Data (RCD) team at Clemson University has developed the Palmetto API, a proxy for the native Slurm RESTful interface, slurmrestd, while providing advanced authentication, authorization, and caching. This paper details the design and implementation of this proxy, evaluates the performance benefits from caching, and verifies compatibility to existing slurmrestd clients. The result is a light-weight and secure method of exposing our cluster scheduler to tools and automations.

</details>


### [78] [Exploration of Energy and Throughput Tradeoffs for Dataflow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29367)
*Abrarul Karim,Joachim Falk,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据流网络中动态功耗管理策略导致的吞吐量下降问题，提出了线性规划（LP）、混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型以及一种名为“Hop and Skip”的多目标设计空间探索策略，在保证吞吐量的前提下实现了能耗与吞吐量的有效权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据流网络中引入时钟门控和电源门控等动态功耗管理策略虽能在空闲时段显著节能，但也会因关断和唤醒延迟导致吞吐量下降，这对有严格吞吐量要求的信号处理系统尤为不利。因此，需要在节能与吞吐量之间寻求最优权衡。

Method: 首先，提出了一个线性规划（LP）模型，用于求解所谓的“自供电”数据流网络的最大吞吐量周期调度；其次，提出了一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，在满足给定吞吐量约束的前提下，通过确定允许空闲掉电的执行单元集合，最小化周期能耗；最后，提出了一种名为“Hop and Skip”的多目标设计空间探索策略，高效遍历能耗与吞吐量的帕累托前沿。

Result: 在现有基准测试和随机生成图上的实验评估表明，所提探索策略相较于暴力搜索能显著减少探索时间。真实案例研究展示了三种配置下的能耗与吞吐量表现：（a）所有执行单元始终保持活跃；（b）所有执行单元均为自供电模式；（c）由设计空间探索策略找到的最优权衡点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数学规划模型与多目标探索策略成功解决了数据流网络中动态功耗管理与吞吐量保证之间的矛盾，实验证明该方法能有效发现能耗与吞吐量的最佳权衡解，为信号处理系统等对吞吐量有严格要求的应用提供了可行的节能优化方案。

Abstract: The introduction of dynamic power management strategies such as clock gating and power gating in dataflow networks has been shown to provide significant energy savings when applied during idle times. However, these strategies can also degrade throughput due to shutdown and wake-up delays. Such throughput degradations might be particularly detrimental to signal processing systems that require a guaranteed throughput. As a solution, this paper first contributes a linear-program formulation for finding a periodic maximal-throughput schedule of a given so-called self-powering dataflow network where actors, realized in hardware, are allowed to go to sleep whenever not being enabled to fire. Depending on which actors are allowed to power down, tradeoffs between throughput and energy savings can be obtained. As a second contribution, we propose a mixed-integer-linear-program formulation to determine a periodic schedule that satisfies a given throughput while minimizing the overall energy per period by identifying a respective set of actors that is allowed to power down in phases of idleness and which rather not. Finally, as a third contribution, we propose a multi-objective design-space exploration strategy called "Hop and Skip" to efficiently explore the Pareto front of energy and throughput solutions. Experimental evaluations on a set of existing benchmarks and randomly generated graphs witness significant exploration time reductions over a brute-force sweep. Finally, a real-world case study is elaborated, and we report on achievable energy savings and throughputs of the related dataflow network where (a) all actors are always-active, (b) all actors are self-powered, and (c) all optimal energy and throughput tradeoff points as found by the proposed design-space exploration strategy.

</details>


### [79] [Polynomial Time Local Decision Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29477)
*Laurent Feuilloley,Soumyadeep Paul,Ami Paz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式决策任务的三个分类系统，发现多项式局部计算与多项式图规模计算在无证书情形下等价，而在有证书情形下，存在性证书类的计算能力随运行时间界限严格递增，普遍性证书类则出现反直觉的不可比较现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分布式决策任务分类系统中不同计算模型与证书类型之间的关系，澄清三类定义（无界计算、多项式图规模计算、多项式局部邻域计算）之间的相互包含与分离关系。

Method: 通过理论证明建立模型间的等价性、包含关系与分离结果：首先证明无证书情形下两种多项式定义的等价性，然后针对存在性证书和普遍性证书分别证明包含/分离/不可比较关系，最终构建完整的类间关系格结构。

Result: 无证书时，两种多项式定义等价；存在性证书类随运行时间界限增大严格扩张（多项式邻域⊂多项式图规模⊂无界）；普遍性证书类出现不可比较现象——多项式图规模界限有时能判定无界证书也无法判定的语言。

Conclusion: 三种分类系统基础类之间形成复杂且严密的包含/分离格结构，挑战了"更大运行时间界限总带来更强计算能力"的直觉认知。

Abstract: We consider three classification systems for distributed decision tasks: With unbounded computation and certificates, defined by Balliu, D'Angelo, Fraigniaud, and Olivetti [JCSS'18], and with (two flavors of) polynomially bounded local computation and certificates, defined in recent works by Aldema Tshuva and Oshman [OPODIS'23], and by Reiter [PODC'24]. The latter two differ in the way they evaluate the polynomial bounds: the former considers polynomials with respect to the size of the graph, while the latter refers to being polynomial in the size of each node's local neighborhood.
  We start by revisiting decision without certificates. For this scenario, we show that the latter two definitions coincide: roughly, a node cannot know the graph size, and thus can only use a running time dependent on its neighborhood.
  We then consider decision with certificates. With existential certificates ($Σ_1$-type classes), a larger running time defines strictly larger classes of languages: when it grows from being polynomial in each node's view, through polynomial in the graph's size, and to unbounded, the derived classes strictly contain each other. With universal certificates ($Π_1$-type classes), on the other hand, we prove a surprising incomparability result: having running time bounded by the graph size sometimes allows us to decide languages undecidable even with unbounded certificates.
  We complement these results with other containment and separation results, which together portray a surprisingly complex lattice of strict containment relations between the classes at the base of the three classification systems.

</details>


### [80] [M3SA: Exploring Datacenter Performance and Climate-Impact with Multi- and Meta-Model Simulation and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29778)
*Radu Nicolae,Dante Niewenhuis,Sacheendra Talluri,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了M3SA，一个多模型数据中心仿真分析框架，通过离散事件仿真结合多个独立模型的预测，解决了现有单一模型仿真器在专业化和适应性方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心消耗大量电力且需求持续增长，现有仿真器缺乏结合多个独立模型的能力，导致单一模型存在专业化局限且难以适应实际运行现象，而其他关键社会基础设施领域（如水利和公共交通）已采用多模型方法。

Method: 设计了多模型并发仿真架构（Multi-Model）、将多模型结果整合为元模型（Meta-Model）的技术，以及量化元模型准确性的流程。采用离散事件仿真预测每个模型在各种现实数据中心条件下对气候和性能的影响，并整合这些预测结果。

Result: 实验表明：(i) M3SA能够复现并增强同行评审实验；(ii) 能预测运行根本不同工作负载痕迹的数据中心运行现象（如故障）；(iii) 支持多种假设分析和配置分析，如基于年度能源生产模式配置CO2感知迁移。

Conclusion: M3SA已集成到开源仿真器OpenDC中，为提升数据中心可持续性和性能提供了更全面准确的仿真决策工具，填补了数据中心仿真领域多模型方法的空白。

Abstract: Datacenters are vital to our digital society, but consume a considerable fraction of global electricity and demand is projected to increase. To improve their sustainability and performance, we envision that simulators will become primary decision-making tools. However, and unlike other fields focusing on key societal infrastructure such as waterworks and mass transit, datacenter simulators do not yet combine multiple independent models into their operation and thus suffer from issues associated with singular models, such as specialization, and lack of adaptability to operational phenomena. To address this challenge, we propose M3SA, a datacenter simulation and analysis framework that uses discrete-event simulation to predict, for each model, the impact on climate and performance under various realistic datacenter conditions, and then combines these predictions. We design an architecture for simulating multiple concurrent models (Multi-Model), a technique for integrating the results of multiple models into a Meta-Model, and a procedure for quantifying Meta-Model accuracy. Through experiments with an M3SA prototype, we show that (i) M3SA can reproduce and enhance peer-reviewed experiments; (ii) M3SA can predict operational phenomena (e.g., failures) of datacenters, running fundamentally different workload traces; (iii) M3SA enables various types of what-if and how-to analysis, such as how to configure CO2-aware migration over yearly energy-production patterns. M3SA has been integrated into the open-source simulator OpenDC and is available at: https://github.com/atlarge-research/opendc-m3sa.

</details>


### [81] [Mathematical Foundations of Modeling ETL Process Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29877)
*Levin Maier,Lucas Schulze,Robert Lilow,Lukas Hahn,Nikola Krasowski,Arnulf Barth,Sebastian Gaebel,Ferdi Güran,Oliver Hanau,Giovanni Wagner,Falk Borgmann,Oleg Arenz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于受控离散时间马尔可夫过程的有向无环图模型，用于准确模拟ETL过程链的时间聚合吞吐量，为资源分配提供仿真基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据处理基础设施中，ETL过程链的吞吐量与资源分配（并行线程数）及随机处理时间波动之间存在复杂关系，且链式结构中可能出现的瓶颈会影响后续所有过程，缺乏准确的数学模型进行描述和仿真。

Method: 将ETL过程链表示为有向无环图上的受控离散时间马尔可夫过程，建模平均吞吐量为并行线程数的有界单调函数（反映边际效益递减），提出流量平衡假设建立线程数、平均吞吐量和平均处理时间的关系，并使用非负重尾分布对随机处理时间进行建模。

Result: 成功构建了一个在时间聚合吞吐量层面具有准确性的ETL过程链数学模型，该模型适合高效仿真，为ETL网络提供了原理性的仿真器。

Conclusion: 该框架为ETL网络的仿真提供了坚实的数学基础，可作为基于学习和控制的资源分配策略的底层支撑。

Abstract: Extract-Transform-Load (ETL) processes are core components of modern data processing infrastructures. The throughput of processed data records can be adjusted by changing the amount of allocated resources, i.e.~the number of parallel processing threads for each of the three ETL phases, but also depends on stochastic variations in the per-record processing times. In chains of multiple consecutive ETL processes, the relation between allocated resources and overall throughput is further complicated, for example by the occurrence of bottlenecks affecting all subsequent ETL processes. We develop a mathematical model of ETL process chains that is accurate at the level of time-aggregated throughput and suitable for efficient simulation. The process chain is represented as a controlled discrete-time Markov process on a directed acyclic graph whose edges are individual ETL processes. We model the mean throughput as a bounded, monotone function of the number of parallel threads, to capture the diminishing benefit of allocating more threads. We furthermore introduce a Flow Balance postulate linking number of threads, mean throughput, and mean processing time. The stochastic processing times are then modeled by non-negative heavy-tailed distributions around the mean processing time. This framework provides a principled simulator for ETL networks and a foundation for learning- and control-based resource allocation.

</details>


### [82] [Scalable AI-assisted Workflow Management for Detector Design Optimization Using Distributed Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30014)
*Derek Anderson,Amit Bashyal,Markus Diefenthaler,Cristiano Fanelli,Wen Guan,Tanja Horn,Alex Jentsch Meifeng Lin,Tadashi Maeno,Kei Nagai,Hemalata Nayak,Connor Pecar,Karthik Suresh,Fang-Ying Tsai,Anselm Vossen,Tianle Wang,Torre Wenaus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于PanDA-iDDS系统的AI辅助探测器设计优化框架，通过多目标贝叶斯优化实现了高效、自动化的高维参数空间探索，并在EIC探测器设计中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代探测器设计面临高维参数空间探索的挑战，需要一种能够协调大规模异构计算资源、自动化迭代仿真的智能优化框架，以提高设计效率和自动化水平。

Method: 开发了一个AI辅助框架，将多目标贝叶斯优化算法与PanDA-iDDS工作流引擎集成。PanDA系统负责在分布式异构计算资源上编排大规模工作流，iDDS组件提供智能调度，两者协同实现迭代仿真的自动化协调。

Result: 通过基准测试问题和对EIC的ePIC和dRICH探测器的实际研究验证了框架的有效性。结果表明该框架在多目标优化中实现了更高的自动化程度、可扩展性和效率。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个灵活且可扩展的AI驱动探测器设计范式，不仅适用于粒子物理探测器设计，还可推广至其他计算密集型科学应用领域，为大规模科学计算的智能化提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Production and Distributed Analysis (PanDA) system, originally developed for the ATLAS experiment at the CERN Large Hadron Collider (LHC), has evolved into a robust platform for orchestrating large-scale workflows across distributed computing resources. Coupled with its intelligent Distributed Dispatch and Scheduling (iDDS) component, PanDA supports AI/ML-driven workflows through a scalable and flexible workflow engine.
  We present an AI-assisted framework for detector design optimization that integrates multi-objective Bayesian optimization with the PanDA--iDDS workflow engine to coordinate iterative simulations across heterogeneous resources. The framework addresses the challenge of exploring high-dimensional parameter spaces inherent in modern detector design.
  We demonstrate the framework using benchmark problems and realistic studies of the ePIC and dRICH detectors for the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). Results show improved automation, scalability, and efficiency in multi-objective optimization. This work establishes a flexible and extensible paradigm for AI-driven detector design and other computationally intensive scientific applications.

</details>


### [83] [A Lightweight Hybrid Publish/Subscribe Event Fabric for IPC and Modular Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30030)
*Dimitris Gkoulis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CNS，一种轻量级、本地优先的混合事件架构，通过结合进程内发布/订阅和NATS支持的分布式上下文，解决了模块化软件中消息传递路径复杂的问题，实现了高效的异步消息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受控分布式环境中部署于小型计算单元的模块化软件，通常需要低开销的进程内协调和选择性的跨节点分发两条消息传递路径。然而在实践中，事件标识、序列化和传输桥接经常被实现为临时的粘合代码，这导致进程间通信（IPC）、结构化路由和关闭行为变得复杂。

Method: 设计了CNS轻量级混合事件架构，核心组件包括：类型化事件键、按系列划分的序列化与验证机制、用于进程内协调的本地发布/订阅上下文，以及用于节点间分发的NATS分布式上下文。通过桥接运行时在两个上下文之间移动事件，同时保持通用的路由词汇表。主要操作模式为即发即弃的发布/订阅，同时也支持双向请求-应答作为次要扩展。

Result: 基于Python原型和单机测试，本地传递平均延迟约30微秒；纯分布式传递平均延迟1.26-1.37毫秒；混合桥接平均延迟1.64-1.89毫秒。验证机制引入的开销相对于序列化选择而言较为适中。

Conclusion: CNS适用于模块化服务内部及受控节点有限集合之间的结构化IPC和实际消息移动，为解决模块化软件中的消息传递复杂性提供了有效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Modular software deployed on mini compute units in controlled distributed environments often needs two messaging paths: low-overhead in-process coordination and selective cross-node distribution. In practice, event identity, serialization, and transport bridging are frequently implemented as ad hoc glue, which complicates inter-process communication (IPC), structured routing, and shutdown behavior. This paper presents CNS, a lightweight local-first hybrid event fabric centered on asynchronous fire-and-forget messaging. CNS combines a typed event key, per-family serialization and validation, a local publish/subscribe context for in-process coordination, and a NATS-backed distributed context for inter-node distribution. A bridge runtime moves events between the two contexts while preserving a common routing vocabulary. The primary operating model is fire-and-forget publication and subscription; bidirectional request-reply remains available as a secondary extension on the same subject space. A Python prototype and single-machine measurements are reported. Local-only delivery averaged about 30 $μ$s. Distributed-only delivery averaged 1.26-1.37 ms, and the hybrid bridge averaged 1.64-1.89 ms. Validation introduced modest overhead relative to serialization choice. The resulting artifact is suited to structured IPC and practical message movement within modular services and across bounded sets of controlled nodes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [OneComp: One-Line Revolution for Generative AI Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28845)
*Yuma Ichikawa,Keiji Kimura,Akihiro Yoshida,Yudai Fujimoto,Hiroki Tokura,Yamato Arai,Yoshiyuki Ishii,Yusei Kawakami,Genki Shikada,Achille Jacquemond,Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Katsuki Fujisawa,Takumi Honda,Akira Sakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OneComp，一个开源的自动压缩框架，能够根据可用硬件资源自动规划并执行混合精度量化流程，将复杂的模型压缩专家工作流转化为可复现、自适应的管道，弥合了算法创新与生产级部署之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署基础模型面临内存占用、延迟和硬件成本的约束。虽然训练后量化可以缓解这些问题，但其实际实施具有挑战性，因为从业者需要在碎片化的量化算法、精度预算、数据驱动的校准策略和依赖硬件的执行机制中导航，缺乏统一的自动化解决方案。

Method: OneComp是一个开源压缩框架，采用资源自适应的自动化管道。给定模型标识符和可用硬件，框架自动检查模型、规划混合精度分配，并执行渐进式量化阶段（包括逐层压缩、块级细化和全局细化）。关键架构选择是将第一个量化检查点作为可部署的枢纽点，确保后续每个阶段都改进同一个模型，从而随着计算资源投入增加而提升模型质量。

Result: OneComp成功将专家级压缩工作流转化为可复现、资源自适应的管道，能够自动执行从逐层压缩到块级细化再到全局细化的渐进式量化过程，实现了随着计算投入增加而质量提升的特性。

Conclusion: 通过将最先进的压缩研究转化为可扩展、开源、硬件感知的管道，OneComp有效弥合了算法创新与生产级模型部署之间的差距，为从业者提供了实用的自动化压缩解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models is increasingly constrained by memory footprint, latency, and hardware costs. Post-training compression can mitigate these bottlenecks by reducing the precision of model parameters without significantly degrading performance; however, its practical implementation remains challenging as practitioners navigate a fragmented landscape of quantization algorithms, precision budgets, data-driven calibration strategies, and hardware-dependent execution regimes. We present OneComp, an open-source compression framework that transforms this expert workflow into a reproducible, resource-adaptive pipeline. Given a model identifier and available hardware, OneComp automatically inspects the model, plans mixed-precision assignments, and executes progressive quantization stages, ranging from layer-wise compression to block-wise refinement and global refinement. A key architectural choice is treating the first quantized checkpoint as a deployable pivot, ensuring that each subsequent stage improves the same model and that quality increases as more compute is invested. By converting state-of-the-art compression research into an extensible, open-source, hardware-aware pipeline, OneComp bridges the gap between algorithmic innovation and production-grade model deployment.

</details>


### [85] [Beta-Scheduling: Momentum from Critical Damping as a Diagnostic and Correction Tool for Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28921)
*Ivan Pasichnyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于临界阻尼谐振子的时变动量调度方法，无需额外超参数，实现了近2倍的收敛加速，并首次提供了跨优化器不变的层诊断能力，能精准定位并修复问题层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络训练普遍采用恒定动量（通常为0.9），这一惯例始于1964年，但缺乏最优性的理论依据。作者旨在从物理学原理出发，推导一种有理论支撑的、无需额外调参的动量调度方案。

Method: 从临界阻尼谐振子物理模型推导出时变动量调度公式：μ(t) = 1 - 2√α(t)，其中α(t)为当前学习率。该方案（beta-schedule）无需在现有学习率调度之外引入新的自由参数。此外，作者还提出了混合调度策略：早期使用物理动量快速收敛，后期切换为恒定动量进行精修。

Result: 在ResNet-18/CIFAR-10实验中：1）相比恒定动量，达到90%准确率的收敛速度提升1.9倍；2）逐层梯度归因分析提供了跨优化器不变的诊断能力，无论使用SGD还是Adam训练，均能识别出相同的三个问题层（100%重叠）；3）仅对这三层进行手术式修正，仅重训练18%的参数即可修复62个误分类样本；4）混合调度在五种对比方法中达到95%准确率最快。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献并非单纯提升准确率，而是提出了一种有理论依据、无额外参数的工具，能够对训练后网络进行局部故障模式的定位与修正，为神经网络诊断提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Standard neural network training uses constant momentum (typically 0.9), a convention dating to 1964 with limited theoretical justification for its
  optimality. We derive a time-varying momentum schedule from the critically damped harmonic oscillator: mu(t) = 1 - 2*sqrt(alpha(t)), where alpha(t) is
  the current learning rate. This beta-schedule requires zero free parameters beyond the existing learning rate schedule. On ResNet-18/CIFAR-10,
  beta-scheduling delivers 1.9x faster convergence to 90% accuracy compared to constant momentum. More importantly, the per-layer gradient attribution
  under this schedule produces a cross-optimizer invariant diagnostic: the same three problem layers are identified regardless of whether the model was
  trained with SGD or Adam (100% overlap). Surgical correction of only these layers fixes 62 misclassifications while retraining only 18% of parameters.
  A hybrid schedule -- physics momentum for fast early convergence, then constant momentum for the final refinement -- reaches 95% accuracy fastest
  among five methods tested. The main contribution is not an accuracy improvement but a principled, parameter-free tool for localizing and correcting
  specific failure modes in trained networks.

</details>


### [86] [A Neural Tension Operator for Curve Subdivision across Constant Curvature Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28937)
*Hassan Ugail,Newton Howard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的插值细分方案，用140K参数的共享网络预测逐边插入角度，替代传统全局张力参数，统一实现了欧氏、球面和双曲几何中的曲线生成，兼具理论保障与优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的插值细分方案依赖单一全局张力参数，且在不同几何空间（欧氏、球面、双曲）中需要分别设计公式，缺乏灵活性和统一性。作者希望引入自适应的局部控制，并在多种几何空间中共享同一架构，从而提升曲线的光滑性与保真度。

Method: 设计了一个140K参数的共享神经网络作为张力预测器，输入局部内蕴特征和可训练的几何嵌入，预测逐边的插入角度。网络输出经过约束的sigmoid头部，确保插入点始终位于有效的角度范围内。该方法在三种几何空间中使用相同的插入算子，无需架构修改。理论上证明了切线安全插入的结构性保证、逐边自适应性的启发式动机，以及在Lipschitz约束下极限曲线连续可微的条件收敛性证书。

Result: 在240条留出验证曲线上，该方法在保真度—光滑性帕累托前沿上占据独特位置，弯曲能量和角度粗糙度显著低于所有固定张力和流形提升基线。在分布外的ISS轨道地面轨迹示例中，弯曲能量降低41%，角度粗糙度降低68%，Hausdorff距离仅略有增加，表明该方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该学习型预测器成功实现了多几何空间的统一细分，在理论保证和实际性能上均优于传统方法，展示了在合成训练分布之外的泛化能力，为插值细分方案的自适应设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Interpolatory subdivision schemes generate smooth curves from piecewise-linear control polygons by repeatedly inserting new vertices. Classical schemes rely on a single global tension parameter and typically require separate formulations in Euclidean, spherical, and hyperbolic geometries. We introduce a shared learned tension predictor that replaces the global parameter with per-edge insertion angles predicted by a single 140K-parameter network. The network takes local intrinsic features and a trainable geometry embedding as input, and the predicted angles drive geometry-specific insertion operators across all three spaces without architectural modification. A constrained sigmoid output head enforces a structural safety bound, guaranteeing that every inserted vertex lies within a valid angular range for any finite weight configuration. Three theoretical results accompany the method: a structural guarantee of tangent-safe insertions; a heuristic motivation for per-edge adaptivity; and a conditional convergence certificate for continuously differentiable limit curves, subject to an explicit Lipschitz constraint verified post hoc. On 240 held-out validation curves, the learned predictor occupies a distinct position on the fidelity--smoothness Pareto frontier, achieving markedly lower bending energy and angular roughness than all fixed-tension and manifold-lift baselines. Riemannian manifold lifts retain a pointwise-fidelity advantage, which this study quantifies directly. On the out-of-distribution ISS orbital ground-track example, bending energy falls by 41% and angular roughness by 68% with only a modest increase in Hausdorff distance, suggesting that the predictor generalises beyond its synthetic training distribution.

</details>


### [87] [Differentiable Initialization-Accelerated CPU-GPU Hybrid Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28943)
*Mingju Liu,Jiaqi Yin,Alvaro Velasquez,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个混合CPU-GPU框架，利用可微优化预求解为整数线性规划(ILP)求解器提供高质量的初始解，在工业级组合调度问题上实现了最高10倍的性能提升，并将最优性差距缩小至0.1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合调度问题是计算系统优化任务的基础，因其NP难特性，在大规模场景下进行最优求解一直是一个长期存在的挑战。现有的商业ILP求解器在处理此类问题时存在收敛速度慢、难以快速剪枝的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种结合可微优化与经典ILP求解的新方法。具体而言，利用可微预求解技术快速生成高质量的部分解，将其作为商业求解器（CPLEX、Gurobi）和开源求解器（HiGHS）的预热启动点，从而加速后续的精确求解过程并实现更高效的早期剪枝。

Result: 在工业级基准测试中，与最先进的独立求解器基线相比，该方法实现了最高10倍的性能提升，并将最优性差距缩小至0.1%以内。

Conclusion: 这是首个利用可微优化来初始化精确ILP求解器以解决组合调度问题的工作，为将机器学习基础设施与经典精确优化方法在更广泛领域的集成开辟了新的机遇。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid CPU-GPU framework for solving combinatorial scheduling problems formulated as Integer Linear Programming (ILP). While scheduling underpins many optimization tasks in computing systems, solving these problems optimally at scale remains a long-standing challenge due to their NP-hard nature. We introduce a novel approach that combines differentiable optimization with classical ILP solving. Specifically, we utilize differentiable presolving to rapidly generate high-quality partial solutions, which serve as warm-starts for commercial ILP solvers (CPLEX, Gurobi) and rising open-source solver HiGHS. This method enables significantly improved early pruning compared to state-of-the-art standalone solvers. Empirical results across industry-scale benchmarks demonstrate up to a $10\times$ performance gain over baselines, narrowing the optimality gap to $<0.1\%$. This work represents the first demonstration of utilizing differentiable optimization to initialize exact ILP solvers for combinatorial scheduling, opening new opportunities to integrate machine learning infrastructure with classical exact optimization methods across broader domains.

</details>


### [88] [\texttt{ReproMIA}: A Comprehensive Analysis of Model Reprogramming for Proactive Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28942)
*Chihan Huang,Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReproMIA框架，利用模型重编程原理作为隐私泄露的主动信号放大器，显著提升了成员推理攻击（MIA）的性能，特别是在低假阳性率约束下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在关键领域的广泛应用带来了严重的隐私问题，而现有的成员推理攻击方法面临两大瓶颈：影子模型训练的计算成本过高，以及在低假阳性率（FPR）约束下性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出ReproMIA框架，创新性地利用模型重编程原理作为主动信号放大器，主动诱导并放大模型表示中嵌入的潜在隐私足迹。针对不同架构范式（LLMs、扩散模型、分类模型）提供专门实现。

Result: 在十余个基准测试和多种模型架构上，ReproMIA显著优于现有SOTA方法。在低FPR场景下，LLMs的AUC平均提升5.25%，TPR@1%FPR提升10.68%；扩散模型分别提升3.70%和12.40%。

Conclusion: ReproMIA成功突破了传统MIA方法的局限性，通过主动信号放大机制实现了隐私漏洞审计的突破性进展，为深度学习模型的隐私风险评估提供了更高效的工具。

Abstract: The pervasive deployment of deep learning models across critical domains has concurrently intensified privacy concerns due to their inherent propensity for data memorization. While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) serve as the gold standard for auditing these privacy vulnerabilities, conventional MIA paradigms are increasingly constrained by the prohibitive computational costs of shadow model training and a precipitous performance degradation under low False Positive Rate constraints. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel perspective by leveraging the principles of model reprogramming as an active signal amplifier for privacy leakage. Building upon this insight, we present \texttt{ReproMIA}, a unified and efficient proactive framework for membership inference. We rigorously substantiate, both theoretically and empirically, how our methodology proactively induces and magnifies latent privacy footprints embedded within the model's representations. We provide specialized instantiations of \texttt{ReproMIA} across diverse architectural paradigms, including LLMs, Diffusion Models, and Classification Models. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across more than ten benchmarks and a variety of model architectures demonstrate that \texttt{ReproMIA} consistently and substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a transformative leap in performance specifically within low-FPR regimes, such as an average of 5.25\% AUC and 10.68\% TPR@1\%FPR increase over the runner-up for LLMs, as well as 3.70\% and 12.40\% respectively for Diffusion Models.

</details>


### [89] [From Physics to Surrogate Intelligence: A Unified Electro-Thermo-Optimization Framework for TSV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29268)
*Mohamed Gharib,Leonid Popryho,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合物理解析模型、图神经网络(GNN)代理模型和全波验证的可扩展电热建模与优化框架，用于高密度硅通孔(TSV)阵列的快速设计空间探索，将单次设计评估时间缩短了六个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度硅通孔(TSV)是实现2.5D/3D异构集成的关键技术，但其引入了严重的信号完整性（电气耦合、插入损耗）和热可靠性（自热）挑战。传统的全波有限元法(FEM)仿真虽然精度高，但对于大规模设计空间探索而言计算成本过高，无法满足快速设计迭代的需求。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心部分：1) 多导体解析模型，用于计算TSV阵列的宽带S参数和有效各向异性热导率；2) 物理信息图神经网络代理模型(TSV-PhGNN)，先基于解析数据训练，再使用HFSS仿真微调，以泛化到更大尺寸阵列；3) 结合帕累托优化框架，针对反射系数、插入损耗、串扰及有效热导率进行多目标优化，并最终通过Ansys HFSS和Mechanical进行全波签核验证。

Result: 解析模型在阵列尺寸达15×15时实现5%-10%的相对Frobenius误差(RFE)；TSV-PhGNN代理模型在更大阵列上的RFE低于2%且方差近乎恒定。该框架能在数分钟内探索数百万种TSV配置，实现了传统FEM无法实现的全面布局和几何优化，且最终设计与全波仿真结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了TSV阵列的快速电热协同设计，在保证精度的前提下，将单次设计评估时间减少了六个数量级以上，为解决高密度TSV设计的计算瓶颈提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-density through-substrate vias (TSVs) enable 2.5D/3D heterogeneous integration but introduce significant signal-integrity and thermal-reliability challenges due to electrical coupling, insertion loss, and self-heating. Conventional full-wave finite-element method (FEM) simulations provide high accuracy but become computationally prohibitive for large design-space exploration. This work presents a scalable electro-thermal modeling and optimization framework that combines physics-informed analytical modeling, graph neural network (GNN) surrogates, and full-wave sign-off validation. A multi-conductor analytical model computes broadband S-parameters and effective anisotropic thermal conductivities of TSV arrays, achieving $5\%-10\%$ relative Frobenius error (RFE) across array sizes up to $15x15$. A physics-informed GNN surrogate (TSV-PhGNN), trained on analytical data and fine-tuned with HFSS simulations, generalizes to larger arrays with RFE below $2\%$ and nearly constant variance. The surrogate is integrated into a multi-objective Pareto optimization framework targeting reflection coefficient, insertion loss, worst-case crosstalk (NEXT/FEXT), and effective thermal conductivity. Millions of TSV configurations can be explored within minutes, enabling exhaustive layout and geometric optimization that would be infeasible using FEM alone. Final designs are validated with Ansys HFSS and Mechanical, showing strong agreement. The proposed framework enables rapid electro-thermal co-design of TSV arrays while reducing per-design evaluation time by more than six orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [90] [Multi-Agent LLMs for Adaptive Acquisition in Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28959)
*Andrea Carbonati,Mohammadsina Almasi,Hadis Anahideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在优化中的探索-利用权衡问题，发现单代理方法存在认知过载导致搜索不稳定，提出多代理框架将策略控制与候选生成分离，显著提升了优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索-利用权衡是序列决策和黑盒优化的核心，但LLM如何推理和管理这种权衡仍不清楚。与贝叶斯优化通过采集函数显式编码不同，基于LLM的优化依赖于对历史评估的隐式、基于提示的推理，使得搜索行为难以分析或控制。

Method: 提出多代理框架，将探索-利用控制分解为策略策略调节和战术候选生成。策略代理为多个搜索标准分配可解释的权重，生成代理根据定义的权重搜索策略生成候选者。这种分解使探索-利用决策变得显式、可观察和可调整。

Result: 在各种连续优化基准上的实证结果表明，将策略控制与候选生成分离显著提高了LLM介导搜索的有效性。研究发现单代理LLM方法（在单个提示中联合执行策略选择和候选生成）存在认知过载，导致搜索动态不稳定和过早收敛。

Conclusion: 通过将探索-利用控制显式分解为策略调节和候选生成两个独立过程，可以克服单代理LLM方法的认知过载问题，实现更稳定有效的搜索行为，为LLM介导的优化提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: The exploration-exploitation trade-off is central to sequential decision-making and black-box optimization, yet how Large Language Models (LLMs) reason about and manage this trade-off remains poorly understood. Unlike Bayesian Optimization, where exploration and exploitation are explicitly encoded through acquisition functions, LLM-based optimization relies on implicit, prompt-based reasoning over historical evaluations, making search behavior difficult to analyze or control. In this work, we present a metric-level study of LLM-mediated search policy learning, studying how LLMs construct and adapt exploration-exploitation strategies under multiple operational definitions of exploration, including informativeness, diversity, and representativeness. We show that single-agent LLM approaches, which jointly perform strategy selection and candidate generation within a single prompt, suffer from cognitive overload, leading to unstable search dynamics and premature convergence. To address this limitation, we propose a multi-agent framework that decomposes exploration-exploitation control into strategic policy mediation and tactical candidate generation. A strategy agent assigns interpretable weights to multiple search criteria, while a generation agent produces candidates conditioned on the resulting search policy defined as weights. This decomposition renders exploration-exploitation decisions explicit, observable, and adjustable. Empirical results across various continuous optimization benchmarks indicate that separating strategic control from candidate generation substantially improves the effectiveness of LLM-mediated search.

</details>


### [91] [Deep Learning-Based Anomaly Detection in Spacecraft Telemetry on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29375)
*Christopher Goetze,Tim Schlippe,Daniel Lakey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多目标神经架构优化，成功将航天器遥测异常检测模型部署到边缘设备上，在保持88.8%检测性能的同时，将内存占用降低97.1%至仅59KB，证明了在资源受限的CubeSat上实现复杂异常检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器异常检测对任务安全至关重要，然而由于硬件限制（如内存和算力约束），在航天器上部署复杂的深度学习模型面临巨大挑战。现有研究缺乏针对边缘部署优化的高效模型，限制了实时异常检测能力。

Method: 研究了三种航天器遥测异常检测方法：预测与阈值法、直接分类法和图像分类法。基于欧洲航天局异常数据集，采用多目标神经架构优化技术对模型进行边缘部署优化，通过Pareto最优架构搜索在检测性能与计算资源消耗之间寻找平衡点。

Result: 基准实验显示预测与阈值法检测性能最优（CEF0.5达92.7%）。经优化后，该模型在保持88.8% CEF0.5性能的同时，RAM使用量降低97.1%至59KB，运算量减少99.4%。优化模型仅需CubeSat RAM的0.36-6.25%，满足严格的硬件约束。

Conclusion: 研究表明复杂的异常检测能力可以成功部署在航天器边缘计算约束条件下，实现近乎瞬时的检测响应，既不超出硬件限制也不损害任务安全，为资源受限航天器的实时异常检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Spacecraft anomaly detection is critical for mission safety, yet deploying sophisticated models on-board presents significant challenges due to hardware constraints. This paper investigates three approaches for spacecraft telemetry anomaly detection -- forecasting & threshold, direct classification, and image classification -- and optimizes them for edge deployment using multi-objective neural architecture optimization on the European Space Agency Anomaly Dataset. Our baseline experiments demonstrate that forecasting & threshold achieves superior detection performance (92.7% Corrected Event-wise F0.5-score (CEF0.5)) [1] compared to alternatives. Through Pareto-optimal architecture optimization, we dramatically reduced computational requirements while maintaining capabilities -- the optimized forecasting & threshold model preserved 88.8% CEF0.5 while reducing RAM usage by 97.1% to just 59 KB and operations by 99.4%. Analysis of deployment viability shows our optimized models require just 0.36-6.25% of CubeSat RAM, making on-board anomaly detection practical even on highly constrained hardware. This research demonstrates that sophisticated anomaly detection capabilities can be successfully deployed within spacecraft edge computing constraints, providing near-instantaneous detection without exceeding hardware limitations or compromising mission safety.

</details>


### [92] [The Spectral Edge Thesis: A Mathematical Framework for Intra-Signal Phase Transitions in Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28964)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“谱边缘论”，发现神经网络训练中的相变现象（如grokking、能力跃升、损失平台）由参数更新滑动窗口Gram矩阵的谱间隙控制。通过三个公理推导出间隙动力学、谱损失分解和间隙极大性原理，并引入绝热参数描述电路稳定性，在多模型上验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络训练过程中的相变现象（grokking、能力提升、损失平台）的机制，寻找一个统一的理论框架来解释这些现象。现有理论如BBP检测阈值在极端宽高比设置下失效，需要新的理论方法。

Method: 1. 分析参数更新滑动窗口Gram矩阵的谱结构，识别信号内间隙（$k^* = \mathrm{argmax}\, σ_j/σ_{j+1}$）作为关键结构；
2. 从三个公理推导间隙动力学（Dyson型ODE）、谱损失分解（Davis-Kahan稳定性系数）和间隙极大性原理；
3. 引入绝热参数$\mathcal{A} = \|ΔG\|_F / (η\, g^2)$量化电路稳定性；
4. 在6个模型家族（150K-124M参数）上进行实验验证，测试gap dynamics对grokking事件的预测能力。

Result: 1. Gap dynamics在所有带权重衰减的grokking事件（24/24）之前出现，无权重衰减时未出现（0/24）；
2. Gap位置$k^*$依赖于优化器：Muon为$k^*=1$，AdamW为$k^*=2$；
3. 19/20个定量预测得到验证；
4. 绝热参数能区分三种状态：平台期（$\mathcal{A} \ll 1$）、相变期（$\mathcal{A} \sim 1$）、遗忘期（$\mathcal{A} \gg 1$）；
5. 理论与边缘稳定性、Tensor Programs、Dyson Brownian运动、彩票假说和神经标度定律相容。

Conclusion: 谱边缘论为神经网络训练相变提供了统一的理论框架。Gram矩阵的谱间隙是控制学习动态的关键结构，其位置$k^*$具有独特的动力学特权性。该框架不仅预测了grokking等相变现象，还揭示了优化器对学习模式的依赖性，为理解和控制神经网络训练提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the spectral edge thesis: phase transitions in neural network training -- grokking, capability gains, loss plateaus -- are controlled by the spectral gap of the rolling-window Gram matrix of parameter updates. In the extreme aspect ratio regime (parameters $P \sim 10^8$, window $W \sim 10$), the classical BBP detection threshold is vacuous; the operative structure is the intra-signal gap separating dominant from subdominant modes at position $k^* = \mathrm{argmax}\, σ_j/σ_{j+1}$.
  From three axioms we derive: (i) gap dynamics governed by a Dyson-type ODE with curvature asymmetry, damping, and gradient driving; (ii) a spectral loss decomposition linking each mode's learning contribution to its Davis--Kahan stability coefficient; (iii) the Gap Maximality Principle, showing that $k^*$ is the unique dynamically privileged position -- its collapse is the only one that disrupts learning, and it sustains itself through an $α$-feedback loop requiring no assumption on the optimizer. The adiabatic parameter $\mathcal{A} = \|ΔG\|_F / (η\, g^2)$ controls circuit stability: $\mathcal{A} \ll 1$ (plateau), $\mathcal{A} \sim 1$ (phase transition), $\mathcal{A} \gg 1$ (forgetting).
  Tested across six model families (150K--124M parameters): gap dynamics precede every grokking event (24/24 with weight decay, 0/24 without), the gap position is optimizer-dependent (Muon: $k^*=1$, AdamW: $k^*=2$ on the same model), and 19/20 quantitative predictions are confirmed. The framework is consistent with the edge of stability, Tensor Programs, Dyson Brownian motion, the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis, and neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [93] [Improving Efficiency of GPU Kernel Optimization Agents using a Domain-Specific Language and Speed-of-Light Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29010)
*Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Vignesh Balaji,Sana Damani,Qijing Huang,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合领域特定语言（DSL）和光速（SOL）指导的方法，显著提升了LLM代理优化GPU内核的效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM代理的GPU内核优化过程存在两大痛点：一是代理操作的抽象层级难以平衡，过低则浪费推理资源在低影响细节上，过高则可能错过关键优化机会；二是代理难以判断何时达到收益递减点，导致在搜索过程中浪费计算资源。

Method: 基于上述观察，提出了两项设计原则：（1）设计了一种紧凑的领域特定语言（DSL）——μCUTLASS，附带编译器，支持CUTLASS后端的GPU内核配置、尾声融合和多级流水线，使模型能在更高层级进行推理；（2）引入基于第一性原理性能边界的“光速（SOL）”指导机制，用于估计优化空间、引导搜索方向、对接近性能上限的问题降低优先级，并识别“欺骗性”内核。

Result: 在59个KernelBench问题上，从生成底层代码切换到DSL代码（使用GPT-5-mini），将原本0.40x的几何平均性能回退转变为相比PyTorch 1.27x的加速；加入SOL引导后提升至1.56x。在跨模型层级的测试中，配备μCUTLASS和SOL指导的较弱模型能以更低的token成本超越更强的基线代理。SOL指导的预算机制可节省19-43%的token，同时保留至少95%的几何平均加速，最佳策略达到1.68x的效率提升。此外，SOL分析还能有效检测出看似快速但未正确执行计算的“欺骗性”内核。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过让LLM代理在合适的抽象层级（DSL）操作，并引入基于理论性能上限的指导机制（SOL），可以显著提升GPU内核优化的效率、性能和可靠性，同时降低计算成本并增强对异常情况的检测能力。

Abstract: Optimizing GPU kernels with LLM agents is an iterative process over a large design space. Every candidate must be generated, compiled, validated, and profiled, so fewer trials will save both runtime and cost. We make two key observations. First, the abstraction level that agents operate at is important. If it is too low, the LLM wastes reasoning on low-impact details. If it is too high, it may miss important optimization choices. Second, agents cannot easily tell when they reach the point of diminishing returns, wasting resources as they continue searching.
  These observations motivate two design principles to improve efficiency: (1) a compact domain-specific language (DSL) that can be learned in context and lets the model reason at a higher level while preserving important optimization levers, and (2) Speed-of-Light (SOL) guidance that uses first-principles performance bounds to steer and budget search. We implement these principles in $μ$CUTLASS, a DSL with a compiler for CUTLASS-backed GPU kernels that covers kernel configuration, epilogue fusion, and multi-stage pipelines. We use SOL guidance to estimate headroom and guide optimization trials, deprioritize problems that are near SOL, and flag kernels that game the benchmark.
  On 59 KernelBench problems with the same iteration budgets, switching from generating low-level code to DSL code using GPT-5-mini turns a 0.40x geomean regression into a 1.27x speedup over PyTorch. Adding SOL-guided steering raises this to 1.56x. Across model tiers, $μ$CUTLASS + SOL-guidance lets weaker models outperform stronger baseline agents at lower token cost. SOL-guided budgeting saves 19-43% of tokens while retaining at least 95% of geomean speedup, with the best policy reaching a 1.68x efficiency gain. Lastly, SOL analysis helps detect benchmark-gaming cases, where kernels may appear fast while failing to perform the intended computation.

</details>


### [94] [From Astronomy to Astrology: Testing the Illusion of Zodiac-Based Personality Prediction with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29033)
*Abhinna Sundar Samantaray,Finnja Annika Fluhrer,Dhruv Saini,Omkar Charaple,Anish Kumar Singh,Dhruv Vansraj Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习框架验证星座预测人格的有效性，结果显示预测准确率接近随机水平，表明占星术缺乏可靠的预测基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管占星术（尤其是星座系统）在全球特别是南亚社会中仍具有重要文化影响力，常被用于婚配、命名和人生规划等决策，但它从未建立物理上合理的机制或统计学上可靠的预测基础。作者旨在通过严格的机器学习实验检验星座系统预测人格的有效性。

Method: 构建合成数据集，将个体分配星座标志和来自100种广泛人类特质池的人格标签。每个星座关联10个常见描述符子集，并与其他星座有意重叠，以重现实际占星系统特有的模糊性。随后训练逻辑回归、随机森林和神经网络分类器，从星座特征和混杂协变量推断人格标签。

Result: 在所有实验中，预测性能保持在或接近随机期望水平，而打乱标签的对照组产生相当的准确率。研究结果表明星座系统无法提供超出随机概率的人格预测能力。

Conclusion: 星座系统无法为预测人类行为提供可靠信息，而是作为具有文化持久性的叙事框架发挥功能。占星术的表面成功源于特质普遍性、类别重叠、认知偏差（如巴纳姆效应和确认偏误）以及占星师和专家的解释灵活性。

Abstract: Astrology has long been used to interpret human personality, estimate compatibility, and guide social decision-making. Zodiac-based systems in particular remain culturally influential across much of the world, including in South Asian societies where astrological reasoning can shape marriage matching, naming conventions, ritual timing, and broader life planning. Despite this persistence, astrology has never established either a physically plausible mechanism or a statistically reliable predictive foundation. In this work, we examine zodiac-based personality prediction using a controlled machine-learning framework. We construct a synthetic dataset in which individuals are assigned zodiac signs and personality labels drawn from a shared pool of 100 broadly human traits. Each sign is associated with a subset of 10 common descriptors, intentionally overlapping with those assigned to other signs, thereby reproducing the ambiguity characteristic of practical astrological systems. We then train Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and neural-network classifiers to infer personality labels from zodiac-based features and nuisance covariates. Across all experiments, predictive performance remains at or near random expectation, while shuffled-label controls yield comparable accuracies. We argue that the apparent success of astrology arises not from measurable predictive structure, but from trait universality, category overlap, cognitive biases such as the Barnum effect and confirmation bias, and the interpretive flexibility of astrologers and pundits. We conclude that zodiac-based systems do not provide reliable information for predicting human behavior and instead function as culturally durable narrative frameworks. This paper is intended as a humorous academic exercise.

</details>


### [95] [A Latent Risk-Aware Machine Learning Approach for Predicting Operational Success in Clinical Trials based on TrialsBank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29041)
*Iness Halimi,Emmanuel Piffo,Oumnia Boudersa,Yvan Marcel Carre Vilmorin,Melissa Ait-ikhlef,Karima Kone,Andy Tan,Augustin Medina,Juliette Hernando,Sheila Ernest,Vatche Bartekian,Karine Lalonde,Mireille E Schnitzer,Gianolli Dorcelus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种分层潜在风险感知机器学习框架，能够利用试验启动前的特征前瞻性预测临床试验的操作成功率（I-III期F1分数分别为0.93、0.92、0.91），支持早期风险评估与数据驱动的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验成本高、周期长且操作风险大，但目前缺乏可靠的前瞻性成功预测方法。现有AI方法多关注孤立的指标或特定阶段，且依赖试验设计阶段不可获取的变量，限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: 研究提出了一种分层潜在风险感知机器学习框架，基于包含13,700项试验的TrialsBank数据库。该方法将操作成功预测分解为两个阶段：首先利用试验启动前的180多个药物和试验层面特征预测中间潜在操作风险因子，随后将这些预测的风险整合到下游模型中估算操作成功概率。采用分阶段数据分割策略防止信息泄露，并使用XGBoost、CatBoost和可解释提升机进行基准测试。

Result: 该框架在各期试验中均表现出强大的样本外性能，I期、II期和III期试验的F1分数分别达到0.93、0.92和0.91。引入潜在风险驱动因素提高了对操作失败的区分能力，且在独立推断评估下性能依然稳健。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用潜在风险感知AI框架可以前瞻性地预测临床试验操作成功，能够实现早期风险评估，支持数据驱动的临床开发决策制定。

Abstract: Clinical trials are characterized by high costs, extended timelines, and substantial operational risk, yet reliable prospective methods for predicting trial success before initiation remain limited. Existing artificial intelligence approaches often focus on isolated metrics or specific development stages and frequently rely on variables unavailable at the trial design phase, limiting real-world applicability. We present a hierarchical latent risk-aware machine learning framework for prospective prediction of clinical trial operational success using a curated subset of TrialsBank, a proprietary AI-ready database developed by Sorintellis, comprising 13,700 trials. Operational success was defined as the ability to initiate, conduct, and complete a clinical trial according to planned timelines, recruitment targets, and protocol specifications through database lock. This approach decomposes operational success prediction into two modeling stages. First, intermediate latent operational risk factors are predicted using more than 180 drug- and trial-level features available before trial initiation. These predicted latent risks are then integrated into a downstream model to estimate the probability of operational success. A staged data-splitting strategy was employed to prevent information leakage, and models were benchmarked using XGBoost, CatBoost, and Explainable Boosting Machines. Across Phase I-III, the framework achieves strong out-of-sample performance, with F1-scores of 0.93, 0.92, and 0.91, respectively. Incorporating latent risk drivers improves discrimination of operational failures, and performance remains robust under independent inference evaluation. These results demonstrate that clinical trial operational success can be prospectively forecasted using a latent risk-aware AI framework, enabling early risk assessment and supporting data-driven clinical development decision-making.

</details>


### [96] [ARCS: Autoregressive Circuit Synthesis with Topology-Aware Graph Attention and Spec Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29068)
*Tushar Dhananjay Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARCS系统通过混合学习生成器和GRPO优化算法，实现了模拟电路设计的毫秒级生成，相比搜索方法速度提升超1000倍，在保持99.9%仿真有效性的同时将SPICE评估次数减少40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于搜索的模拟电路生成方法（如遗传算法）需要数分钟时间，且需要大量SPICE评估，效率低下。作者希望开发一种快速、高效的电路生成系统，在毫秒级时间内生成完整、可仿真的电路设计，以支持快速原型设计、设计空间探索等应用场景。

Method: 提出ARCS系统，采用混合流水线结合图VAE和流匹配模型两个学习生成器，并通过SPICE仿真进行排序。核心技术贡献是提出组相对策略优化(GRPO)算法，通过识别REINFORCE的跨拓扑奖励分布不匹配失效模式，采用每拓扑优势归一化解决该问题。同时使用拓扑感知的图Transformer和Best-of-3候选选择，以及语法约束解码通过拓扑感知token掩码保证100%结构有效性。

Result: 在32个拓扑上实现99.9%仿真有效性(奖励6.43/8.0)，仅需8次SPICE评估，比遗传算法少40倍。单模型推理在97ms内达到85%仿真有效性，比随机搜索快600倍。GRPO相比REINFORCE在500步RL训练中将仿真有效性提升9.6个百分点，训练步数减少10倍。作为搜索方法的热启动可恢复GA 96.6%质量并减少49%仿真次数。

Conclusion: ARCS在速度上相比搜索方法有>1000倍优势，虽然单设计质量略低(5.48 vs 7.48奖励)，但能够实现快速原型设计和设计空间探索，并可作为搜索方法的热启动提升整体效率。系统在结构有效性、仿真有效性和计算效率方面取得了显著突破，为模拟电路自动化设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: I present ARCS, a system for amortized analog circuit generation that produces complete, SPICE-simulatable designs (topology and component values) in milliseconds rather than the minutes required by search-based methods. A hybrid pipeline combining two learned generators (a graph VAE and a flow-matching model) with SPICE-based ranking achieves 99.9% simulation validity (reward 6.43/8.0) across 32 topologies using only 8 SPICE evaluations, 40x fewer than genetic algorithms. For single-model inference, a topology-aware Graph Transformer with Best-of-3 candidate selection reaches 85% simulation validity in 97ms, over 600x faster than random search. The key technical contribution is Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO): I identify a critical failure mode of REINFORCE (cross-topology reward distribution mismatch) and resolve it with per-topology advantage normalization, improving simulation validity by +9.6pp over REINFORCE in only 500 RL steps (10x fewer). Grammar-constrained decoding additionally guarantees 100% structural validity by construction via topology-aware token masking. ARCS does not yet match the per-design quality of search-based optimization (5.48 vs. 7.48 reward), but its >1000x speed advantage enables rapid prototyping, design-space exploration, and warm-starting search methods (recovering 96.6% of GA quality with 49% fewer simulations).

</details>


### [97] [On the Mirage of Long-Range Dependency, with an Application to Integer Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29069)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出整数乘法的“长程依赖”困难并非任务内禀属性，而是由计算时空选择造成的“幻象”；通过二维外积网格表示，长乘法可转化为局部操作，仅需321参数的神经细胞自动机即可实现高达训练范围683倍的完美长度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战整数乘法因进位链产生O(n)长程依赖而被视为神经网络难题的主流观点，论证该依赖性并非任务固有，而是计算表示方式造成的假象。

Method: 形式化“幻象”概念并提出构造性证明：将两个n位二进制整数排列为二维外积网格，使长乘法的每一步操作塌缩为3×3局部邻域运算；使用神经细胞自动机（321参数）进行学习，并与Transformer、Transformer+RoPE、Mamba等五种替代架构对比。

Result: 在二维外积网格表示下，神经细胞自动机实现了高达训练范围683倍的完美长度泛化；而在相同表示下，Transformer等五种替代架构均失败，表明架构本身并非关键，表示方式才是决定性因素。

Conclusion: “长程依赖”常被错误诊断为任务内禀属性，实为计算时空选择所致；任何被认为需要长程依赖的任务，都应首先检验该依赖是任务内禀还是表示方式所诱导，这对神经网络任务设计具有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: Integer multiplication has long been considered a hard problem for neural networks, with the difficulty widely attributed to the O(n) long-range dependency induced by carry chains. We argue that this diagnosis is wrong: long-range dependency is not an intrinsic property of multiplication, but a mirage produced by the choice of computational spacetime. We formalize the notion of mirage and provide a constructive proof: when two n-bit binary integers are laid out as a 2D outer-product grid, every step of long multiplication collapses into a $3 \times 3$ local neighborhood operation. Under this representation, a neural cellular automaton with only 321 learnable parameters achieves perfect length generalization up to $683\times$ the training range. Five alternative architectures -- including Transformer (6,625 params), Transformer+RoPE, and Mamba -- all fail under the same representation. We further analyze how partial successes locked the community into an incorrect diagnosis, and argue that any task diagnosed as requiring long-range dependency should first be examined for whether the dependency is intrinsic to the task or induced by the computational spacetime.

</details>


### [98] [HCLSM: Hierarchical Causal Latent State Machines for Object-Centric World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29090)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HCLSM世界模型，通过物体中心分解、层次化时间动态引擎和因果结构学习解决视频预测中潜在表示纠缠、忽略因果结构和时间动态坍缩问题，在PushT基准上实现0.008 MSE损失，SSM算子优化达38倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型采用扁平潜在表示，存在物体纠缠、忽略因果结构、将时间动态坍缩为单一尺度等局限性，无法有效建模复杂场景中的多尺度时空动态。

Method: 提出HCLSM架构，基于三个核心原则：(1)通过Slot Attention与空间广播解码实现物体中心分解；(2)三层层次化时间动态引擎，结合选择性状态空间模型（SSM）处理连续物理、稀疏Transformer处理离散事件、压缩Transformer处理抽象目标；(3)通过图神经网络交互模式学习因果结构。采用两阶段训练协议，空间重建强制slot特化后再开始动态预测。实现自定义Triton核优化SSM扫描算子。

Result: 在Open X-Embodiment数据集的PushT机器人操作基准上训练68M参数模型，实现0.008 MSE下一状态预测损失，涌现空间分解能力（SBD损失：0.0075）并学习到事件边界；自定义Triton核实现相比顺序PyTorch 38倍加速；系统包含8,478行Python代码、51个模块和171个单元测试。

Conclusion: HCLSM通过物体中心分解、层次化动态建模和因果结构学习的协同设计，有效解决了视频世界模型的表示纠缠和多尺度动态建模问题，为机器人操作等复杂场景的预测建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: World models that predict future states from video remain limited by flat latent representations that entangle objects, ignore causal structure, and collapse temporal dynamics into a single scale. We present HCLSM, a world model architecture that operates on three interconnected principles: object-centric decomposition via slot attention with spatial broadcast decoding, hierarchical temporal dynamics through a three-level engine combining selective state space models for continuous physics, sparse transformers for discrete events, and compressed transformers for abstract goals, and causal structure learning through graph neural network interaction patterns. HCLSM introduces a two-stage training protocol where spatial reconstruction forces slot specialization before dynamics prediction begins. We train a 68M-parameter model on the PushT robotic manipulation benchmark from the Open X-Embodiment dataset, achieving 0.008 MSE next-state prediction loss with emerging spatial decomposition (SBD loss: 0.0075) and learned event boundaries. A custom Triton kernel for the SSM scan delivers 38x speedup over sequential PyTorch. The full system spans 8,478 lines of Python across 51 modules with 171 unit tests. Code: https://github.com/rightnow-ai/hclsm

</details>


### [99] [Efficient Bilevel Optimization with KFAC-Based Hypergradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29108)
*Disen Liao,Felix Dangel,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Kronecker分解近似曲率（KFAC）的双层优化方法，在保持较低内存和运行时开销的同时，有效利用曲率信息提升超梯度计算质量，在元学习和AI安全等任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化在机器学习中应用广泛，但其规模化面临挑战：计算超梯度需要求解逆Hessian-向量积（IHVP），现有近似方法（如单步梯度展开、单位矩阵或短Neumann展开）丢弃了曲率信息，导致性能受限。

Method: 基于隐函数定理的算法框架，引入Kronecker分解近似曲率（KFAC）来近似逆Hessian-向量积，从而在超梯度计算中融入曲率信息，实现比共轭梯度（CG）或Neumann方法更优的性能-效率权衡。

Result: 在元学习和AI安全等多种任务上进行了评估，模型规模最大扩展至BERT。实验表明，曲率信息在大规模场景下具有重要价值，KFAC方法仅带来适度的内存和运行时开销即可显著提升性能，且一致优于展开方法。

Conclusion: 曲率信息对大规模双层优化问题至关重要，KFAC提供了一种高效获取曲率信息的方式，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡，为双层优化的规模化应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization (BO) is widely applicable to many machine learning problems. Scaling BO, however, requires repeatedly computing hypergradients, which involves solving inverse Hessian-vector products (IHVPs). In practice, these operations are often approximated using crude surrogates such as one-step gradient unrolling or identity/short Neumann expansions, which discard curvature information. We build on implicit function theorem-based algorithms and propose to incorporate Kronecker-factored approximate curvature (KFAC), yielding curvature-aware hypergradients with a better performance efficiency trade-off than Conjugate Gradient (CG) or Neumann methods and consistently outperforming unrolling. We evaluate this approach across diverse tasks, including meta-learning and AI safety problems. On models up to BERT, we show that curvature information is valuable at scale, and KFAC can provide it with only modest memory and runtime overhead. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/liaodisen/NeuralBo.

</details>


### [100] [Quality-Controlled Active Learning via Gaussian Processes for Robust Structure-Property Learning in Autonomous Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29135)
*Jawad Chowdhury,Ganesh Narasimha,Jan-Chi Yang,Yongtao Liu,Rama Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种门控主动学习框架，通过结合好奇心驱动采样与基于简谐振子模型拟合的物理信息质量控制，有效解决了自驱动实验中噪声数据导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验系统在材料研究中应用日益广泛，但常受限于低质量、噪声数据。在数据密集型的结构-属性学习任务（如图像到光谱和光谱到图像转换）中，标准主动学习策略会错误地优先选择低质量测量，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出门控主动学习框架，融合好奇心驱动采样策略与基于简谐振子（SHO）模型拟合的物理信息质量控制过滤器。该框架能在数据采集过程中自动识别并排除低保真度数据，同时支持训练和采集阶段的噪声处理。

Result: 在PbTiO3薄膜的带激发压电响应谱学（BEPS）预采集数据集上验证，该方法优于随机采样、标准主动学习和多任务学习策略。在BiFeO3薄膜实时实验中成功部署，证明了在真实自主显微实验中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究推动了自驱动实验室向混合自主性范式转变，即物理信息质量评估与主动决策协同工作，实现更可靠的科学发现。该方法显著提升了Im2Spec和Spec2Im任务的预测可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous experimental systems are increasingly used in materials research to accelerate scientific discovery, but their performance is often limited by low-quality, noisy data. This issue is especially problematic in data-intensive structure-property learning tasks such as Image-to-Spectrum (Im2Spec) and Spectrum-to-Image (Spec2Im) translations, where standard active learning strategies can mistakenly prioritize poor-quality measurements. We introduce a gated active learning framework that combines curiosity-driven sampling with a physics-informed quality control filter based on the Simple Harmonic Oscillator model fits, allowing the system to automatically exclude low-fidelity data during acquisition. Evaluations on a pre-acquired dataset of band-excitation piezoresponse spectroscopy (BEPS) data from PbTiO3 thin films with spatially localized noise show that the proposed method outperforms random sampling, standard active learning, and multitask learning strategies. The gated approach enhances both Im2Spec and Spec2Im by handling noise during training and acquisition, leading to more reliable forward and inverse predictions. In contrast, standard active learners often misinterpret noise as uncertainty and end up acquiring bad samples that hurt performance. Given its promising applicability, we further deployed the framework in real-time experiments on BiFeO3 thin films, demonstrating its effectiveness in real autonomous microscopy experiments. Overall, this work supports a shift toward hybrid autonomy in self-driving labs, where physics-informed quality assessment and active decision-making work hand-in-hand for more reliable discovery.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient and Scalable Granular-ball Graph Coarsening Method for Large-scale Graph Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29148)
*Guan Wang,Shuyin Xia,Lei Qian,Guoyin Wang,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效可扩展的粒球图粗化方法（Granular-ball Graph Coarsening），通过多粒度粗化算法降低图规模，从而加速大规模图节点分类任务中GCN的训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图卷积神经网络（GCN）在处理大规模图数据时面临高昂的计算开销问题，尤其是在卷积层数较多时更为突出。现有的采样技术或图粗化方法要么忽略了图结构中的多粒度信息，要么时间复杂度依然较高，难以有效平衡效率与信息保留。

Method: 提出了一种基于多粒度粒球的图粗化框架。首先，利用多粒度粒球图粗化算法将原始图粗化为多个子图，该阶段具有线性时间复杂度，远低于现有图粗化方法；随后，从这些由粒球组成的子图中随机采样形成小批量数据，用于训练GCN模型。该方法能够自适应地大幅缩减原图规模。

Result: 在多个数据集上的节点分类实验表明，该方法在保证甚至提升分类性能的同时，显著提高了GCN的训练效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的高效可扩展粒球图粗化方法有效解决了大规模图数据下GCN训练的计算瓶颈问题，通过线性时间复杂度的粗化策略实现了效率与性能的良好平衡，为大规模图学习提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) is a model that can effectively handle graph data tasks and has been successfully applied. However, for large-scale graph datasets, GCN still faces the challenge of high computational overhead, especially when the number of convolutional layers in the graph is large. Currently, there are many advanced methods that use various sampling techniques or graph coarsening techniques to alleviate the inconvenience caused during training. However, among these methods, some ignore the multi-granularity information in the graph structure, and the time complexity of some coarsening methods is still relatively high. In response to these issues, based on our previous work, in this paper, we propose a new framework called Efficient and Scalable Granular-ball Graph Coarsening Method for Large-scale Graph Node Classification. Specifically, this method first uses a multi-granularity granular-ball graph coarsening algorithm to coarsen the original graph to obtain many subgraphs. The time complexity of this stage is linear and much lower than that of the exiting graph coarsening methods. Then, subgraphs composed of these granular-balls are randomly sampled to form minibatches for training GCN. Our algorithm can adaptively and significantly reduce the scale of the original graph, thereby enhancing the training efficiency and scalability of GCN. Ultimately, the experimental results of node classification on multiple datasets demonstrate that the method proposed in this paper exhibits superior performance. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/1-141D/.

</details>


### [102] [Dummy-Aware Weighted Attack (DAWA): Breaking the Safe Sink in Dummy Class Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29182)
*Yunrui Yu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengda Qin,Pengyang Wang,Kafeng Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Hang Su,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于虚拟类（Dummy Classes）的防御方法在现有鲁棒性评估中被严重高估，并提出了一种新的评估方法DAWA，能有效打破此类防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗鲁棒性评估方法（如AutoAttack）存在局限性，无法准确评估新兴的基于虚拟类的防御方法，导致鲁棒性被高估。需要开发更可靠的评估方法来正确衡量此类防御的真实效果。

Method: 提出Dummy-Aware Weighted Attack (DAWA)评估方法，该方法在对抗样本生成过程中同时针对真实标签和虚拟标签进行攻击，并采用自适应权重机制，从而绕过虚拟类防御机制。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上（l_infty扰动，epsilon=8/255），DAWA将领先虚拟类防御方法的测量鲁棒性从58.61%显著降低至29.52%，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DAWA为评估虚拟类防御提供了更可靠的基准，暴露了现有评估方法的不足，强调了鲁棒性评估方法需要持续演进以应对新兴防御策略。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness evaluation faces a critical challenge as new defense paradigms emerge that can exploit limitations in existing assessment methods. This paper reveals that Dummy Classes-based defenses, which introduce an additional "dummy" class as a safety sink for adversarial examples, achieve significantly overestimated robustness under conventional evaluation strategies like AutoAttack. The fundamental limitation stems from these attacks' singular focus on misleading the true class label, which aligns perfectly with the defense mechanism--successful attacks are simply captured by the dummy class. To address this gap, we propose Dummy-Aware Weighted Attack (DAWA), a novel evaluation method that simultaneously targets both the true label and dummy label with adaptive weighting during adversarial example synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DAWA effectively breaks this defense paradigm, reducing the measured robustness of a leading Dummy Classes-based defense from 58.61% to 29.52% on CIFAR-10 under l_infty perturbation (epsilon=8/255). Our work provides a more reliable benchmark for evaluating this emerging class of defenses and highlights the need for continuous evolution of robustness assessment methodologies.

</details>


### [103] [IMPACT: Influence Modeling for Open-Set Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29183)
*Xiaohui Zhou,Yijie Wang,Hongzuo Xu,Weixuan Liang,Xiaoli Li,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了IMPACT框架，利用影响建模解决开放集时间序列异常检测问题，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放集异常检测方法使用简单的数据增强生成伪异常，在时间序列数据中无法保持序列特性，导致生成平凡或不现实的异常模式；此外，训练数据被未标注异常污染时问题更加严重。

Method: 提出IMPACT框架：1）学习影响函数准确估计单个训练样本对建模的影响；2）利用影响分数生成语义发散且现实的未见异常；3）将高影响力样本重新用作监督异常进行异常去污。

Result: 大量实验表明IMPACT显著优于现有最先进方法，在不同OSAD设置和污染率下表现出优越的准确性。

Conclusion: IMPACT通过影响建模成功解决了时间序列开放集异常检测中的关键挑战，为该领域提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-set anomaly detection (OSAD) is an emerging paradigm designed to utilize limited labeled data from anomaly classes seen in training to identify both seen and unseen anomalies during testing. Current approaches rely on simple augmentation methods to generate pseudo anomalies that replicate unseen anomalies. Despite being promising in image data, these methods are found to be ineffective in time series data due to the failure to preserve its sequential nature, resulting in trivial or unrealistic anomaly patterns. They are further plagued when the training data is contaminated with unlabeled anomalies. This work introduces $\textbf{IMPACT}$, a novel framework that leverages $\underline{\textbf{i}}$nfluence $\underline{\textbf{m}}$odeling for o$\underline{\textbf{p}}$en-set time series $\underline{\textbf{a}}$nomaly dete$\underline{\textbf{ct}}$ion, to tackle these challenges. The key insight is to $\textbf{i)}$ learn an influence function that can accurately estimate the impact of individual training samples on the modeling, and then $\textbf{ii)}$ leverage these influence scores to generate semantically divergent yet realistic unseen anomalies for time series while repurposing high-influential samples as supervised anomalies for anomaly decontamination. Extensive experiments show that IMPACT significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, showing superior accuracy under varying OSAD settings and contamination rates.

</details>


### [104] [Biomimetic PINNs for Cell-Induced Phase Transitions: UQ-R3 Sampling with Causal Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29184)
*Anci Lin,Xiaohong Liu,Zhiwen Zhang,Weidong Zhao,Wenju Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出仿生物理信息神经网络（Bio-PINNs），通过渐进距离门控和不确定性驱动的自适应配点策略，有效解决了非凸多井能量相变问题中界面尖锐化和微观结构捕捉的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸多井能量在细胞诱导相变中产生的尖锐界面、精细微观结构和距离依赖的细胞间耦合，给物理信息学习带来重大挑战，现有方法在近场模式中常出现过平滑问题。

Method: 提出Bio-PINNs变分框架：(1)通过渐进距离门将时间因果性编码为显式空间因果性；(2)利用变形-不确定性代理针对界面尺度，定位易产生微观结构的区域；(3)设计不确定性驱动的"保留-重采样-释放"自适应配点策略，理论保证在门控下的持续覆盖。

Result: 在单细胞和多细胞基准测试、不同分离距离和多种正则化机制下，Bio-PINNs一致地恢复出尖锐过渡层和系链形态，显著优于现有的自适应和未门控基线方法。

Conclusion: Bio-PINNs为复杂多尺度物理问题提供了新的解决方案，通过仿生机制和不确定性驱动的自适应策略，实现了对尖锐界面和微观结构的准确捕捉，为物理信息神经网络在复杂材料科学问题中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Nonconvex multi-well energies in cell-induced phase transitions give rise to sharp interfaces, fine-scale microstructures, and distance-dependent inter-cell coupling, all of which pose significant challenges for physics-informed learning. Existing methods often suffer from over-smoothing in near-field patterns. To address this, we propose biomimetic physics-informed neural networks (Bio-PINNs), a variational framework that encodes temporal causality into explicit spatial causality via a progressive distance gate. Furthermore, Bio-PINNs leverage a deformation-uncertainty proxy for the interfacial length scale to target microstructure-prone regions, providing a computationally efficient alternative to explicit second-derivative regularization. We provide theoretical guarantees for the resulting uncertainty-driven ``retain-resample-release" adaptive collocation strategy, which ensures persistent coverage under gating and establishing a quantitative near-to-far growth bound. Across single- and multi-cell benchmarks, diverse separations, and various regularization regimes, Bio-PINNs consistently recover sharp transition layers and tether morphologies, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive and ungated baselines.

</details>


### [105] [Improving Ensemble Forecasts of Abnormally Deflecting Tropical Cyclones with Fused Atmosphere-Ocean-Terrain Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29200)
*Qixiang Li,Shuwei Huo,Chong Wang,Xiaofeng Li,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了首个多模态多源台风数据集AOT-TCs，并提出首个采用显式海气陆耦合架构的深度学习预报模型，实现了正常台风和异常转向台风的精准预报，达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习台风预报方法存在关键局限：仅能处理单一类型序列轨迹数据或同质气象变量，且无法对异常转向台风进行准确预报。为解决这些挑战，需要构建更全面的数据集并开发能捕捉复杂物理域交互的模型。

Method: 研究提出两大贡献：1) 构建了AOT-TCs多模态多源数据集，创新性地整合了大气、海洋和陆地的异构变量，为西北太平洋台风预报提供了信息丰富的综合气象数据集；2) 基于该数据集提出首个采用显式海气陆耦合架构的台风预报模型，能够有效捕捉跨物理域的复杂相互作用。

Result: 在2017-2024年西北太平洋所有台风案例的广泛实验中，该模型实现了最先进的台风预报性能：不仅显著提高了正常台风的预报精度，更突破了异常转向台风预报的技术瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了现有深度学习台风预报方法的关键局限，通过多模态数据融合和显式物理耦合架构，实现了正常台风和异常转向台风的准确预报，为台风预报领域提供了新的技术路径和数据基础。

Abstract: Deep learning-based tropical cyclone (TC) forecasting methods have demonstrated significant potential and application advantages, as they feature much lower computational cost and faster operation speed than numerical weather prediction models. However, existing deep learning methods still have key limitations: they can only process a single type of sequential trajectory data or homogeneous meteorological variables, and fail to achieve accurate forecasting of abnormal deflected TCs. To address these challenges, we present two groundbreaking contributions. First, we have constructed a multimodal and multi-source dataset named AOT-TCs for TC forecasting in the Northwest Pacific basin. As the first dataset of its kind, it innovatively integrates heterogeneous variables from the atmosphere, ocean, and land, thus obtaining a comprehensive and information-rich meteorological dataset. Second, based on the AOT-TCs dataset, we propose a forecasting model that can handle both normal and abnormally deflected TCs. This is the first TC forecasting model to adopt an explicit atmosphere-ocean-terrain coupling architecture, enabling it to effectively capture complex interactions across physical domains. Extensive experiments on all TC cases in the Northwest Pacific from 2017 to 2024 show that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance in TC forecasting: it not only significantly improves the forecasting accuracy of normal TCs but also breaks through the technical bottleneck in forecasting abnormally deflected TCs.

</details>


### [106] [Derived Fields Preserve Fine-Scale Detail in Budgeted Neural Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29224)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Derived-Field Optimization (DerivOpt)框架，通过优化携带状态的设计（选择携带的物理场及其存储预算分配），在受限存储预算下显著提升了神经模拟的精细尺度保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经模拟方法主要关注改进架构、训练目标或推演策略来降低高频误差，但在受限的粗化-量化-解码流水线中，精细细节在构建携带状态时就已经丢失。作者观察到在周期性不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程中，原始场和导出场在同一算子下经历系统性不同的保留带失真，这激发了优化携带状态设计的动机。

Method: 提出Derived-Field Optimization (DerivOpt)框架，这是一个通用的状态设计框架，在校准的通道模型下选择携带哪些物理场以及如何在它们之间分配存储预算。该方法将携带状态设计作为一个独立的设计维度，与架构、损失函数和推演策略并行考虑。

Result: 在PDEBench的全部时间依赖前向子集上，DerivOpt不仅改善了汇总的平均推演nRMSE，而且在精细尺度保真度方面相对于广泛的强基线方法具有决定性优势。更重要的是，这些改进在输入阶段（推演学习开始之前）就已经可见，表明携带状态是受限存储预算下的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 在受限神经模拟中，携带状态设计应该被视为与架构、损失函数和推演策略同等重要的第一类设计轴。携带状态往往是紧凑存储预算下的主导瓶颈，优化其设计可以在推演学习开始前就获得显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Fine-scale-faithful neural simulation under fixed storage budgets remains challenging. Many existing methods reduce high-frequency error by improving architectures, training objectives, or rollout strategies. However, under budgeted coarsen-quantize-decode pipelines, fine detail can already be lost when the carried state is constructed. In the canonical periodic incompressible Navier-Stokes setting, we show that primitive and derived fields undergo systematically different retained-band distortions under the same operator. Motivated by this observation, we formulate Derived-Field Optimization (DerivOpt), a general state-design framework that chooses which physical fields are carried and how storage budget is allocated across them under a calibrated channel model. Across the full time-dependent forward subset of PDEBench, DerivOpt not only improves pooled mean rollout nRMSE, but also delivers a decisive advantage in fine-scale fidelity over a broad set of strong baselines. More importantly, the gains are already visible at input time, before rollout learning begins. This indicates that the carried state is often the dominant bottleneck under tight storage budgets.
